# Change history

## Week list, first batch (2026-10-25)

1. **Lily White: big petals.** The petals her flowers burst into (her own flower bullets and the flowers on her branches) are twice their old size, hitbox too (`LILY_PETAL_SIZE` 2).
2. **Ichirin: no throws at the walls behind her.** A throw's way was chosen when the windup began; she stands opposite the player and moves with them, so by the time the hand flew the wall it aimed at could be behind her. Now the hand is aimed again every frame of the windup (from where she and the player are then) and the way is fixed when it is thrown (`IchirinAct.aim`, `Toss.Aimed`); the impact rings are made then.
3. **Chen: every second arrowhead is a basic bullet** (counted over all her shots), flying straight (`splitEverySecond`).
4. **Koishi and Satori: twice as many hearts.** `KOISHI_HEART_INTERVAL_FACTOR` 2 → 1 (as often as the stars); in a spinner every star is a heart (was every second).
5. **Satori: her third eye.** Over the two beats before every reading a pink eye (Flandre's eye picture, tinted) opens 2.2 circle radii in front of the player, along the way they have moved since the last reading (where the cone will point); it closes over 300 ms after the reading. None while the player has not moved (`SATORI_EYE_*`).
6. **Flandre: three eyes, twice as often, twice as fast, 1.5x bigger.** Every 4 beats (`FLANDRE_THROW_EVERY_BEATS`) she puts down three eyes a beat apart (`FLANDRE_EYES_PER_THROW`, `FLANDRE_EYE_GAP_BEATS`), each on the player's place at that moment; each goes off 4 beats later (`flandre_eye_beats` default 8 → 4) with a radius of 4.5 circles (`flandre_eye_radius` 3 → 4.5).
7. **Nue: every fourth bullet, shot out four times.** `NUE_PULL_EVERY` 2 → 4; every bullet the UFOs took in is shot out `NUE_SHOT_COPIES` 4 times (spread over the cone and the shooting time).
8. **Zanmu: stopped bullets divide.** When the fall begins every stopped bullet becomes four pieces of half its size (hitbox too), falling fanned out over 30 degrees round the way of the fall (`ZANMU_SPLIT_*`). Beams are not divided.
9. **Doremy: white and blue glowing copies.** Her copied bullets are white and blue in turn and glow 0.8 (new `BulletEmitter.AltTint` and `BulletEmitter.Bloom`, `DOREMY_COPY_BLOOM`).
10. **Yuyuko + Junko (any beam): the lines from the top down are gone.** Found with the new `long` option of Tools/SectionCheck `field` (prints beam segments that cross most of the screen; `BulletField.BeamProbe`). Cause: where a beam changes from fallen to not fallen, `insertKinks` put one corner point there with the look of the fallen side; at the end of Yuyuko's window the slices just after it do not fall, so the corner could be a slice still flying up with the fallen look, and it was joined to the fallen slices far below. Now that change is a cut (a point on each side with its own look). Also: what falls of a beam falls as separate short pieces (60 px of beam, at most 200 ms of fire time, 60 % piece and 40 % gap: `YUYUKO_BEAM_PIECE_*`, the gaps are gone like blown-up slices), two neighbouring pieces have different purple looks (`BossOps.PurpleBeamAlt`) and are never joined, and two fallen points more than 90 px apart are never joined (draw and hit).
11. **Numbers.** Sunny Milk's split bullets 4x smaller (`SUNNY_SPLIT_SIZE_DIVISOR` 3 → 4). Rumia's dark circles 25 % smaller (`RUMIA_CIRCLE_RADIUS` 3 → 2.25). Letty's beams dissipate twice as often (`LETTY_BULLET_INTERVAL_BEATS` 0.125 → 0.0625). Yukari's side bullets twice as far apart (`YUKARI_SIDE_ANGLE_DEGREES` 5 → 10).
12. **Utsuho's six big bullets and Marisa's beams stay in two-boss sections.** Utsuho's ring was 6 in the code but the two-boss halving made it 3: her big rings are no longer halved (`BulletEmitter.KeepCount`). Marisa's beam bursts are not halved either.
13. **Two bosses attack together.** The second boss's attacks no longer start half an attack phase after the first one's (`turnOffset` 0).
14. **480 BPM at most.** Every beat length the planner reads goes through `DanmakusuBalance.CapBeat`: a tempo above 480 BPM is halved until it fits (960 → 480, 600 → 300). The acts use the period's beat, which comes from it.
15. **Junko.** Her reshot ring comes every 8 beats (`JUNKO_RECORD_BEATS` 16 → 8) and counts every 4th bullet (`JUNKO_RECORD_EVERY`, counted on over the windows). The other bosses' doings (ops, Zanmu's ripples, Flandre's eyes, portals) only touch a slice of hers once it is fully formed (after its 4 see-through beats): it flies straight until then, and they start from where it is then (`LaserEmitter.evaluate`).
16. **Patchouli.** Water: a rider stays on its ring 4 beats after it has turned solid, then flies on the way and as fast as the bullet it replaced (`PATCHOULI_RIDE_STAY_BEATS`). Fire: over the 2 beats before a burning bullet goes off its red ring flashes 8 times, faster and faster and brighter (`PATCHOULI_FIRE_FLASH_*`).
17. **Kaku: returning bullets are talismans** (shape Talisman, no longer darker bullets with a talisman on them).
18. **Marisa: every third bullet in her attacks is a ricochet star** (every ring fired in her attacks, counted over all of them; finish-star size; `MARISA_STAR_EVERY`, new planner helper `splitOff`).
19. **Freeze keys are osu!standard's.** The ruleset's input manager reads osu!standard's key bindings (`DrawableDanmakusuRuleset.CreateInputManager`): key 1 and key 2 as bound for osu!standard are the freeze keys.
20. **No Boss mod removed.** No boss mod on = no boss. One Boss Selection is still put on when the ruleset is selected.
21. **Boss list in four columns** (tiles are a quarter of the list's width).
22. Okina's freeze (Okina + Cirno: 41.6 ms per frame against 6.9 for Cirno alone, rings kept alive by the portals) was found but left for later, as asked.
23. **Okina: beams that come out of a portal fly their own way.** The slices no longer move along with the second portal when it slides (`FollowExit` is not used any more).
24. **Byakuren: bigger, faster homing beams with stars.** Her homing beams are twice as wide (`BYAKUREN_TRAIL_WIDTH` 1 → 2), their heads twice as fast (`BYAKUREN_TRAIL_SPEED` 2), and they turn towards the player only for their first 8 beats, then fly straight on (`BYAKUREN_HOMING_BEATS_LIMIT`). The front tip of each is a star bullet like Marisa's (spinner-star size), drawn by the field, hurting like the beam (`HeadAct.Style.StarHeads`).
25. **Warnings only when a boss appears.** The power-up warning (sound and the stars that fly in) is kept only where a boss's section starts; Mokou's and Ichirin's single warning is now everyone's. Before every other attack, and where a reversed warning (Cirno's, Yukari's, Hecatia's, Kyouko's, Sakuya's) or Yukari's warning ended, every bullet flashes instead: over the warning's length its glow pulses 6 times, faster and brighter, up to 0.85 (`flashTimes`, `flashLevel`, `ATTACK_FLASHES`, `ATTACK_FLASH_BLOOM`). Mokou's jumps and throws flash too (no power-up sound).
27. **Gengetsu (new mechanic `Haste`, no longer a placeholder).** Own shots: double rings of 16 basic bullets every beat (the inner ring 0.75x as fast, turned half a step; `GENGETSU_RING_*`), also in a spinner (the spinner's stars become basic bullets, no spinner beams). In her attacks: every ring (anyone's) is shot again 1/8 beat later (`GENGETSU_AGAIN_BEATS`); every bullet on the screen speeds up, evenly from 1x to 3x over each attack (`GENGETSU_SPEED_UP`; first done per bullet over 4 beats, then changed as asked: it is a speed multiplier on everything, a window of `BossEffects` like Mystia's slowdown, which now has a speed that goes from one value to another over the window; back to 1x when the attack ends; `BulletEmitter.SpeedRampTo` stays as a tool, unused); every 4th bullet is 1.2x as big (`GENGETSU_BIG_*`, `BulletEmitter.BigEvery` / `BigFactor`, which were Remilia's constants); every 4th beam that starts in her section is twice as thick (`GENGETSU_THICK*`). Benchmark (Chirality 3:00, 15 s): 1.3 ms per frame.
28. **Mugetsu (new mechanic `Crescent`, no longer a placeholder).** Own shots: rings of 16 arrowheads flying straight, every beat (`MUGETSU_RING_*`), also in a spinner. In her attacks every 4th ring (anyone's, that knows its outline) is 3x as dense (64 at most) and its bullets curl as they fly out at 40 degrees a second, the even ones one way and the odd ones the other (`MUGETSU_*`, new `BulletEmitter.Curl`). Benchmark: 0.95 ms per frame.
29. Benchmarks of the changed bosses (Chirality 3:00, 15 s): Flandre 8.2 ms per frame (lasers 4.1: three times as many eyes to check the beams' slices against), Zanmu 9.0, Byakuren 1.4, Luna Child 1.2.
30. **Mokou comes back.** When every boss section of the map is over (her last one ends with her death, as the last section's boss always did) she comes back 8 beats later (`REVIVAL_DELAY_BEATS`) as a kiai boss until the last object of the map ends (if that is at least 8 more beats, `REVIVAL_MIN_BEATS`); her big ball and the bullets of her landings are twice as big there (`MOKOU_REVIVAL_SIZE`, `BossPeriod.Revival`). Benchmark (Cyaegha Avarice 2:00, 60 s): 0.57 ms per frame.
31. **Utsuho: the black hole is a dark circle that bends light.** The picture is gone. Now: a black disc with a soft dark edge that hides what is inside it like Rumia's circles (`addHide`), a thin bright photon ring hugging its edge, a warmer one just outside it, a faint image further out (2.16 / 2.3 / 3.1 radii across, shimmering), and the warm halo; the old lens (what is seen near it is pushed outwards) stays. The top layer holds 16 pictures (was 8).
32. **Marisa: the Master Spark.** Her boss beams (not the spinner's) start at an osu! circle's radius at her, widen along an outer curve to 4 circle radii over the first 4 circle radii (`MARISA_SPARK_WIDE`, new `LaserEmitter.GrowthDistance`), and then open 5 degrees to each side, nearly straight, to the end (`MARISA_SPARK_SPREAD_DEGREES`, `LaserEmitter.SpreadTan`). They no longer turn towards the player: they spin at half a spinner beam's speed, always the same way (`MARISA_SPARK_SPIN`). The slices fly straight at the same speed. She stands still while it fires (`holdStill`: where she was when the attack began).
33. **Two kinds of controls (boss menu).** The bottom half on the right of the boss menu (the attack preview) is now a Controls panel with two choices, kept in `%APPDATA%\osu\danmakusu_controls.txt` (`UI/Controls.cs`): **Cursor only** (as before: the character is the cursor, key 1 / key 2 freeze it) and **Keyboard (WASD)**: W A S D or the arrows move the character at 0.4 px/ms (`KEYBOARD_SPEED`), Shift at half that (`KEYBOARD_SLOW`), clamped to the screen; pressing Space teleports it to the cursor, and while Space is held it stays on the cursor; no freeze. The bosses aim at the character then. In a replay (only the cursor is recorded) the character follows the cursor. (`BossPreview` itself is kept, unused.)
34. **Enoko Mitsugashira (new mechanic `Traps`, no longer a placeholder).** Bear traps (`TrapAct`, one act for all her sections) put down at the times of Flandre's eyes (every 4 beats three, a beat apart, each on the player's place at that moment, with the same approach circle closing in 4 beats), half the size of Flandre's eye (`ENOKO_TRAP_SIZE`). Drawn from two baked pictures (`StageTexture.BearTrapOpen` / `BearTrapClosed`: a steel ring of jaws with teeth pointing in and a brass plate; the jaws shut). Once the approach circle has closed the trap is armed, stays, and has a faint red rim. A player within 0.8 of its radius (`ENOKO_TRAP_TRIGGER`) snaps it shut: it hurts once, fades out over 600 ms with a little pop, and holds the character where it is for 4 beats (`ENOKO_HOLD_BEATS`, new `BulletField.holdCharacter`; with the cursor the character is put back on it afterwards; undone by a rewind). At most 24 traps on the map (`ENOKO_MAX_TRAPS`): when there would be more the oldest fades out. Her own shots and her spinner are the default stars (nothing was asked); no spinner beams.
35. **Nemuno Sakata (new mechanic `Blades`, no longer a placeholder).** Own shots: rings of 16 single-bladed knives every 2 beats (`NEMUNO_RING_*`), their pictures twice as long (`NEMUNO_BLADE_STRETCH`, new `BulletEmitter.KnifeStretch` / `BulletInstance.Stretch`; the hitbox stays the knife's circle); in a spinner its stars are these knives. In her attacks the rings of every 5th circle or slider (`NEMUNO_OBJECT_EVERY`) are fired where the player is (`BulletEmitter.FromPlayer`, as if the object were there; nothing hits there) and fall like ellipses whatever their shape (`FallsToPlayer`), 8x more gently (`NEMUNO_FALL_GENTLER`), the way from the object to the player. Every 10th slider beam of her section (`NEMUNO_BEAM_EVERY`) is hers when its slider ends (`LaserEmitter.VanishAt`, `BossPeriod.TakenBeams`, `NemunoAct`): it stops, turns round its middle to point at the player over 1 beat, two copies on each side slide out over 1 beat (2.6 half widths apart, not touching), and 2 beats later all five fall towards the player the way Zanmu's things fall (her gravity, darkening), hurting like a slider body. ("His clones" was read as copies of the beam.)
36. **Sanae Kochiya (mechanic `Miracle`, no longer a placeholder).** In her attacks every ring (anyone's, not her own) has twice as many bullets (64 at most), half their size, and a round one becomes a five-pointed star (`SANAE_COUNT_FACTOR`, `SANAE_SIZE_FACTOR`, new `BulletEmitter.RebuiltAs`, which also lengthens the lifetime when the new outline has nearer points). Her attacks dim the background green (`miracleBackground`, 0.35) with a giant see-through green star turning slowly behind everything (`MiracleAct`, new `StageTexture.BigStar`). Own shots: every 2 beats a ring of 30 ricochet stars half a finish's size in a star outline (`SANAE_RING_*`, `SANAE_STAR_SIZE`); the star of each ring is turned 30 degrees a second further since her section began, every second ring the other way ("spins 30 degrees a second, next in opposite direction"). Her spinner shoots the same rings, one every 2 beats (the stars between are gone).
37. **Hecatia: the Earth and the Moon gather bullets instead of taking them away.** While they are thrown (the first kind of attack), each gathers every bullet it touches (deflectable or not; at most 64 each, `HECATIA_GATHER_MAX`; past that, the old push / take away). A gathered bullet is gone from its ring and lies under its world (drawn below the planet's picture, harmless), spread evenly over a disc 1.15x the world's radius (`GATHER_SPREAD`), filled from the rim inwards by the golden angle, turning with the world. At every impact (a wall, or the other world) the world shoots out everything it holds from its middle, in rings of 4 spread over 180 degrees away from the wall (or the other world) (`GATHER_RING_BULLETS`, `GATHER_SPREAD_DEGREES`); the rings go together, each slower than the one before (speed / (1 + 0.12 x ring), `GATHER_RING_SLOWER`), so they come out one behind the other, every bullet with its own look. The side bullets of a ricochet stay. In the attacks of turning worlds nothing changed (push / take away). New `UI/Stages/Gathering.cs` (shared with Unzan). Like the UFOs, what was gathered stays when time goes back; only what came after that moment is undone.
38. **Hecatia: a throw is never cut short** (asked while this was being done). When a throw ends (and no other throw carries it on) the Earth and the Moon each fly on to their next impact (at most 8 beats, `HECATIA_OVERRUN_BEATS`); that impact still throws its side bullets and shoots out what the world holds; only then does the world fade out, or, when an attack of turning worlds follows, go back to its orbit from there (coasting the way it bounced off, drawn into its place over `HECATIA_RETURN_MS`; before, it was pulled back from wherever it was when the attack ended). After its last impact a world no longer collides with the other one.
39. **Unzan's hands gather bullets too** ("apply Hecatia changes to Unzan Hands"). A hand that is out (thrown, holding at the wall or coming back) gathers the bullets it touches (at most 64 each, `ICHIRIN_GATHER_MAX`), drawn on the hand; at its next impact, the wall hit, everything is shot out like Hecatia's, 180 degrees away from that wall; at a clap both hands shoot theirs from the clap's place towards the player. The hands at home or clapping do not gather. (The no-cut-short throw was already Unzan's.)
40. **(2026-10-26) Utsuho: sharp big bullets.** Her big bullets are drawn the way Mokou's ball is (the big baked disc: border, body, white core, glow and bloom; `BulletEmitter.Sharp`, `sharpOrbs` drawn in `drawStage` into `stageBullets`, which now holds 320 pictures, was 96) instead of a small bullet picture scaled up. That layer is above the bullets and under the black hole (`stageTop`), so the hole stays above them; the hole itself was already above the bullets. The lens moves them like everything else near the hole. Fix (reported with a screenshot: still blurry): with Clownpiece on the screen every ring is replaced by a doubled copy (`doubleRing`), which was built field by field and lost the flag; it now keeps `Sharp` and `KeepCount`. Second fix (still blurry with Marisa, also in spinners): Marisa's every-third-star split (`splitOff`) rebuilt every ring in her attacks, Utsuho's included, without the flag; it now keeps it (her stars are not round, so they stay stars), and so do Ringo's echoes of them.
41. **(2026-10-26) Gengetsu: 1x to 1.5x.** `GENGETSU_SPEED_UP` 3 → 1.5.
42. **(2026-10-26) Enoko: rarer traps, shorter hold.** Traps come 4x more rarely (every 16 beats three, `ENOKO_RARER` 4), at most 6 on the map (`ENOKO_MAX_TRAPS` 24 → 6), a trap holds for 2 beats (`ENOKO_HOLD_BEATS` 4 → 2).
43. **(2026-10-26) Nemuno: straight, well-spaced copies.** The taken beam straightens smoothly into a line while it turns to the player (its points laid out along the line through its ends, as far apart as along the beam, so it keeps its length), so the copies slide out as straight parallel beams; they are further apart (`NEMUNO_COPY_GAP` 2.6 → 5 half widths between middles).
44. **(2026-10-26) Sanae: bigger, fainter background star.** `SANAE_STAR_SCREEN` 0.9 → 1.8, `SANAE_STAR_ALPHA` 0.18 → 0.09 (twice as big, read "transparent" as more see-through: half as opaque).
45. **(2026-10-26) Marisa: the Master Spark stays straight.** Its slices no longer fly on the way they were fired (that curled the turning beam): every slice lies the way the beam points now (`LaserEmitter.Rigid`; Clownpiece's moon and Flandre's eyes see it that way too, `SliceAngle` / `SliceOrigin`). The widening part has 32 points at fixed distances (it had a point only where the width changed by 5 % of the widest width, which with the 5-degree opening was very coarse: the staircases). The dark edge and the coloured layer round the white core are half as thick (`LaserEmitter.ThinOutlines`, `LaserInstance.Thin`). Spin 4x slower (`MARISA_SPARK_SPIN` 0.5 → 0.125).
46. **(2026-10-26) Utsuho: the black hole swallows, and shoots everything out of its rays.** A bullet that touches the hole no longer vanishes at once: it flies on as it was, harmless, and shrinks to nothing over 160 ms (`UTSUHO_SWALLOW_MS`, a stage bullet with `Fade`). Everything it takes in comes out of the two rays, first one ray and then the other in turn, then (corrected: "bullets come out from both rays") every bullet split in two, one out of each ray (`UTSUHO_MAX_OUTPUT` 600 → 1200, so it still takes in 600) (was: every second one round the hole in flat ellipses, the "galaxy", which is gone; the `UTSUHO_GALAXY_*` numbers are unused). The rays turn half as fast (`UTSUHO_JET_TURN_FACTOR` 0.5 → 0.25 of a spinner beam's speed).
47. **(2026-10-26) Utsuho's big balls: under the bullets, and they grow in.** They are drawn in a new layer right under the bullet renderer (`stageOrbs`; `mokouOrb` takes the layer; `stageBullets` back to 96 pictures), and grow from nothing to their size over 220 ms when they are shot (`UTSUHO_BALL_GROW_MS`; only the picture, the hitbox is whole from the start).
48. **(2026-10-26) Marisa: the Master Spark no longer turns.** `MARISA_SPARK_SPIN` 0.125 → 0: it stays where it was aimed when it started (at the player). (Read as a request; if it meant "they stopped spinning by mistake", the number goes back.)
49. **(2026-10-26) Boss menu.** The attack preview is back in the bottom half on the right; the Controls choice moved under the boss list on the left (the two choices side by side, 160 px tall, above the Start button), and the list ends that much higher. The preview's character dodges by itself even with keyboard controls chosen (`keyboardControls` is off in a preview field).
50. **(2026-10-26) Utsuho: the rays shoot again.** What the rays shot out was a stage bullet that the ops act on, and her own pull (the Redirect op of her pauses, which takes everything fired meanwhile too) turned it straight back into the hole: nothing seemed to come out. The rays' bullets now fly their own straight line (new `StageBullet.Untraced`).
51. **(2026-10-26) Utsuho: the black hole leaves her big balls alone.** Not pulled (new `TrajectoryOp.SkipSharp` on her pull), not taken in (`HoleAct.Takes`), and not bent by the lens.
52. **(2026-10-26) Utsuho: attacks and pauses twice as long** (her own only: `UTSUHO_PHASE_FACTOR` 2, on the attack burst and the pause in `addBoss`).
53. **(2026-10-26) Utsuho: Mokou's impact sound for every ring of big balls** (a sound cue `mokou_wall_hit` at each ring's time; rings at the same moment sound once).
54. **(2026-10-26) Marisa: thinner Master Spark.** Every width 20 % less (`MARISA_SPARK_THICKNESS` 0.8: where it starts and how wide it grows; how far it widens is the same), the opening 20 % less (`MARISA_SPARK_SPREAD_DEGREES` 5 → 4).
55. **(2026-10-26) Utsuho: every ring of big balls is turned further.** "Double the angle between rings" (asked: between successive rings): each ring is turned 30 degrees further than the one before (`UTSUHO_RING_TURN_DEGREES`, half the gap of a ring of six, so the balls of one fly between those of the last; counted over the whole map), on top of aiming at the player.
56. **(2026-10-26) Utsuho: the black hole bends the light (asked again with a picture of a real one).** A true bending of the whole picture would need a shader of its own (not done); instead: every bullet near the hole is drawn stretched along the circle round it and squeezed across (the stretch is the lens's magnification, primary image / true distance, at most 5x, `UTSUHO_LENS_MAX_STRETCH`; new `BulletInstance.LensStretch` / `LensAxis`, applied by `drawShape` to every picture of the bullet); a bullet within 3 lens radii has a faint second image on the other side, stretched, in a thin band hugging the dark edge (`UTSUHO_LENS_SECOND_*`); and 180 faint stars of the background round the hole (out to 5 lens radii, `UTSUHO_LENS_STARS`, `UTSUHO_LENS_STAR_REACH`) are drawn bent the same way, each also as a second image in the band, swirling slowly round it, the inner ones faster (`UTSUHO_LENS_SWIRL`). The dark fringe round the hole is smaller (3.2 → 2.15 radii across) so the band shows. `stageBack` holds 1024 pictures (was 640).
57. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: everything stops before the black hole.** Over the 2 beats before every pause (`UTSUHO_STOP_BEATS`) every bullet on the screen slows evenly to a stop, stands still until the pull begins (400 ms into the pause), and gets its speed back over 300 ms (`UTSUHO_RESUME_MS`): three speed windows of `BossEffects` (like Mystia's and Gengetsu's; they act on everything, her big balls too). Her pull is put where the bullets' own (slowed) clocks stand still, so they turn to the hole where they stopped (`BossOps.UtsuhoStopFrom`).
58. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the pull at half speed** (new `TrajectoryOp.RedirectSpeed`, `UTSUHO_PULL_SPEED` 0.5: a pulled bullet flies at the hole at half its own speed).
59. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the rays shoot all through the pause.** Each swallowed bullet comes out at its own moment between when it was taken and when the hole goes, spread evenly (golden-ratio steps), instead of 120 ms after it went in.
60. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the rays turn half as fast again** (`UTSUHO_JET_TURN_FACTOR` 0.25 → 0.125).
61. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: bullets near the hole are not see-through any more** (the hole's hide circle is gone; the dark is drawn over what is in it).
62. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the lens bends everything, and is twice as wide.** Her big balls are bent too (pushed out and stretched, `mokouOrb` takes a stretch and a turn); `UTSUHO_LENS_FACTOR` 1.1 → 2.2 hole radii (the band of the second images hugs the dark: `lensEdge`).
63. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the black hole's music.** The user's `Serious-Sam-4-SnapYT.App.mp3` from 9:52 to its end (32.85 s, decoded with BASS by a scratch tool, 20 ms fades at both ends) is `Resources/Samples/utsuho_hole_loop.wav`, looped from when the slowing down begins until the hole is gone (`updateHoleSound`).
64. **(2026-10-27) Sunny Milk: the rainbow goes out of the prism.** The seven pieces a bullet splits into when it leaves the prism could be sent back into it (the mirrored way ±60 degrees): every piece whose way points in is now reflected off the side it left through, and they start just outside the glass. (A first reading, Sunny standing outside her prism, was rolled back.)
65. **(2026-10-27) Boss menu: WASD speed slider** under the controls (0.25x to 2x of the keyboard speed, steps of 0.05, kept in `%APPDATA%\osu\danmakusu_keyboard_speed.txt`, `Controls.KeyboardSpeed`); the controls block is 210 px tall.
66. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the lens bends into arcs, half as wide, with no empty ring.** `UTSUHO_LENS_FACTOR` 2.2 → 1.1. A stretched bullet was one straight stretched picture; now its picture is cut into 8 strips across the side that runs round the hole and every corner is laid on the circle round the hole's middle (`BulletInstance.LensCentre`, `drawBent`): it is bent into an arc. The bent stars are three overlapping pieces along their arc. The empty ring between the dark and the lens's radius (everything is pushed out to at least that radius) is closed: what is right behind the hole is seen at the dark's edge, the push less and less further out (`lensDistance`, for the bullets, her big balls and the stars).
67. **(2026-10-27) Sunny Milk: the rainbow goes on out of the prism (corrected).** The base way of the seven pieces turned back the part of the way that ran along the side (so they went back the way they came, into the prism); it is now mirrored across the side's line instead, so they go on out of it (the reflection of 64 stays as a safety).
68. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: a jolt on every ring of big balls instead of shaking all the time** (`UTSUHO_SHAKE` 0.5 dying away over 300 ms, `UTSUHO_SHOT_SHAKE_MS`; read "every time bullet shots" as her big balls, since her plain bullets come several times a beat and would shake all the time again).
69. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the black hole's music twice as loud** (the WAV cut again with gain 2; the original peaks at 0.95, so the loudest 5 % of samples go through a soft limiter above 0.8 instead of clipping).
70. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: flicker around the hole.** The pull was put exactly where the stopping bullets' clocks come to rest: rounding put it a hair before or after from frame to frame, so a still bullet's picture flicked between its own way and the hole's. It is now 3 ms earlier. The second images are not made while the hole fades in or out (`lensPresence` under 0.5) and fade with it.
71. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the rays' bullets twice as fast** (`utsuho_jet_speed` default 0.75 → 1.5; the tuning values are not saved, so the new default is what plays).
72. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: her big balls do not stop before the black hole.** The stop windows are kept apart in `BossEffects` and her big balls (`Sharp`) run on a clock with every window but those (`BossEffects.Effective(BulletEmitter, double)`, which every call of the field now goes through: 27 calls changed from `Effective(x.Time, ...)`). Mystia's and Gengetsu's windows still act on them.
73. **(2026-10-27) Boss menu: a Random button** on the right of the search field (first 30 % of the row; then, as asked, a narrow 120 px button labelled "Random" and the search field takes the rest): it checks one boss (two in a two-boss selection) at random among the bosses with a mechanic of their own that the search shows (all of them if it shows none), replacing what was checked.
74. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the pull stops when the hole goes.** A pulled bullet was made to vanish where it reached the middle, also after the hole was gone (it looked as if the pull went on). The pull now has a release moment (new `TrajectoryOp.ReleaseAt`: the hole's end on the clock of a bullet that was flying before the stop): one that would reach the middle after it flies on through.
75. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the rays' bullets twice as fast again** (`utsuho_jet_speed` default 1.5 → 3, its slider now goes up to 6).
76. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: wider, brighter cones of light, above the bullets.** `UTSUHO_JET_SPREAD` 0.09 → 0.18 (the cone and the spread of the rays' bullets, which fill it); the cones are drawn in the top layer (above the bullets, under the dark of the hole; `stageTop` holds 96 pictures and has the wedge), as a warm cone plus the same again added as light.
77. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: a flash where a ray shoots a bullet out** (a warm glow 5x the bullet's size, fading over 180 ms, over the dark: `UTSUHO_JET_FLASH_*`).
78. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the rays' bullets come out just outside the dark, flash there, are spaghettified, and are twice as fast again.** They start 1.15 hole radii out (`UTSUHO_JET_OUT`; were 0.55, under the dark, so the flash was on the black), the flash with them; they are drawn twice as long along their way and 1/√2 as wide (new `StageBullet.Elongate` / `BulletInstance.Elongate`, `UTSUHO_JET_STRETCH` 2; the hitbox stays round; knives and talismans are drawn as they are); `utsuho_jet_speed` default 3 → 6 (slider up to 12).
79. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the black hole stuttered.** Likely cause (not measured: the drawing happens on the draw thread, which the bench tool does not run): the lens stretches every bullet a little however far it is (the magnification never reaches 1), and every stretched bullet was drawn bent, as 8 strips (body, bloom, soft copy each): about eight times the drawing of the whole field. Now a stretch under 1.04 is not drawn (`UTSUHO_LENS_MIN_STRETCH`), and a bent bullet has as many strips as its bend needs (one per 0.12 radians it reaches round the hole, 8 at most; one = drawn straight). The bent stars are 120 (was 180) and their second images one piece each (were three): about 480 pictures instead of 1080.
80. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: big balls 10 % smaller** (`utsuho_ring_scale` default 12 → 10.8).
81. **(2026-10-27) Nemuno: rings round the player wider and fading in.** The rings fired where the player is start twice as far round them (`NEMUNO_SPAWN_WIDER` 2, the spawn radius) and fade in over 300 ms (new `BulletEmitter.FadeInMs`, `NEMUNO_FADE_IN_MS`; only the picture).
82. **(2026-10-27) Nemuno: the copies grow out, aim, flash, and are thrown.** Twice as far apart (`NEMUNO_COPY_GAP` 5 → 10 half widths); a copy widens from nothing as it slides out of the beam to its place; when they have grown, the place the player is at is taken and every beam turns round its own middle to aim at it over a beat (`NEMUNO_REAIM_BEATS`); they flash (whiter, faster and brighter, the field's attack flash) until they are thrown, each falling towards that place (was: all the way from her to the player). The hit follows the copy's width.
83. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: a bullet one black hole let go is not pulled by the next** (a pull with a release takes a bullet once: `Trajectory.Trace` keeps whether it has been pulled).
84. **(2026-10-27) Sharp big bullets.** The disc picture of the bullet sheet is baked at 128 px (was 64, as big as the other cells now: round bullets and ellipses stay sharp up to about twice a plain bullet's size), and every round bullet at least twice as big as a plain bullet of the map (`SHARP_BULLET_SIZE`, `BulletRadiusForCircle`) is drawn from the big picture as Utsuho's are (rings and stage bullets; frozen / tinted ones in their tint's colour).
85. **(2026-10-27) Enoko: a trap is set with the sound of Flandre's eye going off** (Mokou's impact, when its approach circle closes; it still sounds when it snaps shut).
86. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: big balls' size no longer adjustable** (`utsuho_ring_scale` removed from the boss menu; `UTSUHO_RING_SCALE` 10.8).
87. **(2026-10-27) Gengetsu: grey dim, radial blur, riser.** Over each attack, growing evenly (`BulletField.Haste`): the background dims grey up to 0.5 (`hasteBackground`, `GENGETSU_DIM`); every bullet gets three faint copies between it and the middle of the screen, the last 12 % of the way there (a zoom blur pointing at the middle: further out, longer; `GENGETSU_BLUR_*`); a quiet riser plays (new `Resources/Samples/gengetsu_riser.wav`, made by a scratch tool: 4 s of soft filtered noise and a low chord that loops without a seam), one loop for the whole attack, never restarted, its pitch rising evenly from 0.6x to 1.8x and its loudness from 0.05 to 0.3, at its top right as the attack ends, then dying away over 200 ms.
88. **(2026-10-27) Gengetsu: subtler blur, lighter golden dim.** The blur reaches half as far (`GENGETSU_BLUR_REACH` 0.12 → 0.06), is half as visible (`GENGETSU_BLUR_ALPHA` 0.35 → 0.175), has 2 copies instead of 3 ("twice less tails"), drawn soft-edged so they melt into a smooth smear ("twice more smooth"); the dim is a grey twice as bright with a touch of gold (0.2, 0.2, 0.22 → 0.44, 0.41, 0.32).
89. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: the pull speeds up towards the hole.** A pulled bullet speeds up evenly on its way, from the pull's speed (half its own) to 1.5 times that where it reaches the hole (new `TrajectoryOp.RedirectSpeedUp`, `UTSUHO_PULL_SPEED_UP`): a constant acceleration towards the hole, worked out from the distance so the speed is 1.5x exactly on arrival; the closer it is, the faster. A bullet the hole lets go before it arrives keeps its plain speed (no acceleration).
90. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: Gengetsu's radial blur.** The same blur (`hasteBlur`, now with a signed level, `radialBlurLevel`): in her attacks the tails point in at the middle, in her black hole's pauses out, away from it; full strength the whole phase, coming and going over 300 ms (`registerNukeBlur`). With Gengetsu on the screen too the two add up.
91. **(2026-10-27) Blur sharp again, Gengetsu's phases twice as long, the pull 1x to 2x.** The radial blur's copies are sharp-edged again (the soft edges of 88 rolled back, for Gengetsu and Utsuho; still 2 tails). Gengetsu's attacks and pauses last twice as long (`GENGETSU_PHASE_FACTOR` 2, like Utsuho's). The black hole's pull speeds up from 1x to 2x on the way in (`UTSUHO_PULL_SPEED_UP` 1.5 → 2).
92. **(2026-10-27) Radial blur (Gengetsu and Utsuho): one tail, half as visible.** `GENGETSU_BLUR_COPIES` 2 → 1 (the one copy sits the whole 6 % of the way), `GENGETSU_BLUR_ALPHA` 0.175 → 0.0875.
93. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho's big balls and Yukari's trains keep their size (corrected).** "Can't be resized" meant by other bosses' mechanics: the boss-menu size of the big balls is back (`utsuho_ring_scale`, 10.8; 86 undone), and new `BulletEmitter.KeepsSize` (the big balls and trains) is left alone by every resizing: `Scaled` (Sanae's half size, Ringo's...), Gengetsu's every-fourth-bigger (`BigScale`), Clownpiece's doubling (`doubleRing`), Sanae's and Mugetsu's rebuilt rings, and, on the field, Raiko's ripples, Sannyo's smoke, Sukuna's growth and a look's scale (picture and hitbox).
94. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: no blur.** Gengetsu's radial blur on Utsuho (90) is taken away again; Gengetsu keeps hers.
95. **(2026-10-27) Freeze and bodiless bullets with Utsuho + Clownpiece (reported with a screenshot).** Clownpiece doubles every bullet, so with the rule of 84 (twice a plain bullet's size and more = drawn as a sharp ball) every round bullet went that way: five pictures each in a layer of 320 (bodies past 64 balls were not drawn, only their glows: the "smooth lights"), plus two glows each, hundreds of pictures a frame. The game logic was not it (bench, Chirality 0:48 + 30 s, Utsuho + Clownpiece: 5.8 ms per frame, worst 22). Now a round bullet is drawn as a sharp ball only from 3.5 times a plain bullet's size (`SHARP_BULLET_SIZE` 2 → 3.5; up to that the renderer's 128 px disc is sharp at 1080p), at most 80 of them a frame besides Utsuho's (`SHARP_BULLETS_MAX`; the rest are drawn by the renderer), in a layer with room for them and hers.
96. **(2026-10-27) The game "froze for good" in Utsuho's black hole with Clownpiece (Little Raven [Solar Flare], ~1:19).** The bench did not reproduce it (whole map, Utsuho + Clownpiece: 9.3 ms per frame, worst 43, no hang). osu!'s log of that play: at 82.5 s "Ignoring likely invalid time value provided by BASS during gameplay", the gameplay clock half a second behind the audio, again and again, until the player quit: the update fell behind and never caught up. Likely cause: when a frame is heavy the clock slips back a little, and the field took every step back of more than 1 ms as a seek (`rewindTo`): it threw away what the moon and the black hole had taken and worked every ring out again from its start, which made the next frame heavier still. Now in play (not a replay) a step back of less than 1.5 s holds the field where it is until time has caught up (`CLOCK_SLIP_MS`); replay seeks and big jumps still rewind.
97. **(2026-10-27) Utsuho: her big balls hurt like a slider body** (not used up; slider damage every interval while the character is in one, the timer of Sannyo's smoke; new `BulletEmitter.HurtsLikeSlider`, carried by `copy` and Marisa's split). Read "Utsuho bullets" as the big balls.
98. **(2026-10-27) The freeze, from the user's video (freezing.mp4, 13.9 s).** Frames decoded with osu!'s own ffmpeg (a scratch tool, `vidframes`: osu!'s build has no file protocol, so the file is fed through a custom AVIO read/seek): the picture changes normally until 4.5 s, then stands still until the pause menu at 12.5 s, while osu!'s counter shows 1.1 ms / 160 fps - the game was running, only gameplay time stood still. osu!'s log of that play (16:03:57): "Ignoring likely invalid time value provided by BASS during gameplay", provided 84253.64 / expected 83752.89, the expected value never moving again: after the audio's time once jumped by just over 500 ms between two frames (a stall of about half a second somewhere), osu!'s gameplay clock took it for a glitch, kept its own time, and went on ignoring the audio, now ever further ahead: the gameplay locks for good (osu!'s behaviour). The bench of Utsuho + Clownpiece on that map shows no such frame in the field's update (worst 43 ms), so the stall is not found yet. Two things added: (1) the field writes every frame of its own that takes more than 150 ms, and every wait of more than 300 ms between two of its frames, to osu!'s runtime log with its slowest steps (`logSlowFrame`, `SLOW_FRAME_LOG_MS`; the profile steps are now measured every frame) - the next freeze will say whether it was the field and where; (2) when the gameplay time has stood still for 400 ms in play (not paused, not a replay, not failed) while the music plays on ahead, the field seeks the gameplay clock to the music's time (`unstickClock`, `CLOCK_STUCK_MS`; logged too): a lock-up becomes a short jump. The "clock slipping back" theory of 96 was wrong (the clock never went back; that change stays, harmless).
99. **(2026-10-27) The freeze found: the game falls behind the music in a crowded stretch (freeze 2.mp4).** osu!'s log: the gameplay clock locked again at 83 873 ms (last time 83 753: the same place), again just over 500 ms behind the music; none of the new slow-frame lines (no frame of the field over 150 ms) and the recovery of 98 never ran - the field is not updated at all once gameplay time stands still. Bench of the stretch before it (Little Raven [Solar Flare], Utsuho + Clownpiece): 1:10-1:22 average 16.1-16.3 ms per update, against osu!'s 16.7 ms steps (and the game's own work comes on top): a frame takes longer than the time it covers, osu! runs several steps per frame to catch up, each as costly, the game drifts further and further behind the music until it is half a second behind, and osu!'s clock locks. In the video about a third of the frames repeat for seconds before the stop. Fixes: (1) while osu! is catching up (`FrameStableClock.IsCatchingUp`) the field does not make the picture for the steps that are never seen (retiring and sorting the rings, every bullet, the stage's pictures, the moon's, the beams': `updateRenderer` returns after the screen bounds), so a catching-up step costs much less than one that is drawn and the game catches up instead of drifting; (2) the recovery moved to `DrawableDanmakusuRuleset.Update` (it runs every frame): when gameplay time has stood still for 400 ms in play, nothing paused, while the music plays on ahead, the gameplay clock is sought to the music's time (logged).
100. **(2026-10-27) Several cores.** Two steps of the field's update are shared out between the cores (at most `ProcessorCount - 3`, up to 8, leaving room for osu!'s drawing and sound; only from 64 live rings on): Clownpiece's moon taking bullets (`absorbIntoMoon`: every ring is looked at on its own and only its own bullets change; the pulled arrowheads, set up lazily, stay on one core) and finding the rings that are gone (`ringGone` on every core, the retiring and the snapshot pool afterwards in order). The results are the same whatever order the cores finish in (each ring's work only reads the field and writes the ring). Bench, Little Raven [Solar Flare], Utsuho + Clownpiece, 1:16 + 6 s: 15.95 → 11.81 ms per update (moon 4.00 → 0.90, retire and sort 2.74 → 1.78); whole map 9.34 → 6.56. Every boss alone (1:00 + 20 s) runs without errors. The bench prints every step with the environment variable `BENCH_ALL_STEPS`.
101. **(2026-10-28) Several cores, second step: collisions and the stage scan.** Collisions: every ring is checked against the character on its own core (`collideRing`: it only marks its own bullets used up and keeps its own snapshot; the snapshot pool is locked, a snapshot being cleared when handed out), writing what it hit (`ringHits`, `ringSliderTouch`); then, ring by ring and bullet by bullet in the same order as before, the damage is done, the hits recorded and the slider timer set (`sliderTouch`), so the health and the replays are as they were. Stage scan (what the black hole, the planets, the UFOs, the prism, the pile... take, push and catch): where every bullet is at every 10 ms step of its scan is worked out first, ring by ring on several cores (`precomputeScan`, `ScanBuffer`), and then the acts go through the rings in order as before, using those places; a bullet an act pushes or catches during the scan is worked out again live from then on (`changed`). Bench, Little Raven [Solar Flare], Utsuho + Clownpiece, 1:16 + 6 s: 11.81 → 9.95 ms per update (collisions 1.74 → 0.41, absorb 2.67 → 2.02; worst 16.6 → 14.7); whole map 6.56 → 5.67; every boss alone (1:00 + 20 s) without errors. Not done: the beams (1.4 ms there, and their part of the zones' 1.0): their points, the moon's memory of slices, Zanmu's, Flandre's and Okina's caches are shared and would all have to be made safe for several cores - much risk for 1-2 ms.
102. **(2026-10-28) Utsuho: her big balls never come sooner than their interval.** Every attack started its own series of rings of big balls at its start, so when an attack (or a new section) began shortly after the last ring of the one before, rings came one right after the other and filled the screen. Now the series goes on over the whole map: a ring comes at the soonest one interval after the last one (`utsuhoLastBig`). The mechanics now go through the sections in the order they come in the map (`planMechanics` sorts them by their start; a stable sort, so nothing changes where they already were in order).
103. **(2026-10-28) Hecatia (and Unzan's hands): one even ring, held bullets unseen.** What a world (or a hand) holds is shot out as one ring, every bullet its own way, spread evenly over the 180 degrees away from the wall, all at the same speed (were rings of four stacked by speed; `GATHER_RING_BULLETS` and `GATHER_RING_SLOWER` gone). Held bullets are not drawn (`GATHER_SHOW_HELD` false; true brings back the disc under the world).
104. **(2026-10-28) Okina: a beam's teleport stays where it first was.** Every slice of a beam was taken through the portals as they were at the moment it reached the wall; the portals slide (one follows Okina, one the player), so each slice went in and came out somewhere else and the beam behind the second portal moved about. Now the first moment a beam goes through is kept (`PortalAct.beamEntered`, per beam; forgotten for what came later when time goes back) and every later slice of it uses the portals as they were then (`Transfer` takes the beam; the old body is `transferThrough`).
105. **(2026-10-28) Clownpiece: her stars no longer vanish in the middle of the screen with Utsuho.** A star of the moon's lines is worked out afresh every frame and was dropped once older than its life (the screen's diagonal at its speed, longer only for freezes). Utsuho's black hole turns them back towards the middle at half their speed: they took much longer than that and vanished on the way. A thing a pull turns back now lives twice its life more plus 2 s (`flyWithOps`, `PULLED_EXTRA_LIFE_MS`), and the moon builds its stars that far back (`pullOpsOn`); it is gone where the pull takes it, or off the screen. Bench (Little Raven [Solar Flare], 1:00 + 30 s): Utsuho + Clownpiece 8.8 ms per update, Okina + Clownpiece 7.3, Hecatia + Okina 4.9, Ichirin + Utsuho 5.8, no errors.
106. **(2026-10-28) Hecatia (and Unzan's hands): five even rings at an impact** (103's one ring → five). What was held is dealt out in turn into five rings of (nearly) as many (`GATHER_RINGS` 5), each spread evenly over the 180 degrees away from the wall (full circle where it was before), shot one after the other an eighth of a beat apart (`GATHER_RING_GAP_BEATS` 0.125), all at the same speed; the second ring is turned half a step one way and the fourth the other way, so their bullets fly between the others'. Fewer than five held: one ring each.
107. **(2026-10-28) A star rating by bullet density (first version, not in the game yet).** New `Bullets/BulletDensity.cs`: the map is planned without bosses (only the rings, spinner stars and slider beams of the map itself); every 50 ms of the song it adds up how much of the playfield the bullets cut through per second (a bullet sweeps a strip its hit width + the hitbox wide at its speed while it is on the playfield; aimed rings taken as aimed at the middle; bouncing bullets on the playfield for their whole life) and the beams cover (a beam counts as cutting the stretch it covers every 500 ms, `DENSITY_BEAM_SWEEP_MS`); the density at a moment is that of about the last second (`DENSITY_MEMORY_MS`); the densest moment of every 400 ms part, from the densest down, each counting 0.9 times the one before (like osu!'s), make the value; stars = 0.45 x value ^ 0.6 (`DENSITY_STAR_SCALE` / `_POWER`, fitted so that the stars come out closest to osu!'s). New tool mode `sectioncheck stars <maps>` prints it next to osu!'s stars. On the 24 example difficulties (Chirality, Cyaegha, Blows Up Everything, epimerization, Little Raven): rank correlation with osu!'s 0.88; the same map gets 2.2-8.0 stars against osu!'s 2.4-7.6; beams are 1-5 % of the density; 30-270 ms per difficulty. Differences: Drop's Extra (Cyaegha) 7.95 against osu!'s 6.48 (the densest stretch of all: 12.9 playfields per second at 1:00), Asymmetry (Chirality) 6.55 against 7.56, Easy (Chirality) 2.24 against 2.82. Still osu!'s rating in the game (`CreateDifficultyCalculator`) until the numbers are agreed.
108. **(2026-10-28) The star rating by bullet density is the game's, beams weigh four times as much.** A beam counts as cutting the stretch it covers every 125 ms (`DENSITY_BEAM_SWEEP_MS` 500 → 125): beams are now 3-16 % of the density (were 1-5 %). Refitted to osu!'s stars: `DENSITY_STAR_SCALE` 0.45 → 0.41 (power stays 0.6); on the example maps 2.3-7.9 stars, rank correlation with osu!'s 0.86. New `DanmakusuDifficultyCalculator` (osu!'s calculator with the star rating replaced: aim, speed and so the pp stay osu!'s) is what `CreateDifficultyCalculator` makes now; the rate of DT / HT (and the CS / AR of HR / EZ, through the map) count. Only the whole map is rated that way: the rating object by object for a pp counter keeps osu!'s (it would plan the map again for every object), and a map the planner fails on keeps osu!'s too (logged). The `stars` tool mode prints what the game's calculator gives (`game`, `DT`), the same as the density's.
109. **(2026-10-28) Lily White: half the flowers in a spinner, small petals.** In a spinner her rings have 6 flowers instead of 12 (`LILY_SPINNER_RING_BULLETS`; her other rings keep 12). Every petal her flowers burst into is as small as the petals of Yuyuko's tree, hitbox too: the branch flowers' petals are exactly as big as the petal they were (`LILY_PETAL_SIZE` 2 gone), her flower bullets' petals have the radius of an average tree petal (`LILY_PETAL_RADIUS` = `YUYUKO_PETAL_SIZE` x 0.5 x `YUYUKO_TREE_HEIGHT` x `SCREEN_HEIGHT` 480, ≈5.4 px; were 2x a spinner star's).
110. **(2026-10-28) Cirno: shards twice as fast.** `cirno_burst_speed` default 0.25 → 0.5 (boss menu; the adjustments are not saved, so it applies).
111. **(2026-10-28) Sunny Milk: every second bullet is not split.** A bullet with an odd number in its ring still turns along the prism's side and comes out the mirrored way, but as one bullet with its own size, colour and look instead of seven small rainbow ones (`PrismAct` `Crossing.Whole`).
112. **(2026-10-28) Utsuho: her big balls cannot be destroyed.** Nothing takes or catches them any more: every stage act's `Takes` / `Catches` is skipped for them (Nue's UFOs, Hecatia's worlds and Unzan's hands, Sunny's prism, Reimu's pile, Zanmu's...; pushes still act: bounces, Okina's portals), Clownpiece's moon lets them through (`scanMoonOf`), and Cirno's freeze holds them but neither removes them (`Remove` ops skip `Sharp` rings) nor bursts them into shards. They still hurt like a slider body (not used up).
113. **(2026-10-28) Yukari: only every second bullet freezes.** In her pauses only the even bullets of every ring freeze and turn into talismans (`EveryOther` on her freeze and on the pull to the middle); the others fly on. The frozen ones stay talismans while they are pulled to the middle (checked with the new tool mode `sectioncheck looks <map> yukari`: Chirality [Expert] 0:53, 594 → 310 talismans, all still talismans after the pause; homing arrowheads and bouncing stars are frozen as whole rings as before). Rejected first reading: no talisman at all.
114. **(2026-10-28) Sorting by difficulty (question, nothing changed).** osu!'s song select sorts by the star rating it saved for the map's own mode (`BeatmapInfo.StarRating`, osu!standard's; `BeatmapCarouselFilterSorting.compare` / `compareDifficulty`), never by the rating a ruleset shows. Since 108 the stars shown are the bullet density's, so the order no longer follows them. A ruleset cannot change what osu! sorts by.
115. **(2026-10-30) The nerf: fewer bullets per ring, speed scales linearly.** Bullets per ring by OD 0..10 (`BulletsPerRing`): base 10..40 → 1..20, ellipses (clap) 10..30 → 1..15, stars (finish) always 5 → 1..10, homing arrowheads (whistle) 1..3 → 1..5; slider ticks, arrows and ends follow as half of base (5..20 → 1..10), Mystia's and Seija's rings as base; the bosses' own fixed rings stay. In kiai the OD part still grows half as fast. Speed: `SliderSpeedFactor` and `LocalBpmFactor` take the ratio itself instead of its square root (clamps stay: 0.36..3.24, 0.25..4), read as both speed scalings. Above a factor of 1 linear is faster, not slower: SM 2 was x1.20 and is now x1.43, SM 2.5 x1.34 → x1.79, SM 1 x0.85 → x0.71, and high-SV stretches more. Measured with `sectioncheck stars` on the 24 example difficulties: bullets 28-56 % fewer (least on the hardest maps, where stars and arrowheads went up), but the density value rose on 19 and fell only on the 5 easiest (Chirality [Easy] 17.0 → 10.8; Chirality [Asymmetry] 92.8 → 100.9; Cyaegha [Drop's Extra] 134 → 183; Blows Up Everything [OTOSAKA's ReMASTER+] 131 → 217), so the stars are now 1.7-10.3 (were 2.3-7.9): the value counts bullets x speed. `DENSITY_STAR_SCALE` / `_POWER` not refitted (the new fit would be 0.80 / 0.43), until the speed is settled. Every boss alone on Chirality [Easy] (0:48 + 12 s) without errors, worst 3.1 ms per update.
116. **(2026-10-30) Lily White: half the spinner's flowers again.** Every shot of the spinner's spiral was a whole ring of her flowers (at OD 10 eight rings a beat, 48 flowers a beat after 109, against 6 a beat in her attacks). Now only every second shot is one (`LILY_SPINNER_SHOT_EVERY` 2, by count, so it is the same every play): 24 flowers a beat at OD 10, and the petals with them; rings stay at 6 flowers.
117. **(2026-10-30) Speed scaling rolled back to the square root** (115's linear undone in `SliderSpeedFactor` and `LocalBpmFactor`; the bullet counts of 115 stay). **Lily White: her branches always grow at the same speed.** A section shorter than the 16 beats of growth squeezed the whole growth into 85 % of the section; now the growth always takes 16 beats (`LilyAct.growMs`) and a short section only grows the branches part of the way (they grow back from there); a flower only sits where the branches get to before the section ends. Yuyuko's tree keeps the old rule (not asked).
118. **(2026-10-30) Star rating: bouncing bullets fixed, rescaled.** `BulletDensity` counted a bouncing bullet as on the playfield for its whole `Lifetime` (6000 / speed: up to about a minute), but it bounces once (`MAX_RICOCHETS`) and leaves. Now it flies to the edge, back across, and is gone (`bounced`). So stars weighed far too much, which is most of what 115 reported: the rise on 19 maps was this bug together with stars going from 5 to 1..10, not mainly the speed (with the square root back the values still rose: Drop's Extra 134 → 184). Fixed measure, sqrt speed, old against new counts of 115 on the 24 example difficulties: density -32 % to +1 %, median -6 %. Refitted: `DENSITY_STAR_SCALE` 0.41 → 1.22, `DENSITY_STAR_POWER` 0.6 → 0.43; stars 2.5-7.5; the match with osu!'s order is weaker (rank correlation 0.83 → 0.66): some maps are far off (Cyaegha [Normal] 4.69 against osu!'s 2.45, Solar Flare 5.12 against 7.17).
119. **(2026-10-30) HP: damage and regeneration rescaled.** A bullet takes 5 % (HP 0) to 20 % (HP 10) of the bar (was 4 % to 16 %), a slider damage tick the same as before (they are defined as equal; beams are a fraction of the tick, so they follow). Regeneration is 10 % of the bar per second on every map (`HP_REGENERATION_PER_SECOND`; was osu!'s drain rate for the map x 1 to 4, `RegenerationScale` and the probe in `DanmakusuHealthProcessor` gone).
120. **(2026-10-30) Enoko: a red glow round the held character.** While a trap holds it the character has a soft additive red glow (the trap rim's red; `ENOKO_HELD_GLOW_SIZE` 64 px, `_ALPHA` 0.9, in and out over `_FADE_MS` 120), above the bullets (`stageTop`), read as the character for "frozen cursor". Benches of Lily White and Enoko (Chirality [Expert] 0:48 + 30 s) without errors.
121. **(2026-10-30) The zip on Google Drive is updated by every build.** `archive.ps1` overwrites `osu.Game.Rulesets.Danmakusu.zip` in the shared folder of the second Google account's drive (`-DriveRoot` `I:\`, `-DriveFolder` `<shared folder>\Danmakusu`, looked for in every top folder of the drive, so My Drive's name in any language works and the script stays plain ASCII) in place, so that the shared link stays; it never creates one, and a failure does not stop the build.
122. **(2026-10-30) The game stood still at the start of a map, only the music played (+ERABY+E CONNEC+10N [OU+W0RLD D1REC+10N]).** osu!'s log: the gameplay clock started, gameplay stopped at -1040 ms (-973 on the second try) with the clock 782 ms ahead, and `unstickClock` (98, 99) tried again and again without any effect. osu!'s `FrameStabilityContainer.updateClock`, read from its IL: with no replay, when the clock is more than 500 ms from gameplay and that frame did not itself take over 500 ms, gameplay time does not move, on every frame, for good. So 98/99's fix could never work: it sought the clock forward onto the music, further away. Now the clock is sought back to where gameplay stands (`CLOCK_LOCK_MS` 500, after standing still for `CLOCK_STUCK_MS` 400 → 100): the music jumps back by that little and gameplay goes on. The hitch itself is not found: every start of a map has a stall of about 400 ms, here a second one followed; the field took only 1-9 ms in those frames. Not reproducible headless (the bench has no osu! clock), so not tested in play yet.
123. **(2026-10-30) Byakuren: her beams always home; bigger stars, thinner beams, fewer spots.** Why some did not home: a beam starts pointing exactly away from the player, and a homing thing only turns by the sideways part of the pull, which is nothing at exactly behind it and small near it; with the player standing still it flew dead straight and left the screen, which ended it (leaving the playfield ended it at once, too, from a boss or a spot near the edge). Now a head that points away turns at least half the full turn (`BYAKUREN_TURN_FLOOR` 0.5, `Style.TurnFloor`, one way round, `Head.Side`; dead straight: by its number; Sekibanki's heads keep the old rule), and a head that goes past the edge while it still homes comes back (it ends there only after its 8 beats). Simulated with the same step and numbers (CS 5, player still, 120 and 180 BPM, AR 9 and 10): four throws that used to fly off without coming closer now pass within 46 px at most. Homing stays 8 beats (doubled, then asked to be left out). Tip star 2x, hitbox too (`BYAKUREN_TIP_STAR_SIZE`); the star on a remembered spot 2x, picture and what hurts (`BYAKUREN_SPOT_STAR_SIZE`); homing beam width halved (`BYAKUREN_TRAIL_WIDTH` 2 → 1; her spinner's beams unchanged); she remembers every tenth circle or slider (`BYAKUREN_RECORD_EVERY` 5 → 10; undone in 124).
124. **(2026-10-30) Byakuren: 10 remembered spots at a time (123's reading corrected).** She remembers every fifth circle or slider again (`BYAKUREN_RECORD_EVERY` 10 → 5), but holds at most 10 spots waiting to fire (`BYAKUREN_MAX_SPOTS`), counted over the whole map, so spots left from an earlier section count too: with 10 waiting the next one is not remembered, and when one has fired there is room again.
125. **(2026-10-30) Byakuren: homing twice as long.** `BYAKUREN_HOMING_BEATS_LIMIT` 8 → 16: her beams turn towards the player for 16 beats, then fly straight on; only after that does leaving the playfield end them. (123 left this out on request; now asked for.)
126. **(2026-10-30) Byakuren: 5 remembered spots at a time.** `BYAKUREN_MAX_SPOTS` 10 → 5.
127. **(2026-10-30) Enoko: two beats between the traps of a set.** `ENOKO_TRAP_GAP_BEATS` 2 (was Flandre's 1); the sets stay 16 beats apart.
128. **(2026-10-30) Urumi: what has been hit falls on the player.** New `ObjectFallAct`: in her section every circle at its hit and every slider at its end turns grey (`TINT_GRAY`) and falls, as Zanmu's bullets fall (`ZANMU_GRAVITY`, darkening `ZANMU_FALL_SHADE` over `ZANMU_FALL_DARKEN_MS`), each the straight way to the player as they were when it began, and keeps that way; a slider falls whole, keeping its shape. From the field's `CircleZone` / `SliderZone` (so Doremy's and Sagume's copies fall too); spinners do not. A circle hurts like a bullet (its hitbox the usual share of the osu! circle) and a slider like a slider body, both only after `URUMI_FALL_GRACE_MS` 300, so that standing in a circle as it is hit does not cost twice. They are drawn by the act, under the bullets, with a slight glow (`URUMI_FALL_GLOW` 0.25, 2.8x its size), not as the field's big bullets, whose glow is strong; sliders are plain beams. `stageOrbs` has room for 600 more pictures. Gone after `URUMI_FALL_MS` 7000. The zones are now set before the stage is built in `BulletField` (the act read them: they were null, found by the bench). Bench (Chirality [Expert] 0:48 + 45 s): Urumi 2.6 ms per update, with Enoko 1.9, Zanmu 3.3, Doremy 2.5, Sagume 2.6, no errors.
129. **(2026-10-30) Urumi: falling circles hurt like sliders.** They hurt like a slider body, every `SLIDER_DAMAGE_INTERVAL_MS` while the character touches them, as far as they are drawn (the osu! circle's radius), and are not used up by a hit (128: like a bullet, used up, the bullet hitbox share). They are no bullets of the act any more, only its own objects, so nothing else can take them.
130. **(2026-10-30) Zanmu: stopped bullets divide into three.** `ZANMU_SPLIT_PIECES` 4 → 3 (asked for as "3 instead of 5"; it was 4).
131. **(2026-10-30) Urumi: falling objects look like the real ones; smaller swell; no previews.** The falling circles and sliders are now real osu! circles and sliders (new `UI/FallLayer.cs`), like Doremy's copies, skin and combo number included, with a grey combo colour (`URUMI_FALL_COLOUR`). Each stands on a clock of its own, stopped 1 ms before it is due, so that it stays whole and is never judged; the layer only moves and darkens it. Under the bullets (`Depth` 2), so that they hide none (Doremy's copies are above them). The light round them stays the act's (along a slider too). `CircleZone` / `SliderZone` got `Source`. With no layer (the boss menu's preview, the bench) the act still draws the plain ones of 128. Announced bullets swell half as much (`URUMI_SIZE` 2 → 1.5, hitbox too; first asked to go, then to be halved). The faint copies that showed where bullets would be after a push are gone (`URUMI_GHOST_VISIBLE`, `PushPlan.GhostTime`). Not tested in play: the bench has no drawables.
132. **(2026-10-30) Urumi: only every eighth bullet swells; less glow on bullets, more on what falls.** Of what a push catches, every eighth bullet swells (`URUMI_SWELL_EVERY` 8; by the ring's time plus the bullet's number, since her rings can have fewer than eight); the others are still caught, grey, shaken and pushed, at their own size. Bullets glow half as much (`URUMI_BLOOM` 0.8 → 0.4), the falling circles and sliders twice as much (`URUMI_FALL_GLOW` 0.25 → 0.5).
133. **(2026-10-30) Urumi: half the shake.** What a push has caught shakes 0.5-1 px instead of 1-2 (`URUMI_SHAKE` 0.5 of `FROZEN_SHAKE_*`; Zanmu's and Cirno's stay).
134. **(2026-10-30) Flandre: smaller eyes, half as often.** Her eyes and their explosion are half the size (`flandre_eye_radius` default 4.5 → 2.25; the boss menu's slider still goes from 1 to 8). Three eyes every 8 beats instead of 4 (`FLANDRE_THROW_EVERY_BEATS`), two beats apart instead of one (`FLANDRE_EYE_GAP_BEATS`). Enoko's traps are built on these numbers and stay as they were: `ENOKO_TRAP_SIZE` 0.5 → 1 (the same size), `ENOKO_RARER` 4 → 2 (still every 16 beats).
135. **(2026-10-30) Enoko: traps last 32 beats.** A trap nobody has stepped into snaps shut by itself 32 beats after it was put down (`ENOKO_TRAP_LIFE_BEATS`, 4 of them the approach circle), with the closed jaws and the sound, catching no one (they stayed until 6 others pushed them out).
136. **(2026-10-30) Flandre: eyes half as big again.** `flandre_eye_radius` default 2.25 → 3.375 (the slider stays 1 to 8). Enoko's traps no longer follow it: `ENOKO_TRAP_RADIUS` 2.25 circle radii, their size as it was (`ENOKO_TRAP_SIZE` gone), so the boss menu's eye slider does not change them either.
138. **(2026-10-30) Byakuren: smaller tip stars, beams that narrow out, tails on her stars.** Tip star 2 → 1.5 times a spinner star's size (`BYAKUREN_TIP_STAR_SIZE`, 75 %). Her homing beams narrow to nothing over the last half of a slice's 4 beats, so a beam ends in a point (`BYAKUREN_TRAIL_NARROW_SHARE` 0.5, `Style.NarrowShare`; `addPlainBeam` takes widths; what hurts narrows with it). New passive (`UI/BulletField.Tails.cs`): every eighth ricochet star fired in her sections, whoever fires it, has a beam for a tail, the last 2 beats of its way, round the bounce too, narrowing behind it, 0.4 of the star's radius at most (`BYAKUREN_STAR_TAIL_*`, `BulletEmitter.TailMs`, set at the end of `Plan`). It hurts like a laser. Which stars: by the ring's time plus the bullet's number, as for Urumi's swell.
139. **(2026-10-30) Hecatia and Ichirin: more room between the rings of an impact.** `GATHER_RING_GAP_BEATS` 0.125 → 0.25.
140. **(2026-10-30) Meiling: her ball impacts, ricochets once, fades.** When the middle of the thrown ball reaches a wall it impacts like Mokou's ball (`MEILING_IMPACT_RINGS` 20 of her rings of 8, her gap, twist and speed; her shake, flash and sound) and ricochets; at the next wall it impacts again, stops and fades out by shrinking over `MEILING_FADE_MS` 300. A ball that a hit has used up makes no impact. It was a straight bullet that left the screen. The other bosses' ops no longer steer it (`Ops` off: its two legs are worked out when it is thrown), and a ball still flying after her section is still drawn.
141. **(2026-10-30) Yorihime: she copies beams too, half as wide.** In her attacks every slider's beam whose front reaches the border of the screen is copied from where she is then, towards the place it got to: a straight beam half as wide (`YORIHIME_RETURN_FACTOR`, the same boss-menu number as her bullets), at the beam's own speed, fired as long as the original, hurting like a slider body (`ReturnAct.copyBeams`). All of them, not every second as her bullets; beams that turn and beams fired from a boss are not copied.
142. **(2026-11-01) Byakuren: red arrows for her stars outside the screen.** While the star at the tip of one of her homing beams is outside the screen, a red arrowhead on the edge of the screen points at it (`HeadAct.Beads`, `BYAKUREN_ARROW_SIZE` 2 plain bullets; it cannot hit).
143. **(2026-11-01) Byakuren: tails on every fourth star, 8 beats long, smaller tip stars; the tail follows the star's way exactly.** `BYAKUREN_STAR_TAIL_EVERY` 8 → 4, `BYAKUREN_STAR_TAIL_BEATS` 2 → 8, `BYAKUREN_TIP_STAR_SIZE` 1.5 → 0.75. The reported misaligned start was measured (`TAIL_PROBE=1 sectioncheck field`, Dynamite [DARKNESS], 282,443 tails): the first point of a tail is on the star's centre, always under a quarter pixel. What was off was the bend: the tail was 16 samples of the last 2 beats, which cut the corner at a bounce (at 8 beats that would have been a quarter of a beat wide). It is now the star, every exact place it turned (`BulletEmitter.TurnsBetween`: walls and pushes), and where it was 8 beats ago, so it is on the star's way all along; what hurts narrows along the pieces. A tail stays after its star has left the screen until its end has followed it out, also past the end of the ring (`Lifetime` + the tail).
144. **(2026-11-01) Gengetsu: attacks twice as long, 1x to 2x, on every bullet.** Attacks 32 → 64 beats (`GENGETSU_ATTACK_FACTOR` 2 on top of `GENGETSU_PHASE_FACTOR`), pauses stay 32. `GENGETSU_SPEED_UP` 1.5 → 2. The speed-up was already on every ring (a clock of `BossEffects`); stage bullets that no ops layer traces flew on real time, and now fly on that clock too with Gengetsu on the field (`stageClock`; not what an act lays down step by step or what orbits something). Beams keep their speed, as do what acts steer step by step (bats, heads).
145. **(2026-11-01) Gengetsu: speed lines, cleaner and louder riser.** Anime speed lines over each attack: streaks from the edge of the screen towards the middle, under the bullets, 0 to 80 of them and brighter as the attack goes on, new ones every 50 ms (`drawSpeedLines`, `GENGETSU_SPEED_LINE*`). `gengetsu_riser.wav` remade as a soft detuned chord without the noise (4 s, loops without a seam, made by a scratch tool); `GENGETSU_RISER_VOLUME_*` 0.05-0.3 → 0.15-0.7.
146. **(2026-11-01) Meiling: ball twice as fast, impact rings from its middle.** `MEILING_BALL_SPEED` 2 (twice the average of what was fed in). The rings of an impact start at the ball's middle, not at its edge.
147. **(2026-11-01) Utsuho: big balls always from her, one alarm at a time, the rays start aimed at the player.** Her big balls come out of where she is drawn (`bossVisualOf`; they used the delayed shot position, so at the start of an attack, while she flew to the middle, they came from behind her), and Chen no longer takes them over (`ShotFromBoss` skips `Sharp`). `nuclear_alarm` is left out while the last one still sounds (`alarmChannel`). The black hole's rays start aimed at where the player was when the warning came and turn away from there at the same speed (they started 90 degrees off and turned towards it).
148. **(2026-11-01) Mugetsu: curl at an even speed.** A curling bullet goes round the centre at a constant speed, `MUGETSU_CURL` 0.4 of its speed outwards, however far out it is (a logarithmic spiral, counted from 16 px out); it was a constant angle, 40 degrees a second, so the speed round grew with the distance.
149. **(2026-11-01) Hecatia: throws twice as fast, a flash where bullets bounce.** `HECATIA_THROW_SPEED` 1.1 → 2.2. Where a bullet bounces off the Earth or the Moon a flash shows on the surface (`pushFlashes`, `HECATIA_PUSH_FLASH_MS` 220, `_SIZE` 1.6 circle radii). Checked, nothing to change: both the Earth and the Moon already push in the turning phase, and what they shoot out at an impact is stage bullets, which `Takes` and `Pushes` never see.
150. **(2026-11-01) Marisa: the beam stops growing at 5 circle radii.** A Master Spark swells from 0.8 circle radii to `MARISA_SPARK_WIDE` 5 circle radii from its middle to each side (`MARISA_SPARK_THICKNESS` no longer scales that), over `MARISA_SPARK_GROW` 10 circle radii of its length, ever more slowly, so it runs smoothly into the straight beam; the cone after it is gone (`MARISA_SPARK_SPREAD_DEGREES`, `SpreadTan`). It was 3.2 circle radii and then 4 degrees open on each side.
151. **(2026-11-01) Wriggle Nightbug: fireflies (`Swarm`, no longer a placeholder).** All through her section every sixteenth bullet on the screen (`WRIGGLE_EVERY`, by the ring's time plus the bullet's number) becomes a stage bullet of `SwarmAct`: twice as big, bright green, with bloom and a pulsing green glow, drifting on at 0.4 of its speed and wandering about 1.5 circle radii to each side (`StageBullet.Wander`, two slow waves, a closed form of time). She shoots stars as the placeholder did, and her spinner's stars stay stars.
152. **(2026-11-01) Saki Kurokoma: new mechanic `Stampede`.** Her attacks are Remilia's (`FateSpear`'s planner case: rings denser and turning further as the attack goes on, every fortieth bullet three times as big; everything glows more and more, 2 px shake as it starts) without the red: the background dims brown (`stampedeBackground`, `SAKI_BACKGROUND_ALPHA` 0.6) and a giant see-through horse's head shows on it (`StageTexture.HorseHead`, a baked outline; `SAKI_HORSE_*`). Every bullet on the screen speeds up evenly to twice its speed over an attack and back over the pause (two windows of `BossEffects`, `SAKI_SPEED_UP` 2; stage bullets too, `stageOnClock`). She shoots nothing but rings of beams, every 4 beats all the time, in a spinner too (`LunaAct` with `SAKI_RING_ARCS` 6 arcs of 85 %, `Smooth` false, so a beam ring can be slipped through; the spinner's stars are gone).
153. **(2026-11-01) Shizuha Aki: new mechanic `Autumn`.** She shoots autumn leaves (orange petals, the spinner's stars too). In her attacks every second bullet of a ring turns orange, more and more (`autumnLevel`). When a pause starts each of those that is on the screen is a leaf of `LeavesAct`: it stops and falls, swaying, at an eighth of Zanmu's pull (`SHIZUHA_FALL`, `StageBullet.FallScale`), slides along the bottom to a place of its own (by the golden ratio, so they lie evenly spread however many there are) and when the next attack starts falls upwards at the same pull, 5 degrees to the left or right, half each way. The leaves hurt like bullets all along, also lying on the bottom.
154. **(2026-11-01) Yuuka Kazami: sunflowers (`Flowers`, no longer a placeholder).** All through her section every eighth bullet that goes out of the bottom of the screen grows a sunflower there (`SunflowerAct`, at most `YUUKA_MAX_FLOWERS` 48 standing). The heads are alike: dark brown middle, eight yellow petals. The stems are not: height, lean and bend of their own, two leaves, drawn like tree wood (`AddStems`). The stem does not hurt, the head hurts like a slider body (a bead). After 8 beats the stem shrinks back, the head flies up 5 degrees to the left or right by turns, and its petals fly away as a ring of 8 yellow petals, which are bullets. Her own shots stay stars.
155. **(2026-11-01) Joon Yorigami: new mechanic `Fortune`.** She shoots round golden bullets that fall at a quarter of Zanmu's pull (the spinner's stars too). Every 4 beats of her section every tenth bullet on the screen (`FortuneAct`, by the ring's time plus the bullet's number) turns gold, glows and falls at Zanmu's pull from where it was, keeping its velocity; it bounces off the sides and the bottom as a ball, twice, keeping half its energy each time (`JOON_ENERGY_KEPT`), and then falls out. In an attack the share grows from every tenth to every second by its end and starts over with the next attack. The flight is worked out when the bullet turns (a `Trail`).

Every 8 beats of her section she stamps (`ShockwaveAct`): a smooth ring at Raiko's ripple speed goes out from where she is drawn, the screen shakes 1-2 px, Mokou's impact sound. A bullet the ring touches is thrown straight out at 2.5x its speed right by her, down to 1.2x at half the screen's width, glowing as much as it is near her (`YUUGI_PUSH_*`, `YUUGI_BLOOM`); it is a stage bullet from then on, so a later ring leaves it alone.
157. **(2026-11-01) Chiyari Tenkajin: new mechanic `Bloodlust`.** Everything she shoots is one of Koakuma's bats that does not home and flies until it leaves the screen (`BatAct` with `straight`; other bullets do not turn into bats). In her attacks three rings of 3 pools of blood turn round her, at 3, 5 and 7 circle radii, each the other way round (`BloodAct`, `CHIYARI_*`; a pool is 2 circle radii across, so the rings just touch). A bullet that touches a pool turns red and glowing as at the end of Remilia's attack, and is aimed at where the player is then, for good. The pools do not hurt.

When an attack begins the way of the line from her to the player is taken (as `DeathAct` takes the way of a fall: the same for everything, and it does not change), and every bullet on the screen, also what is fired during the attack, turns that way once, at its own speed (`StampedeAct.Pushes`). What falls, homes or has ops of its own keeps its way (`CanBeDeflected`); stage bullets of other acts too.
159. **(2026-11-01) Hisami Yomotsu: new mechanic `Recording`.** She shoots four rings of 12 plain bullets at once every 2 beats, each ring slower than the last (`addDoubleRings` with layers; the spinner's stars are plain bullets). Every 4 beats of an attack everything within 2 circle radii of the player is recorded (`RecordAct`): the bullets fly on, each leaving a see-through preview where it was, which stays there wherever the player goes; 2 beats later the previews turn solid and fly off with the bullet's size and velocity. A faint ring marks the place. Every slider's beam fired in her attacks is fired again 4 beats later from the same place (`LaserEmitter.Later`).
160. **(2026-11-01) Tenshi Hinanawi: earthquake (`Earthquake`, no longer a placeholder).** She shoots ricochet stars (the spinner's stars too). All through her section every eighth bullet that goes out of the bottom is shot back up as 3 bullets half its size, fanned out over 120 degrees (`QuakeAct`, `TENSHI_*`). Every 8 beats a shockwave goes out from her, twice as fast as Raiko's ripples, with Mokou's impact sound; the screen shakes for as long as a wave is on it, and meanwhile every bullet that goes out of any wall the wave has reached comes back the same way, straight in from that wall.
161. **(2026-11-01) Eiki Shiki: her circle (`Judgement`, no longer a placeholder).** Very dense rings of talismans (48 every 2 beats; the spinner's stars are talismans). In her attacks a circle stands in the middle of the screen, 4 circle radii from middle to edge (`JudgementAct`); the character cannot leave it by any way of moving (`Limit`, after cursor, keys and teleport); it does not hurt. Everything flying and fired meanwhile, bullets and beam slices, is sent once at the middle at half its speed and flies on through it (a `Redirect` op shared by bullets and beams, `ReleaseAt` never). Bullets outside are dim (`StageDim.Outside`). It fades out in the pause.
162. **(2026-11-01) Chimata Tenkyuu: new mechanic `Market`.** Every 4 beats of her section two walls of talismans without a gap come in, from the top and bottom, the next time from the left and right (`MarketAct`). Everything that comes within 2 circle radii of the player, talismans and ring bullets alike, turns into a blue-handled knife and creeps at where the player was at a quarter of its speed, for good. Every 2 beats (every beat in a spinner) a ring of 20 talismans 5 circle radii round the player closes in on where they are. She shoots nothing else (the spinner's stars are gone).
163. **(2026-11-01) Eiki: the circle is placed round the player.** When an attack begins the circle is placed where the character is and stays there for that attack (161 had it in the middle of the screen). What is sent goes at that place (`TrajectoryOp.PointKept`: `JudgementAct.Limit` sets the op's point in the first frame of the attack, before any bullet moves). After a seek back a circle placed before that moment stays where it was.
164. **(2026-11-01) Koakuma: bats home like Byakuren's beams.** Read as an instruction ("koakuma homing is similar to byakuren now"). A bat homes for its first 16 beats (`KOAKUMA_HOMING_BEATS`), then flies straight on; pointing away from the player it still turns, one way round (`KOAKUMA_TURN_FLOOR` 0.5, as `HeadAct.advance`); and leaving the screen only ends it once its homing is over. It homed for as long as it lived, could get stuck flying straight away from the player, and was gone at the edge of the screen. Chiyari's bats do not home and are unchanged.
165. **(2026-11-01) Shion Yorigami: new mechanic `Misfortune`.** Talismans in hexagons, 36 every 2 beats (`RingShape.Hexagon`; the spinner's stars too); one of hers that goes out of the screen comes back in the way it came, once (`MisfortuneAct`). Every 8 beats, from 8 beats into her section, everything there is, bullets and beams, turns dark blue (`TINT_DARK_BLUE`) and flies back the way it came (new op `Reverse`, shared by bullets and beams). For the 4 beats before, a red arrow on every second bullet of a ring shows the way (`reverseLevel`). Beams have no arrows.
