166. **(2026-11-01) Mononobe no Futo: new mechanic `Drift`.** Everything fired in her section that flies straight loses speed the nearer it gets to the wall ahead of it, evenly from its own speed where it starts to 0.3 of it at the wall, and keeps that (`BulletEmitter.SlowsToWall`, an exponential, a closed form of time, so they stay plain rings and the other bosses' mechanics still act on them; measured from where each bullet starts, not from Futo). When a pause starts she throws a boat at the player (`BoatAct`, `StageTexture.Boat`, baked): it flies at 1.5 times a bullet's speed, turns as it goes, hurts like a slider body, and every bullet it touches goes straight up or down, by the side of the boat it was on, at twice its speed, once per boat. Her own shots stay stars.
167. **(2026-11-01) Ran Yakumo: golden spirals (`Foxes`, no longer a placeholder).** When an attack begins 8 beams grow out of where she stands along golden spirals (`FoxAct`; the radius grows by the golden ratio every quarter turn), evenly round her, at 3 times a bullet's speed; where they start stays. Once all 8 have passed the farthest corner of the screen they go from the end they started at, as fast as they grew, and meanwhile a bullet that crosses one bounces off it, once. They hurt like a slider body. What is against a spiral is worked out from angle and distance, so the cost does not grow with the beams. She throws rings of 8 talismans every beat that start at 2x speed and slow down to 0.25x a playfield's length out (`SlowFrom`, `SlowTo`, `SlowOver`, the same exponential as Futo's). New `StageAct.DrawBeams`, so an act's beams need no list of their own.
168. **(2026-11-01) Eika Ebisu: new mechanic `Stacking`.** Rings of 20 ellipses every 2 beats (the spinner's stars too). Every 4 beats of her section every bullet on the screen gets as much speed again at right angles to the line between it and her: clockwise in every odd circle radius of distance, anticlockwise in every even one (`StackingAct.Pushes`). In her attacks bullets of different rings that touch stick together, up to 8 in a clump, and fly on as one, with all their momentum shared by all their mass, a bullet's mass being its area. A grid of the bullets of each step keeps it to neighbours. A bullet can stick from 300 ms after it was fired. Clumps do not stick to each other, and the pushes do not act on them.
169. **(2026-11-01) Two boss mods, with a count.** One Boss Selection, Two Bosses Selection, Always Double Boss and Always Double Boss Selection are gone. `Boss Selection` (BS, the default, 1 boss) and `Double Boss Selection` (DS, at least 2) have a slider for how many bosses the map has and the list of heads; what is not checked the combo colours fill, then the next in the roster. The slider is in the picker, as it needs the map, and goes up to `BulletPlanner.MostBosses`: how many fit when each has one attack and one pause (32 beats; a spinner holds one); Double twice that. The value of the setting is `count|ids`. Bosses take the sections in turn, one at a time, so with Boss Selection the longest section no longer has both of two bosses (that belonged to the colours choosing two, which nothing asks for any more). More bosses than sections: the sections are cut into even pieces (`cutForBosses`), spinners never. Double: every section has the next two in the list (`partnerOf`). `BossHud` with more than two bosses shows only those on the screen. A score set with one of the old mods loses that mod.
170. **(2026-11-01) Yumemi Okazaki: new mechanic `Possibility`.** She shoots nothing but flowers of beams: every 4 beats a five-petalled flower grows out of where she stands (`PossibilityAct`, a formula, with a gap between the petals: 15 % of each, so it can be slipped through), hurting like a slider body. In her attacks every tenth bullet of any ring has a beam for a tail, 4 beats long, flying along with it (`TailEvery`; the tails of `BulletField.Tails`, followed in 16 steps when ops act on the ring). In her pauses everything, bullets and beam slices, also what is fired meanwhile, is pulled straight away from where she is when the pause begins, 8 times as hard as Zanmu's pull (new ops `Outward` and `Coast`; the way is fixed when the pull starts), and the screen shakes. New `StageAct.Early`: runs before anything moves in a frame.
171. **(2026-11-01) Soga no Tojiko: new mechanic `Storm`.** The screen dims dark grey (`stormBackground`) with see-through thunderclouds along the top (`StormAct`). Every fourth bullet that goes out of the top falls back as an ellipse half its size at an eighth of Zanmu's pull. Every 2 beats a jagged bolt grows from the top to the bottom in 1 beat, see-through and harmless for 2 beats, solid for 4 (hurting like a slider body, bullets near it dim), and goes the way it came in 1 beat. Every 4 beats of an attack she shoots a bolt at where the player is, which stays that way (1 beat solid, no see-through time). She shoots nothing else. In a spinner the clouds are in the middle, and the ellipses and bolts go from there outwards.
172. **(2026-11-01) Wakasagihime: new mechanic `Tide`.** She shoots Marisa's stars, half as big and twice as often. In her attacks the screen dims ocean blue (`tideBackground`), bullets and beams turn blue (`WAKASAGI_TINT` 0.6, not the seaweed), and everything fired meanwhile swims a circle radius to each side of its way (`BulletEmitter.WaveSize`, a closed form; even bullets one way, odd the other). Every sixteenth bullet on the screen is a see-through bubble twice as big (`TideAct`): it does not hurt, drifts at half speed, and pops into a ring of 8 bullets after 8 beats, or at once when the player touches it. Every sixteenth bullet that goes out of the bottom grows a blade of dark green seaweed a quarter of the screen high, which sways, hurts like a slider body, and after 8 beats comes off the ground and falls upwards at Zanmu's pull.
173. **(2026-11-01) Daiyousei: new mechanic `Verdant`.** In her attacks the background dims dark warm green and every bullet on the screen turns green and glows (`verdantLevel`, `DAIYOUSEI_BLOOM` 0.7). What is fired meanwhile hurts like a slider body and goes round once (`BulletEmitter.WrapsOnce`, a closed form): out of one wall, in again on the same line from the opposite one, gone the second time out. Those bullets cannot be destroyed: what would take one (Takes of any act: Meiling's pull, Zanmu's ripple, Flandre's eyes...) gets a copy of it once and the bullet flies on (`borrowedBullets`). Not covered: ops (Cirno's freeze) still stop such a ring, and a ring with ops of another boss does not go round. Her own shots stay stars.
174. **(2026-11-01) Youmu Konpaku: slashes (`Slash`, no longer a placeholder).** In her attacks every sixteenth bullet that leaves the screen is remembered where it left (`SlashAct`). Every 4 beats of an attack the last two places are joined by a straight slash: see-through and harmless for 4 beats, solid and twice as thick for 2, hurting like a slider body, fading over 1. Where a slash turns solid it pokes a hole in the beams of sliders across it (Flandre's `IBeamBlasts`, spinner beams excepted), and the slices cut out fall as swaying pink petals, which are bullets. She throws rings of 16 straight beams every 2 beats, 2 circle radii long, flying straight out of her, all through her section, in a spinner too (the spinner's stars are gone); they hurt like a slider body and leave room between them. With fewer than two places there is no slash.
175. **(2026-11-01) Youmu: slashes run across the whole screen.** Only between the two remembered places → through them and on past the screen at both ends.
176. **(2026-11-01) Toyosatomimi no Miko: cones and beams (`Rays`, no longer a placeholder).** Always in the middle, no pauses (`RaysAct`). Every 8 beats the 20 busiest of the 120 3 degree directions from her light up as cones to past the edge of the screen; bullets on the screen in them, and what flies in for the next 6 beats, stop, glow and are pulled to her, arriving when the beams end. The cones brighten over their last 2 beats, and after 8 beats are bright still beams as wide as the cones for 4 beats, hurting like a slider body, with a black dim of the background (0.5) and the bullets (0.35); the beams fade over 1 beat while what was pulled in comes out as 5 evenly spread rings half a beat apart, 64 bullets a ring at most (the rest is swallowed, so that the rings can be dodged). A new set of cones lights up as the last one turns into beams. Every 4 beats 16 bullets twice the size, glowing, fly at her from outside the screen, the first from behind the player. No sets that would not fit into the section; no spinner stars.
177. **(2026-11-01) Kaguya Houraisan: the moon (`ImpossibleRequests`, no longer a placeholder).** Stands top centre, the edge of her magic circle 16 px below the top of the screen, no pauses (`MoonAct`). Dark blue background (0.8) and a giant glowing moon (a new baked picture, `BigMoon`, the 320 px one would blur; radius 0.62 of the screen's width, 20 % of the screen's height shows) comes down from the top over 2 beats, with the `yuyuko_tree` sound. Every 8th bullet that leaves the top falls straight down as a finish-sized star at bullet speed; its beam's slices live for 4 beats and hurt like a slider body. Her own: every 4 beats 9 rings (one followed by 8) of 20 half-size bullets, a quarter of a beat apart. Every ring fired in her sections, hers or an object's, turns round its centre as it flies, each the other way from the one before (new `BulletEmitter.CurlsTogether`, ricochet and homing rings excepted). She comes back 8 beats after the last boss section until the song ends (as Mokou does, `BossPeriod.Revival`; with both on the map the one first in the list comes back): slices live for 8 beats, every 4th bullet falls, the bullets of each 4 beats are drawn as ellipses, stars, talismans, then all three mixed (new `MixedShapes`), and every 20th is 3x as big. No spinner stars.
178. **(2026-11-01) Yorihime: she repeats the other bosses.** In a section of hers alone, on a map with other bosses, she repeats their mechanics: the boss before her in the list first and so on backwards, carried on from section to section, 16 beats each (`YORIHIME_REPEAT_BEATS`) after a gap of 4 (`YORIHIME_REPEAT_GAP_BEATS`). Each is planned as a hidden second boss of that piece (`BossPeriod.Borrowed`): it stands where she stands, is not drawn and is not in the HUD. Half power: half as many beams in a burst and half as many bullets in its own rings; what a mechanic does without bullets (a freeze, a turned screen) is at full strength, and so are bullets made by its act. Her pentagon talismans go on all the time. What she did before (bullets and beams back from the edge, 141) is now only for when there is nobody to repeat: she is the only boss of the map, she shares the section (no room for a third boss), or the section is too short for a piece.
179. **(2026-11-01) Boss descriptions: two parts, Basic Attack and Mechanics.** `BossDescriptions.texts` is now (basic, mechanics) for every built boss; the boss menu shows the name, then one line of each. Basic Attack is what she shoots herself (taken from the planner's `bossShape` and own-ring chain; a boss with nothing else of her own says "Spirals of stars"), Mechanics is everything else. A placeholder says "Spirals of stars" and "None yet". Also fixed: Kaguya shot nothing in a spinner (the planner's own-shots chain is skipped there, and her spinner stars are removed): her rings now run in a spinner too.
180. **(2026-11-01) Beams: no more infinite stretching.** Slices that teleported are separated. The look's `Portal` was only a count (1 = has been through a portal), so two slices that went through different portals, or came out of different walls of a bent portal, counted as one beam and were joined by a line that grew as they flew apart. `Portal` is now which teleport it was (act and exit wall, `PortalAct.Transfer`), and neighbours with different ones are cut (the search for where the beam changes finds the place). On top of that, for every boss, `LaserEmitter.cutStretches`: two neighbouring slices further apart than 1.5 times the way they would be if nothing had happened to them, plus 32 px and the beam's width (`BEAM_STRETCH_FACTOR`, `BEAM_STRETCH_SLACK`), are cut, and so is a point that is not a number. Drawing and hitting both go by this, so a stretch no longer hurts either. A slice that fell after going through a portal stays a fallen slice. Bench with a moving player, Dynamite 0:20 + 90 s, segments over 60 % of the screen in all frames: Junko + Okina 3474 → 0, Yuyuko + Okina 512 → 0. (The bench's player stood still before, which keeps the portals still: `BulletField.CursorProbe` moves it under the `long` option; `KEEP_STRETCHES=1` shows the old behaviour.)
181. **(2026-11-01) Yuyuko: falling beams do not interact with portals.** Once a slice has fallen from the top the portals leave it alone (`carry`).
182. **(2026-11-01) Yuyuko + Okina froze the game.** 150-430 ms a frame (3-6 fps), on every map, the only one of Junko / Yuyuko / Okina and their pairs to do so; very likely what "Okina often crashes" was, though no log shows a real crash (no ruleset exception in the last 50 runs, no crash or hang report for osu! since 10-12, and that one was an image upload that was fixed long ago). A beam with ops on it cannot use the portals' fast way, so every slice was followed from where it started in 24 ms steps, all through the attack, even after it had gone through a portal, 16 times more for every change of look, and a fallen beam has dozens of those. Now: nothing is followed further once it has gone through or fallen; steps are as long as half the time to the nearest wall (`wallGap`, up to 1 s); no search between two fallen slices (they have points at the ends of their pieces); a stage bullet, and a slice of a beam with ops, goes on from where it was last frame (`CarryMark`, for beams in `PortalBridge`). Moving player, 0:20 + 90 s, on Dynamite, Cyaegha [Avarice] and epimerization: about 5 ms a frame, p99 14-20, with a single frame of 100-150 ms somewhere in each run. Okina + Cirno is still 29 ms (2854 rings kept alive; see 22).
183. **(2026-11-01) Gameplay no longer runs in slow motion when frames are heavy.** The log of the first play of the day: no frame of the field over 16 ms in the log's terms, but 189 "gameplay stood" lines, 159 of them within 4 minutes on Demetori - Koyoi wa Hyouitsu na Egoist, about one a second, each about 620 ms behind the music; map time moved about 320 ms a second. osu! moves gameplay in even steps of 16.67 ms however long a step takes, so at about 50 ms a step gameplay ran at a third of the music's speed until it was over 500 ms behind and stood still, and `unstickClock` then jumped the music back, every second. Frames under 150 ms were never logged, so the log could not say whose they were. Now gameplay takes uneven steps while playing (`FrameStablePlayback` off, through reflection as osu! keeps it internal; a replay keeps the even steps): heavy moments cost frames, not speed, and the music does not jump. The ~4 s gaps in the same log are pauses. New log lines: `Danmakusu: bosses ...` once per map, and `Danmakusu: slow second at ...` for every second of map time averaging 12 ms a frame or more (`SLOW_SECOND_LOG_MS`), with its costliest steps.
184. **(2026-11-01) Chimata: her walls are talismans.** They were talismans in the code but drew as plain round bullets: stage bullets never set `Morph`, so a talisman was all "bullet under it" and no talisman. Every stage bullet and bead now has `Morph` 1 (her rings too, and anything else of that kind).
185. **(2026-11-01) Wriggle: fireflies are sharp.** Their radius had the 2x in it and `ScaleTo` then doubled it again: 4x, past what the picture of a bullet can show sharply. Now 2x, as the description always said.
186. **(2026-11-01) Yuuka: petals as big as those of Yuyuko's flowers** (0.0125 of the tree's height: 5.4 px on a full screen, was about 11), on the head and when they fly. The head and the stem's leaves keep their size; the petals stand round the edge of the head.
187. **(2026-11-01) Youmu: slashes go through bullets near the middle.** No more places where bullets left the screen (two on one wall gave a line along that wall): every four beats, of every sixteenth bullet on the screen, the two nearest to the middle, inside the middle 70 % of the screen (`YOUMU_INNER`), at least 2 circle radii apart (`YOUMU_APART_CIRCLES`).
188. **(2026-11-01) Futo: the boat is seen from above, longer and bigger.** New picture (a pointed wooden hull, planks, three benches), 10 circle radii long and 2.5 wide (was 4 by 4, a side view), bow first along its way instead of spinning. It hurts and pushes along its whole length.
189. **(2026-11-01) Shizuha: every 4th bullet (was every 2nd) and maple leaves.** New `BulletShape.Maple`, the leaf of the flag of Canada traced from the flag's own outline, for her shots, her spinner and the leaves that fall.
190. **(2026-11-01) Gengetsu: speed lines half as visible** (`GENGETSU_SPEED_LINE_ALPHA` 0.8 → 0.4).
191. **(2026-11-01) Miko: cones do not overlap.** No two cones of a set next to each other (at least one 3 degree step free between them), and none on a cone of the set before, if there are enough places (it is turning into beams right then).
192. **(2026-11-01) Boss HUD: names no longer lie over each other in a change.** With more than two bosses both sides show one name, the newest on that side: the first boss of a section on the left, the second on the right (was: whichever were on the screen, in list order, so a third one took the same place).
193. **(2026-11-01) Boss picker: the number can be typed, and the label shows how many are chosen.** A number box beside the label, as far as the map has room; "Bosses: 5 (up to 21 on this map), 3 chosen".
194. **(2026-11-01) Boss distribution: every boss gets an attack and a pause.** Only a piece that holds both counts: at least attack + pause long, and not a spinner, which is one attack (`holdsABoss`). Sections are cut until there are as many of those as bosses (they were only cut when there were fewer sections than bosses, so a boss could get just a short piece). Those pieces go round the bosses; a short piece or a spinner goes to the boss of the piece after it (`bossSlots`, used by the HP bar preview too, which had its own order). `MostBosses` counts only those pieces, so the slider's top is lower on maps that counted their spinners. In a section too short for a boss's own attack and pause (Gengetsu's 64 beats) the attack gives way, down to 4 beats (`BOSS_SHORTEST_ATTACK_BEATS`). New test `sectioncheck fit <maps>`: every boss count on every map.
195. **(2026-11-01) Ability sheets.** `Touhou bosses.xlsx` and `Touhou_Boss_Abilities.xlsx` written from the two-part descriptions: 73 built, and the 71 not built reimagined in Basic Attack / Mechanics form (the old ideas are in `Touhou_Boss_Abilities.xlsx.bak`).
196. **(2026-11-01) Bosses without pauses: one attack lasts as long as another boss's attack and pause together.** 16 → 32 beats (Hecatia's already were), so a turn of theirs is as long as anyone's; what happens once an attack (a jump of Mokou's, a rewind of Sagume's) comes half as often.
197. **(2026-11-01) The slow-motion fix (183) made heavy moments worse, fixed.** Log of 73 bosses with Double Boss Selection on Koyoi wa Hyouitsu na Egoist [Egoistic Flowers]: "gameplay stood" 189 → 1 as meant, but frames of 300-450 ms at 2:44-2:50 and 4:29-4:43, nearly all `absorb`, growing from second to second. A ring's scan took up to 40 steps of 10 ms: a long frame cost more than a short one and made the next longer (with even steps it never got past 2). And every ring asked every act, hundreds with 73 bosses, at every step. Now at most 4 steps a frame (`SCAN_MOST_STEPS`), and the acts are looked through once a frame (`frameTakers`). Bench with that map, the song and a moving player, time moving on as far as the frame took (`BENCH_AUDIO`, `BENCH_REALTIME`, `SCAN_LIKE_BEFORE=1` for the old scan; double bosses): worst frame at 2:47 343 → under 96 ms, p99 57 → 38, absorb 5.4 → 2.0 ms a frame. The bench also did not go by the song till now, so it planned other sections than the game.
198. **(2026-11-01) Boss selection: the order shows, and the description's pictures are a grid.** The order clicked already was the order in the map (`sectioncheck fit` now checks that too); every boss in the boss menu's panel has its number. The pictures fill a grid instead of one row, more than 4 names in the title become "N bosses", and the line about bosses being on the screen together is only there with Double Boss Selection (it said "both" for any two or more).
199. **(2026-11-01) Shion: her warning arrows are small, flash, and point at her.** Beside every second bullet, on the side towards her, instead of showing each bullet's own way back; half as big (`SHION_ARROW_SIZE` 0.9 → 0.5), flashing twice a beat (`SHION_FLASH_BEATS`). Still the four beats before everything turns round.
200. **(2026-11-01) Boss list and log.** The description's grid shows every chosen boss, numbered, with cells placed by hand (a fill flow cut the last ones off after rounding). The log line now says `Danmakusu: bosses (double|single) ...`.
201. **(2026-11-01) Boss switch: no short attack before a boss leaves.** `addBoss` starts no new attack when fewer than 8 beats are left in the section (`BOSS_LAST_ATTACK_BEATS`, new): the last attack runs on to the end of the section instead, unless a pause comes first. The first attack of a section is exempt.
202. **(2026-11-01) Mokou under her own ball.** The boss proxy above the field drew her over her impact ball. `CreateBallsProxy()` puts `stageBulletsUnder` and `stageBullets` back above her, on maps with a moon layer (`addLayer` in `DanmakusuPlayfield`).
203. **(2026-11-01) Two bosses share a circle only when they really stand on each other.** The orbit logic compared where the two would have stood, opposite the player, so Mokou and Kaguya (placed by their acts) and Chen in flight shared a circle half way between them. Now a boss an act or a throw places keeps her own circle (`placed` in `BulletField.Update`).
204. **(2026-11-01) Yorihime does what she repeats herself.** Her borrowed pieces used to be a separate boss who appeared on her own: now they carry Yorihime's rank, `latestBoss` and `BossCharacter` skip them, and acts of a borrowed boss move her (`stageBossPosition`, `bossAtCentre`). In a shared section she repeats the boss beside her for the whole section, starting 4 beats later (`repeatBoss`). In a section of her own: as before, the last boss first, 16 beats each after a gap of 4.
205. **(2026-11-01) Kaguya comes back like Mokou, and both together.** The revival only looked for `Rebirth`. Now every immortal on the map comes back (Mokou, Kaguya: a section of two, counts halved). The last section stops 8 + 32 beats before the end to leave room (`REVIVAL_ROOM_BEATS`, new); a spinner there cannot be cut short, so they take it over (`stopFor`, on epimerization they had no revival at all, as the map ends in one). `sectioncheck periods` (new) shows it: all 23 example maps now have one.
206. **(2026-11-01) Chimata.** Knives half as big (`CHIMATA_KNIFE_SIZE` 0.5, new) with blue handles (`knifeBlueTexture`), talismans turn into knives at twice the distance (`CHIMATA_NEAR_CIRCLES` 2 → 4), the talismans she puts round the player stay talismans (`addTalisman(..., turnsIntoKnife)`). Half as dense: `CHIMATA_WALL_SPACING` 2 → 4 and `CHIMATA_RING_BULLETS` 20 → 10 (the alternative was walls half as often).
207. **(2026-11-01) Wakasagihime.** Seaweed twice as often (`WAKASAGI_EVERY` 16 → 8), narrowing to a sharp tip, and thinner there to touch too. Bubbles half as often (`WAKASAGI_BUBBLE_EVERY` 32, new: they shared the 16), hollow (rims and a highlight, `WAKASAGI_BUBBLE_ALPHA` gone), popping 16 beats after they appear on their own timers (`WAKASAGI_BUBBLE_BEATS` 8 → 16), flashing red for the last 4 (`WAKASAGI_BUBBLE_WARN_BEATS`). Background lighter blue, less dim (`WAKASAGI_BACKGROUND_ALPHA` 0.55 → 0.32), breathing in two slow waves, with six pale patches of light drifting across.
208. **(2026-11-01) Seiran: one ring fewer.** Shots in a row 4 → 3. The tuning id is new (`seiran_shots` → `seiran_volley`) so a saved 4 does not stay; a saved value of the old id is ignored.
209. **(2026-11-01) Tojiko.** Bolts are a hard zigzag, every point thrown to the other side (`TOJIKO_BOLT_JAG` 0.7 → 1.5). Four smaller branches at sharp angles, narrowing to a point, growing out as the bolt reaches them; they hurt too (`TOJIKO_BRANCHES` and friends, new). Clouds: dark billows with lit tops that swell (`TOJIKO_CLOUD_ALPHA` 0.45 → 0.95), flashing white for a beat when a bolt turns solid.
210. **(2026-11-01) Yuuka.** Sunflowers flash for a beat before they come out (`YUUKA_WARN_BEATS`, new). Petals half as fast (`YUUKA_PETAL_SPEED` 0.5, new). Bodies straighter (`YUUKA_LEAN` 0.14 → 0.07), more leaves (`YUUKA_LEAVES` 2 → 5), twice the petals on the heads (`YUUKA_PETALS` 8 → 16).
211. **(2026-11-01) Sukuna: scaling 0.5-1.5×.** `SUKUNA_MIN_SIZE` 0 → 0.5, `SUKUNA_MAX_SIZE` 3 → 1.5.
212. **(2026-11-01) Eiki.** Judgement circle radius doubled (`EIKI_RADIUS_CIRCLES` 4 → 8), its edge is two magic circles turning opposite ways.
213. **(2026-11-01) Yumemi.** Flowers twice as often (`YUMEMI_FLOWER_EVERY_BEATS` 4 → 2), sharp at both ends of every petal, and thinner there to touch too. A big flash over the 2 beats before each pause (`YUMEMI_WARN_BEATS`, new), and a big grey wave out from where she stands as the pause starts (`YUMEMI_WAVE_*`, new).
214. **(2026-11-01) Satori.** Eye twice as big (`SATORI_EYE_SIZE` 1.6 → 3.2), further out so its edge stays as far from the player (`SATORI_EYE_DISTANCE` 2.2 → 3).
215. **(2026-11-01) Tenshi: no change.** The pieces of a spread bullet are stage bullets, which never reach `Takes`, so a spread bullet already could not spread again.
216. **(2026-11-01) Youmu.** Every 2 beats instead of 4 (`YOUMU_SLASH_EVERY_BEATS`), and each time 9 slashes instead of 1: the closest pair plus 8 more, through next-nearest pairs (`YOUMU_MORE_SLASHES`, new). The alternative reading was 8 more beams in her rings.
217. **(2026-11-01) Chiyari: pools are not circles.** A baked `BloodPool` texture: uneven outline, bright rim, two white vampire's fangs, turning slowly; more visible (`CHIYARI_POOL_ALPHA` 1, new).
218. **(2026-11-01) What crawls fades.** A bullet at under 0.03 px/ms (`VERY_SLOW_SPEED`) fades over 500 ms, 32 beats after it was fired, or when the boss who slowed it leaves if that is later (`VERY_SLOW_BEATS`, `VERY_SLOW_FADE_MS`). Rings slowed by Futo or Ran through `fadeWhenCrawling` and `BulletEmitter.FadesFrom`, stage bullets in `AddBullet`.
219. **(2026-11-01) Koakuma: bats like Byakuren's beams.** The homing was already the same code; homing bats now also fly at her beams' speed (`stageBulletSpeed * BYAKUREN_TRAIL_SPEED`).
220. **(2026-11-01) Ebisu.** A bullet joining a clump is pushed out of those already there (`beside`, up to 8 rounds), and a clump is never slower than the fastest bullet that joined it (`Clump.Fastest`).
221. **(2026-11-01) Utsuho's black hole leaves Yuyuko's petals alone.** `TrajectoryOp.SkipPetals` on its pull, `HoleAct.Takes` skips them, and with a hole on the map the petals of the flowers are not traced by the ops at all (`TreeAct.holeOnMap`: on such a map they also ignore other bosses' ops; maps without a hole are as before).
222. **(2026-11-01) Kaguya.** The moon is Hecatia's picture (`StageTexture.Moon`, `BigMoon` gone), radius up (`KAGUYA_MOON_SIZE` 0.62 → 0.9) with the same height showing, coming slowly down over 32 beats (`KAGUYA_MOON_BEATS` 2 → 32; the sky keeps its 2, `KAGUYA_MOON_LEAVE_BEATS`). Stars fall 3° left and right of straight down by turns (`KAGUYA_FALL_DEGREES`), their beams narrow to a point and hurt only as wide as they are at that place. The picture is 320 px, so it is soft at this size. (Shared circle: 203. Coming back: 205.)
223. **(2026-11-01) Seija: where you are.** After every turn of the screen, start and end of an attack, a big smooth red circle closes in on the player from 6 circle radii over 2 beats (`InversionAct`, `SEIJA_CIRCLE_*`, new).
224. **(2026-11-01) Ran.** Golden Ratio grows twice as fast (`RAN_BEAM_SPEED` 3 → 6).
225. **(2026-11-01) Smaller ones.** Futo: what the boat touches goes off to the boat's sides, not up or down. Aki: leaves turn slowly and smoothly (`SHIZUHA_TURN`), fallen bodies spin each at a speed of their own, and they start falling at different times (`SHIZUHA_RISE_SPREAD_BEATS`). Hisami: circle area doubled (`HISAMI_REACH_CIRCLES` 2 → 2.83), more visible (`HISAMI_RING_ALPHA` 0.4 → 0.9), stays longer (`HISAMI_RING_BEATS` 4, new). Sakuya: the time-stop preview half as visible, knives twice as far from the player (`SAKUYA_RELEASE_DISTANCE_FACTOR` 3.2 → 6.4).
226. **(2026-11-01) Boss difficulty: 1 to 5 stars.** New `Bosses/BossDifficulty.cs`, one line per boss, set by hand from what they do and then moved where `sectioncheck field ... singles` showed far more on the screen than the text suggested (Cirno 1 → 3, Sanae 2 → 3, Ringo, Yukari, Futo, Shion, Tenshi 3 → 4, Raiko, Seiran 1 → 2). Placeholders have 1. The colour goes with the rating: 1 green, 3 yellow, 5 red, between those for 2 and 4 (`ColourOf`). `DifficultyStars` is a row of five stars with the rest dark: in the boss list under the name on its left edge (tiles 44 → 52 px high), in the description a `Difficulty:` line under each boss's name.
227. **(2026-11-01) The description shows big pictures.** The sprites were 172×193 (the game's boss needs no more, and keeps them). New `Resources/Bosses/Portraits/<id>.webp`, 144 of them, up to 1024 px, 12.9 MB in all (dll 33 → 46 MB), made by the new `..\Tools\Portraits` from `old builds and assets\Touhou Characters`. 26 of those pictures are on a solid background (one, Rika, on a fake see-through checkerboard): 19 are cut along the enlarged shape of their sprite, 7 by colour, as the sprite lost thin parts of them or has holes it should not have (`cut_by_colour`). `BossInfoPanel` reads them off the update thread and only keeps what is still wanted (`showPortrait`); `*.webp` is embedded in the csproj.
228. **(2026-11-01) Boss distribution: what is too short goes on with the section round it.** A section shorter than an attack and a pause (32 beats), spinner, break or kiai, has no boss of its own any more: it is a part of the section before it, or at the start of the map of the one after it (`continued`, at the end of `Sections`, so the slider, the HP bar and the game agree), and the boss goes on through it without a new warning. One further than 32 beats from any other stays as it is and has the boss before it (`bossSlots`: it was the boss after it). A spinner of 32 beats or more can have a boss of its own (`holdsABoss` no longer looks at the kind; a spinner is still not cut, a build-up takes no turn, and `PausesWithTail` counts the end of such a spinner). The stars of a spinner inside a section of another kind are now the boss's own bullets too (the check for `Spinner` is gone), also for spinners inside a kiai section, which stayed stars.
229. **(2026-11-01) Boss distribution: attacks first, and nothing shorter than the default.** In a section too short for a boss's own attack and pause the attack used to give way, down to 4 beats (Gengetsu and Utsuho had 4-beat attacks on every map at the most bosses). Now the pause gives way, down to the default 16 beats, and only then the attack, down to the default 16 beats; below 32 beats the attack is whole and the pause has what is left (a piece of Yorihime's: a whole attack and no pause). Attacks are always whole: when no whole attack fits after this one what is left goes to this one, so a section ends in a whole pause, or in that attack when a whole pause does not fit. `BOSS_SHORTEST_ATTACK_BEATS` and `BOSS_LAST_ATTACK_BEATS` are gone (the last one replaces the 8 beats of 201). New `sectioncheck lengths <maps>`. With as many bosses as each of the 24 maps has room for, also with Gengetsu and Utsuho among them: no attack and no pause under 16 beats (before: 1 to 5 short attacks a map, down to 4 beats). The slider's top went up a little, as the short sections now add to the long ones (Dynamite 21 → 25).
230. **(2026-11-01) Yuuka: sunflowers sway left and right.** The head goes 0.6 circle radii to each side (`YUUKA_SWAY_CIRCLES`), less further down the stem, once in 8 beats (`YUUKA_SWAY_BEATS`), each flower in a time of its own. Its head hurts where it is drawn.
231. **(2026-11-01) Hisami Yomotsu: the circle fades in, is clear in the middle, and has twice the radius.** `HISAMI_REACH_CIRCLES` 2.83 → 5.66, so four times as much is recorded; the fill in the middle is gone; it fades in over a beat (`HISAMI_RING_FADE_BEATS`, new) and the line is as thick as it was. Her stars 3 → 4.
232. **(2026-11-01) Byakuren: the red arrows move.** The arrow of a star outside the screen stays at the wall nearest to the star and points at the wall, and now grows from 2 to 4 plain bullets as the star comes nearer (`BYAKUREN_ARROW_NEAR_SIZE`, over `BYAKUREN_ARROW_FAR` of the screen's height), beats against the wall twice a beat (`BYAKUREN_ARROW_BEATS`) and has a red glow that beats with it.
233. **(2026-11-01) Aki: twice as many leaves on the floor, and they fade in.** A falling leaf fades out as it lands, and two fade in on the floor, each in its place (`SHIZUHA_FLOOR_LEAVES`, `SHIZUHA_FLOOR_FADE_MS` 500, new `StageBullet.AppearOver`); no more sliding to the pile (`SHIZUHA_SLIDE_MS` gone). They cannot hit until they are there, and each starts to fall upwards at a time of its own.
234. **(2026-11-01) Ebisu: bodies touch.** Bullets merge when their bodies touch, by their shapes (`BulletCollision.Reach`: an ellipse reaches its whole length along itself and 0.65 of it across), not when the circles of their hitboxes do, and a bullet that joins lies with its body against the body of the nearest one, and on none of the others (`beside`). They keep the way they faced (`KeepFace`), so the bodies stay against each other whichever way the clump flies. A shape with no outline of its own (knives, talismans) counts as a circle.
235. **(2026-11-01) What leaves the screen is gone.** The fast bullets from the edges: nothing removed a bullet that had left the screen until its lifetime was up, so what steers bullets brought it back. Above all the falling ellipses of the hit objects (on 62 of 73 bosses): pulled towards the player from off the screen they came back about 3.7 times as fast as a plain bullet; also Eiki's circle, Shion's turn and Utsuho's pull, bullets and beams. Now a bullet of a ring that is out of the screen altogether (`leftForGood`, by the radius it is drawn with) is taken away for good, and so is a stage bullet. Not gone: what homes (Byakuren, Koakuma, what Nazrin pulls: a trail), what goes round by itself (Daiyousei `WrapsOnce`, Zanmu `WrapHeight`), what bounces at the wall, the trains and Sakuya's knives, what started outside. A slice of a beam is gone when it was out of the screen at the first op that could bring it back (`LaserEmitter.leftTheScreen`: until then it flew straight, so this is cheap, and a function of time alone, as a slice's place is; not with Okina's portals, not a straight beam; tracing every slice through every op tripled the frame time of Eiki + Reisen). What is taken at a wall has been taken by then, so portals and what comes back work as they did: compared by `ACT_PROBE` over 30 bosses, old build against new, Okina, Yukari, Daiyousei, Seiga, Shion's talismans, Tenshi, Reimu, Miko, Meiling, Hecatia, Ichirin, Junko, Sagume and Doremy are unchanged or within a few bullets; Hisami records 9 % fewer, Nazrin 12 %, Utsuho takes in 17 %, Patchouli's fire 27 %, all of it what they reached outside the screen. Bullets that came in from outside: Circle with ops 4515 → 307, falling ellipses 840 → 138.
236. **(2026-11-01) Yuyuko's and Lily White's flowers after 235.** Both counted what was out of the screen at the start of an attack, which is no longer there: Yuyuko lost 64 % of her petals, Lily White 28 %. A bullet that goes out in a pause now counts for the next attack at once (`Sow`). Lily White ends up with more flowers than before (832 → 1006); Yuyuko with about 60 % of her petals (2495 → 1515), as what left long before an attack does not count.
237. **(2026-11-01) Tojiko.** Bolts from the sky half as often (`TOJIKO_BOLT_EVERY_BEATS` 2 → 4), and the branches no longer hurt: too aggressive. Clouds flash only where a bolt starts, not all of them (hers start from her: no flash). Their edge is half as wide (`StageTexture.Cloud`).
238. **(2026-11-01) Chiyari.** Her bats, which do not home, half as fast (`CHIYARI_BAT_SPEED` 0.5). Koakuma's are not touched. A pool outside the screen catches nothing: it sent bullets at the player from out of the screen.
239. **(2026-11-01) Yuuka and Wakasagihime.** Sunflowers twice as often (`YUUKA_EVERY` 8 → 4). Seaweed twice as big: half the screen high (`WAKASAGI_BLADE_HEIGHT` 0.25 → 0.5), twice as wide (`WAKASAGI_BLADE_WIDTH` 2, new).
240. **(2026-11-01) Sharp bullets from 1.5, with no limit.** `SHARP_BULLET_SIZE` 3.5 → 1.5, `SHARP_BULLETS_MAX` gone: the layer takes as many as there are (`StageLayer.Grows`). At 2 with no limit Clownpiece once choked the game: each is five sprites.
241. **(2026-11-01) No bullet smaller than 0.2 of a plain one.** `BULLET_SMALLEST`: at the end of the plan (hitbox with it), for stage bullets, and where Sukuna and the ops scale what is drawn.
242. **(2026-11-01) Smaller ones.** Miko: the cones before they brighten half as visible (`MIKO_CONE_ALPHA` 0.35 → 0.175). What crawls fades at 0.1 of a plain bullet's speed or slower (`VERY_SLOW_SHARE`, was 0.03 px/ms). Seiga: every second bullet comes back, by index (`KAKU_RETURN_EVERY` 2). Yukari: three bullets of every eight freeze, not four (`YUKARI_FREEZE_OF_EIGHT`, `TrajectoryOp.OfEight`). Boss list: a Max button left of the number.
243. **(2026-11-01) Kaguya.** Her rings are plain size (`KAGUYA_RING_SIZE` 0.5 gone). The dark blue goes darker to the bottom. The moon is `moon_big.webp`, 1280 px: the picture sent is the same 320 px one the game has, enlarged and sharpened; put a bigger picture at that path, or tell Claude where it is.
244. **(2026-11-01) Yumemi: petals are pointed arches.** The outline was a cosine, round at the top, and the sharp ends of 213 were the ends of the beam. Now two arcs of a circle from the gaps meet at a sharp tip (`radiusAt`, which hitting uses too), with a point right on the tip, and each side thickest in its middle.
245. **(2026-11-01) Reimu: a ring of five orbs.** Every throw is five yin-yang orbs in a ring, the first at the player (`REIMU_ORB_RING`), with one throw sound. Her stars 3 → 4.
246. **(2026-11-01) Cirno: every second bullet freezes.** `EveryOther` on her freeze and on what takes the frozen ones away, and only those burst (`BurstsEverySecond`). Stars and homing arrowheads go by the clock of their ring, which cannot stop for half of it: of those every second ring freezes (`OrEverySecondRing`). Beams all freeze, as before.
247. **(2026-11-01) Daiyousei: green bullets a quarter bigger.** What is fired in her attacks, hitbox too (`DAIYOUSEI_SIZE` 1.25), and everything else that turns green with her, as it is drawn.
248. **(2026-11-01) Reisen: her preview twice as see-through.** `REISEN_PREVIEW_VISIBLE` 0.45 → 0.225. Sakuya's went by hers: it has its own now, at the same 0.225 it was (`SAKUYA_PREVIEW_VISIBLE`).
249. **(2026-11-01) Tojiko: branches hurt again, bends are gentler.** The branches hurt as they did before 237, thinner towards their tips. The bolts stay a zigzag, with half the swing (`TOJIKO_BOLT_JAG` 1.5 → 0.75): "too aggressive" was about the bends. So the bolts are every 2 beats again as well (`TOJIKO_BOLT_EVERY_BEATS` 4 → 2, undoing 237). A bolt swells to 1.5 times as thick as it turns solid (`TOJIKO_SOLID_WIDTH`, over a quarter of a beat), its branches with it, narrowing to a tip; what hurts is as thick as what is drawn.
250. **(2026-11-01) Chimata's own rings and Tojiko's zigzags.** The rings of talismans round the player are half as fast (`CHIMATA_RING_SPEED` 0.5, new) and half as often (`CHIMATA_RING_EVERY_BEATS` 2 → 4; in a spinner 1 → 2). Her walls and knives are as they were. Every stretch of a bolt's zigzag has a length of its own, from 0.35 to 1.65 times the average (`TOJIKO_SHORTEST_ZIG`, `zigzagPlaces`, by the number of the bolt, so replays are the same), and a short stretch is thrown less, so the bends stay as gentle; branches too.
251. **(2026-11-01) Sounds, and four smaller ones.** New samples: `tojiko_thunder` (the first 4 s of `thunder.mp3`, faded out: it is 14 s of rumble and a bolt turns solid every 2 beats) as every bolt turns solid; `yumemi_bomb` (`cinematic bomb.wav`, its first 3.5 s) as her pause pushes everything away; `touhou_strike` (2.3 s of `Touhou-SFX`, from where the sound at 17 s starts) when Ran's beams start, at every throw of Byakuren's stars and Sekibanki's heads, and as Youmu's slashes turn solid (once for all nine). Yukari: every ring of her attacks is two, not three. Meiling: three rings at an impact (`MEILING_IMPACT_RINGS` 20 → 3). Chimata: knives from eight circle radii (`CHIMATA_NEAR_CIRCLES` 4 → 8). Kaguya: the moon comes down in 16 beats (`KAGUYA_MOON_BEATS` 32 → 16).
252. **(2026-11-01) Meiling, Gengetsu, Tojiko, short spinners.** Meiling: five rings at an impact (`MEILING_IMPACT_RINGS` 3 → 5). Gengetsu: attacks twice as long (`GENGETSU_ATTACK_FACTOR` 2 → 4: 128 beats, in a section with room for them). Tojiko: half as many bends (`TOJIKO_BOLT_POINTS` 14 → 7), the stretches from 0.1 to 3.7 before they are fitted to the bolt, most of them short (`TOJIKO_SHORTEST_ZIG`, `TOJIKO_LONGEST_ZIG`); a point is thrown as far as the shorter stretch beside it allows, so the bends stay as gentle. Seven branches instead of four, from anywhere along the bolt, and two smaller ones off each (`TOJIKO_SUB_*`); they hurt as far as they are thick. Bullets are dimmed along the whole bolt (`TOJIKO_DIM_EVERY` 3 → 1: there are half as many points). A spinner too short for an attack and a pause is prolonged to exactly that when there is room before the next section or the end of the map, starting earlier if it must (`continued`): it is then a section of its own, of the kind Break, as the spinner shoots nothing after it ends. Without room it goes on with the section before it, as in 228.
253. **(2026-11-01) Short breaks, Tojiko's rain, strikes and clouds, Eiki, Byakuren.** Breaks are prolonged like the spinners of 252 (any break that is a section at all: at least `BOSS_MIN_BREAK_BEATS`). Tojiko's rain: every bullet that leaves through the top falls back (`TOJIKO_EVERY` 4 → 1: twice as frequent, then twice more), twice as fast (`TOJIKO_FALL` 8 → 2: four times the pull is twice the speed at any height), twice as bright (`TOJIKO_RAIN_BRIGHT` 2: a `Shade` below 0) and not dimmed by bolts or beams (`Undimmed`: dimmed it would be darker beside a bolt than it was before). A bolt that turns solid throws five bullets back the way it came from where it ends or leaves the playfield, over 120 degrees, half a plain bullet's size, in the new `TINT_LIGHTNING`, never dim (`TOJIKO_IMPACT_*`, `throwBack`). Her clouds are over her lightning and rain and under every other bullet: the renderer draws them between (`CloudSprite`, `SetClouds`, `UnderClouds`), they left `stageBack`; the beams of every boss are drawn before them, so a beam of another boss passes under them too. Stars 3 → 4. Eiki: judgement circle a quarter smaller (`EIKI_RADIUS_CIRCLES` 8 → 6). Byakuren: `touhou_strike` is heard at most once in 8 beats (`BYAKUREN_SOUND_EVERY_BEATS`; her remembered spots can throw every 2).
254. **(2026-11-01) Short sections, Yukari, Gengetsu, Zanmu, Seiran, Eiki, Yuyuko, Kaguya.** Sections: a short break (or spinner) with no free room round it went on with the section before it, as 252 and 253 left it (Dynamite [DARKNESS]: 24 beats at 1:59, between two kiai sections). Now it takes the time from the section after it, or else the one before it, if that still holds an attack and a pause, and takes in whole what is too short for a boss on the way (`prolonged`); a spinner gives nothing, as it is not cut. Yukari: a train also when every attack begins (`addTrain`). Gengetsu: no pauses (attacks of 128 beats follow each other); stars 4 → 5. Zanmu: stopped bullets divide into two (`ZANMU_SPLIT_PIECES` 3 → 2). Seiran: bullets as big as a plain bullet (`SEIRAN_SIZE_FACTOR` 0.25 of a star, 0.375 of a plain bullet → 1 plain bullet); stars 2 → 3. Eiki: nothing is sent at the middle when the attack begins: what enters the circle turns to its middle at half speed there, and has its speed back when the circle has faded (`TrajectoryOp.Radius`, `RestoreAt`, `entersCircle`). What is inside when the attack begins goes at once; what leaves the circle again stays slow until then. Each circle takes a thing once, and a later circle can take it again (they used to take it once ever). Yuyuko: beams no longer fall from the top: that is Kaguya's now, in all her sections and her comeback (`YUYUKO_BEAM_PIECE_*` → `KAGUYA_BEAM_PIECE_*`, same speed as Yuyuko's petals, same purple pieces); Yuyuko still removes the turning beams of others in her sections. `sectioncheck breaks` is new, and `fit` and `lengths` no longer use Gengetsu, who has no pause to fit.
255. **(2026-11-01) Every short section, Tojiko, Sakuya.** Any section too short for an attack and a pause is prolonged now, as 254 did for breaks and spinners: kiai sections and the ones made from the song too, keeping their kind (a spinner still becomes a break). A build-up is left alone: it leads into the section after it and has that boss. Tojiko: fewer branches (`TOJIKO_BRANCHES` 7 → 5, `TOJIKO_SUB_BRANCHES` 2 → 1: 21 in all → 10; it was 4 before 252) on either side at random. Zigzags are made by one `zigzag`, for bolts, branches and smaller branches alike: every stretch leans 20 degrees off the line, however long it is, give or take a fifth (`TOJIKO_ZIG_DEGREES`, `TOJIKO_ZIG_SPREAD`, `TOJIKO_BOLT_JAG` is gone). The lengths stay as random as they were. It used to be a fixed throw of a point, so a short stretch bent at about 45 degrees and a long one at about 8. Sakuya: her preview half as see-through in the sense of 248 (`SAKUYA_PREVIEW_VISIBLE` 0.225 → 0.45). `sectioncheck breaks` also lists what is still short.
256. **(2026-11-01) 255 corrected.** Tojiko: the angles are not to be equal, only less scattered than they were: a stretch leans 10 to 29 degrees at random (`TOJIKO_ZIG_SPREAD` 0.2 → 0.5; before 255 it was about 8 to 45). Sakuya: "transparency halved" meant twice as transparent, so 255 went the wrong way: `SAKUYA_PREVIEW_VISIBLE` 0.45 → 0.1125 (it was 0.225 before 255).
257. **(2026-11-01) No exceptions for short sections; Tenshi nerfed.** 255 left build-ups out: on Between Life and Death [Requiem] the one short section left was the 8-beat build-up at 0:30, a sliver on the health bar. Now every short section goes through `prolonged`; a build-up that is prolonged is a break with a boss of its own, and one with no room stays as it was and leads into the section after it. Tenshi: asked again that what has multiplied cannot multiply again. Measured on that map with Tenshi + Wriggle and alone, 200 s: 7804 bullets taken, none twice, exactly 3 pieces each, so that was already so (see 215), and the fallback asked for applies: `TENSHI_EVERY` 8 → 16. While a shockwave is on the screen every bullet that leaves comes back, as before, and that is nearly all of it: at 16, 215 bullets taken by the rule and 7999 by waves on that map, 392 and 16604 on Dynamite [DARKNESS], so this nerf takes about 3 % off. Stars stay at 4. `sectioncheck breaks` also lists every section and takes `-` for no song.
258. **(2026-11-01) Tenshi: shockwaves multiply every fourth bullet.** While a wave is on the screen, every fourth bullet that leaves through a wall it has reached comes back as three, not every bullet (`TENSHI_WAVE_EVERY` 4, counted on its own, `waveCrossings`). Stars 4 → 3.
259. **(2026-11-02) Mima: half moon (`HalfMoon`, no longer a placeholder); Tojiko like real lightning; Chimata; Tenshi.** Mima: no pauses. Every 16 beats of her section (`MIMA_EVERY_BEATS`) a crescent moon is placed on the player (`HalfMoonAct`): the middle of its body on them, its back away from the middle of the playfield; outer radius 80 % of the playfield's height (`MIMA_RADIUS`), the hollow a circle of 0.825 of that set 0.325 towards the middle (`MIMA_HOLLOW*`, from the shape the user sent: tips about 131 degrees round from the back). It grows from one tip to the other over 4 beats, see-through (`MIMA_GHOST_ALPHA` 0.35), while the screen shakes (`MIMA_SHAKE`), with `mima_moon` (the user's Moon Basic.wav); then lights up the same way round over 1 beat (`mima_moon_active`, Moon Active.wav) and is solid until 8 beats after it stopped growing: it hurts like a slider body where it has lit up, the bullets near it are dim (`MIMA_DIM`). While it is all lit up everything that flies into it bounces off it once: a new op, `TrajectoryOpKind.Crescent`, so ring bullets, the bullets of the stages and the slices of every beam (slider beams and the big ones) all bounce, the way Eiki's circle takes them; what is inside it already flies through; `SkipTrains` leaves Yukari's train alone (Komachi's boat and Ichirin's hands are not bullets). When it goes, `yuyuko_petals` plays and 24 white stars with 2-beat tails fly off its edges, half outwards from the outer edge and half into the hollow (`DeriveKind.MoonEdges`, `MIMA_EDGE_STARS`, `MIMA_TAIL_BEATS`). Every second moon grows the other way round. Drawn as a beam whose width follows the crescent, white with bloom (`MIMA_BLOOM`), so it is sharp at any size. Her own bullets stay the stars every boss has, so her spinner keeps its stars. Stars 3. Benchmark (Dynamite [DARKNESS], 40 s): Mima alone 2.8 ms a frame (p99 7.0), with Eiki 7.1 (12.3), with Reisen 6.3 (18.8), with Yukari 6.1 (11.8): every beam that an op touches is traced slice by slice, as with Eiki. Tojiko: thunder for every second bolt only; the bolt is white with a blue edge and glows (`TOJIKO_BLOOM`), every stretch broken twice over at small kinks (`TOJIKO_JAG_LEVELS` 2, `TOJIKO_KINK` 0.18), branches one level of kinks, a third of the bolt's thickness and fainter (`TOJIKO_BRANCH_WIDTH` 0.6 → 0.35, `TOJIKO_BRANCH_ALPHA` 0.6), leaving it at 24 to 48 degrees, anywhere but near its end; still 5 branches with one smaller each, fewer than the picture; dims every 4th point (`TOJIKO_DIM_EVERY` 1 → 4: there are four times as many). Chimata: only the talismans of her walls turn into knives (the ring bullets of everyone no longer do), at half the distance (`CHIMATA_NEAR_CIRCLES` 8 → 4), and the walls have no gaps (`CHIMATA_WALL_SPACING` 4 → 2, as before 206). Tenshi: every eighth bullet at the bottom again (`TENSHI_EVERY` 16 → 8), every fourth in a wave as in 258.
260. **(2026-11-02) Mima's moon: plain white, smaller, flashes.** No glow (`MIMA_BLOOM` 0.8 is gone). It was also drawn with the edge and the core every beam has, and a white beam's core is near black (`WHITE_BULLET_CENTRE_COLOUR`): a dark stripe ran along it. Now it is white all through with one thin grey outline, 1.5 px (`MIMA_OUTLINE`): two beams of the new kind `LaserInstance.Solid`, drawn in their own colour only, the grey one that much wider under the white one. A quarter smaller: `MIMA_RADIUS` 0.8 → 0.6 of the playfield's height. Where something bounces off it a white flash shows for 200 ms (`MIMA_FLASH_MS`, `MIMA_FLASH_SIZE` 1.5 circle radii): the trace notes every bounce it works out in `TrajectoryOp.Bounces` (by 25 ms and 8 px, so what is traced again counts once), and the act draws them.
261. **(2026-11-02) Mima's moon: sharp, narrow tips.** It was drawn as wide as the body is along a line from its middle, which near the tips is far wider than the crescent (the hollow's edge meets that line slanting), so the tips were blunt. Now its middle line is where a point is as far from the outer edge as from the hollow's (`lineAt`, by halving), drawn that wide: both tips come to a fine point; the grey outline thins out towards them too.
262. **(2026-11-02) Mima's moon flashes, then fades; smaller stars.** In its last beat the whole moon flashes: a white glow that swells and dies (`MIMA_FLASH_BEATS` 1, `MIMA_FLASH_BLOOM` 1, the bloom of its white beam). When its stars fly off it fades out over a beat (`MIMA_FADE_BEATS`) instead of vanishing; it stops hurting and bouncing things when the stars go, as before. The stars are half as big (`MIMA_STAR_SIZE` 0.5 of a finish's star), tails too.
263. **(2026-11-02) Mima's moon: one even shape.** It was a chain of beam pieces that overlap where they join: see-through (while it grows, and the light of the flash) the overlaps added up into streaks across it. Now it is a flat shape of pieces side by side (`MeshQuad`, `BulletRenderer.SetMeshes`, drawn over the beams and under the bullets): the white body between its outer edge and its hollow's, exactly the crescent, and the thin grey outline kept as strips just outside both edges, thinning out at the tips. `LaserInstance.Solid` (262) is gone again. The flash before it goes is a soft light beyond the outline that fades out to nothing: half as visible as the bloom it had and reaching half as far (`MIMA_FLASH_BLOOM` 1 → `MIMA_FLASH_ALPHA` 0.3, about half of what the three layers of a beam's bloom add up to at its edge, `MIMA_FLASH_REACH` 0.375 of its half thickness, the bloom reached 0.75).
264. **(2026-11-02) Mima's moon: no dim.** The bullets near a solid moon are no longer dimmed (`MIMA_DIM` 0.5 and `MIMA_DIM_EVERY` are gone, and with them the moon's middle line, `lineAt`, which only the dim used).
265. **(2026-11-02) Mima: more stars, moons that always finish, tails, glow, louder.** The stars of a moon that goes are full size again (`MIMA_STAR_SIZE` 0.5 → 1) and four times as many (`MIMA_EDGE_STARS` 24 → 96): two rings of 48, one along each edge (`BulletEmitter.Piece`: 0 the outer edge, flying out, 1 the hollow's, flying into it), as a ring holds at most 64. A moon always starts, every 16 beats while her section lasts, and goes through to its end also when the section is over (before, a moon that did not fit before the end did not come at all). Her own stars, her spinner's too, have tails of beams 4 beats long that narrow to a point (`MIMA_OWN_TAIL_BEATS`). The flash of 263 is now the moon's light all the time (`MIMA_GLOW_ALPHA` 0.3, `MIMA_GLOW_REACH` 0.375 of its half thickness), and in its last beat it reaches out twice as far and back (`MIMA_FLASH_REACH` 0.75). Tails of stars that ops act on were traced in 16 steps all along, every frame and twice (drawn, and checked for hits): with all of Mima's stars tailed she cost 6.7 ms a frame; now only while an op acts on what the tail covers (`opsActBetween`), otherwise straight between the turns the star knows of. The spawn and activation sounds are twice as loud: the samples themselves (a channel cannot go above full volume), the few peaks that would pass full scale rounded off softly. Stars 3 → 4.
266. **(2026-11-02) The week's list, the simple ones.** Wriggle: twice as many fireflies (`WRIGGLE_EVERY` 16 → 8), stars 1 → 2. Sukuna's 0.5x–1.5x was already in (211). Nazrin: the bullets she turns fly twice as fast as they flew (new `NAZRIN_CONVERTED_SPEED` 2). Sagume: the copies keep a quarter of the bullets (`Halved()` twice; was half), stars 5 → 4. Eika: a clump flies 1.5 times as fast as the law of impulse gives it (new `EIKA_CLUMP_SPEED`; the impulse velocity itself is kept apart, `Clump.Flight`, so that a bullet that joins does not speed it up again). Raiko: the ripple's line is fainter (`RAIKO_RIPPLE_ALPHA` 0.4 → 0.3) and its edges soft over 4 px (new `RAIKO_RIPPLE_SOFT`, `StageLayer.AddSoftRing`: three solid rings over each other, each wider; the layers' arcs 32 → 64). Only one boss explosion is heard: with two bosses the two last sections each played it (and replayed it while the later one had not ended); now once, when the first of them ends. Yumemi: the grey wave is twice as thick (`YUMEMI_WAVE_HALF_WIDTH` 34 → 68). Nemuno: 4 of every 10 slider beams are hers (the 3rd, 5th, 8th and 10th; was the 10th: new `NEMUNO_BEAMS_OF_EVERY` 4), stars 3 → 4. Koakuma: bats live half as long: they home for 8 beats (`KOAKUMA_HOMING_BEATS` 16 → 8), fade after 4 beats near you (new `KOAKUMA_BAT_NEAR_BEATS`; Byakuren's beams keep 8) and are gone after 30 s (new `KOAKUMA_BAT_LIFE_MS`; Byakuren's keep 60). Byakuren: the red arrows are half the size (`BYAKUREN_ARROW_SIZE` 2 → 1, `BYAKUREN_ARROW_NEAR_SIZE` 4 → 2). Kaguya: twice as many falling stars (`KAGUYA_EVERY` 8 → 4, `KAGUYA_EVERY_AGAIN` 4 → 2). Sakuya's and Reisen's previews twice as see-through (`SAKUYA_PREVIEW_VISIBLE` 0.1125 → 0.05625, `REISEN_PREVIEW_VISIBLE` 0.225 → 0.1125). Yuyuko: twice as many flowers on the tree (`YUYUKO_OWN_FLOWERS` 4 → 8, the "Tree flowers share" default 0.125 → 0.25). Joon: twice as many golden bullets, twice as often (`JOON_EVERY` 10 → 5, `JOON_EVERY_BEATS` 4 → 2; the end of an attack stays every second), stars 2 → 3.
267. **(2026-11-02) Tojiko's bolts in one piece and white; twice the rain. Hisami's copies, Sakuya's knives, Kaguya's moon.** Tojiko: a bolt and its branches were separate beams, each drawn whole (edge, body, core) before the next, so the blue edge of every branch lay over the white of the bolt where it starts: they are now one piece, drawn layer by layer together like Yuyuko's wood (new `LaserInstance.Piece`, `BulletField.beamPieces`). Their body (0.94, 0.96, 1) counted as a white bullet, so the core was the dark middle white bullets have: a dark stripe down every bolt. The core is now white (`LaserInstance.CoreColour` for beams that are not wood), and the body is light blue (0.7, 0.78, 1) while the bolt is see-through, turning pure white as it swells solid. Every bullet that goes out of the top now falls back as two ellipses, one where it left and one elsewhere along the top by the number of the crossing (new `TOJIKO_RAIN_DROPS` 2; `TOJIKO_EVERY` was already 1). Hisami: the copies she records are her hair's colour, black with a violet sheen, whatever the bullet's was (new `TINT_HISAMI_HAIR` (0.2, 0.16, 0.3)). Sakuya: her held knives have green previews too, where the mirroring will put them (as `DeriveKind.Held` does: through the centre, `SAKUYA_RELEASE_DISTANCE_FACTOR` further out) and pointing the way they will fly. Kaguya: the moon was a 320 px photo enlarged (`moon_big.webp`) drawn about 3500 px wide; only its bottom ever shows, so that part is now a picture of its own, `moon_cap.webp`, 3840 × 800 (2667 px per radius): the photo's seas and bright ground enlarged smoothly, with 16000 small craters made on the sphere (squashed towards the edge; a lighter rim round a slightly darker floor, as at full moon; young ones bright with rays) and grain at four scales, by the new `Tools/MoonCap`. The whole moon is still drawn from `moon_big.webp` if a screen is so tall that more than 0.3 of its radius shows.
268. **(2026-11-02) Shion's arrows, Saki's side shots, Meiling's feeding balls, Ringo's skewers, Futo's boat.** Shion: her warning arrows point the way each bullet will go when it turns, back the way it came (they pointed at her; `reverseBossAt` is gone). Saki: every 4 beats of an attack (from 4 beats in) every bullet on the screen shoots two bullets half its size, one to its left and one to its right of the way it flies, as fast as it flies (new `SAKI_SIDE_EVERY_BEATS` 4, at first 2 and halved at once, `SAKI_SIDE_SIZE` 0.5; `StampedeAct.Takes`; what they shoot does not shoot again). Bench (Chirality 3:00, 15 s): 1.6 → 2.4 ms a frame (3.5 at every 2 beats). Meiling: pulls of 16 beats, so half as many balls (`MEILING_PULL_BEATS` 8 → 16), and a thrown ball goes on feeding: every bullet it touches on its way is swallowed and the ball grows by its area, drawn, dimming and hit-tested at its new size (`Throw.Fed`, `swallows`; the act now looks at the bullets while a ball flies, also after her section). Ringo: every fourth three (a bullet and the two copies behind it) is a dango on a skewer: a thin beam in the bullets' colour joins the three, 0.3 bullet radii to each side, and hurts like a slider body; where one of them is gone it joins the others (new `RingoAct`, `BossPeriod.Skewers` from the planner, `RINGO_SKEWER_EVERY` 4, `RINGO_SKEWER_WIDTH` 0.3). Her big fast bullets now sound like Mokou's impact (`mokou_wall_hit`) every time she fires them. Futo: no bullet can be in her boat: every step, a bullet whose body the boat touches is put on its edge the shortest way and flies on the way it was flying, as fast (it was thrown out of the side at twice its speed, once per boat; `FUTO_BOAT_PUSH` is gone; the push is no ricochet).
269. **(2026-11-02) Bosses stay until their mechanics are over; Reimu's pile; ricochet flashes; Mima's tails cheaper.** The "six-angle beam" (Embezzlement, 1:51, Nue + Raiko) was Miko's: the cones of her last mark light up and turn into beams 8 beats later, after her section had ended, so they came from nowhere in the next one. A boss whose mechanic goes on after her section now stays where she stood until it is over, and fades then: `StageAct.MechanicsEnd` → `BossPeriod.MechanicsEnd`, shown by two new lingering `BossCharacter`s (one per rank; the next boss of her rank keeps the usual one; no warning stars). Miko stays until her last beams and rings, Mima until her last moon has faded; the other acts end with their sections. Reimu: the pile kept every bullet it had ever caught, long gone ones too, and once it held `REIMU_PILE_MAX` it caught nothing more for the rest of her section; it now starts again when everything an attack caught is gone. Bullets that a boss's op acts on (with another boss beside her, or flying on into the next boss's section) were never caught: they are now, and lie still whatever the ops would do (`BulletEmitter.Evaluate` keeps a stuck bullet with ops where it lies). Every ricochet off a wall flashes like a bounce off Mima's moon, 4 bullet radii across at most, over 200 ms (new `BulletField.Flashes.cs`, `RICOCHET_FLASH_MS`, `RICOCHET_FLASH_SIZE`, `RICOCHET_FLASH_MOST` 64; pushes do not flash). Mima's star tails are no longer traced in 16 steps when the star flew straight the whole tail (same way at both ends, as far as its speed takes it): 9.4 → 7.8 ms a frame (Chirality 2:00, 90 s); the rest is the moon ops on the beam slices.
270. **(2026-11-02) Every ricochet flashes; Seija's red glow.** (The user: a boss who is finishing her mechanics never keeps the next boss from coming: so it is, the next boss of her rank comes on time and the lingering one is drawn apart, where she stood.) The ricochet flash of 269 was only for bullets of ricocheting rings off the walls; now everything that bounces flashes where it bounces (`BulletField.Flashes.cs`): pushes of acts that are bounces (new `StageAct.PushIsRicochet`: Ran's beams, Kyouko's glass; `Deflection.By` now says which act turned a bullet), the bounces in the ops (Seija's border, Kyouko's glass: `TrajectoryOp.Bounces` and `Trajectory.recordBounce`, also for bullets, beams' slices, stage bullets and homing bullets that bounce off Seija's border), and the acts that work out their own bounces hand them over (`addFlash`): Reimu's orbs off the border and off each other, Hecatia's worlds, Joon's golden bullets, Shion's talismans that come back off the border, Tenshi's bullets that come back off a wall. Mima's moon and Meiling's and Mokou's balls flashed already. Pushes that are no bounce (Futo's boat, Ringo's big bullets, Okina's portals, Eika, Saki, Raiko) do not. At most 64 flashes at once. Seija: while the screen is upside down a big see-through red glow lies round the player, coming and going with the turn (new `SEIJA_GLOW_RADII` 8, `SEIJA_GLOW_ALPHA` 0.35).
271. **(2026-11-02) Reimu's pile packed tight; Saki: every fourth bullet.** Reimu: the pile is still spread evenly all round the border (the golden-ratio places), but packed as tight as the bullets' shapes allow, body against body as Eika's clumps stick: a bullet lies against the border by how far its shape reaches that way (was: its circle), and goes in from its place only as far as it must (steps of a quarter radius, was a half, then halved six times between the last step without room and the first with it) to lie against what is there by their shapes (`BulletCollision.Reach` both ways; was circles that could overlap by a tenth). A lying star keeps the turn it had spun to (`Pile.Shape`, `Pile.Face`). (The user at first asked for Eika's attachment and then, on seeing the start, to keep the even spread with no room to spare.) Saki: only every fourth bullet shoots the two side bullets (new `SAKI_SIDE_EVERY` 4; every 4 beats as before).
272. **(2026-11-02) Marisa's sparks turn to you; Wakasagihime's seaweed; Yuyuko's flowers while the tree grows.** Marisa: the Master Spark is 10 % thinner (`MARISA_SPARK_WIDE` 5 → 4.5, `MARISA_SPARK_THICKNESS` 0.8 → 0.72), and once it has grown (its widening part is out: `LaserEmitter.TurnsFrom`) it turns slowly towards the player, 10 degrees a second (`MARISA_SPARK_TURN_DEGREES`), always the same way round: the way that was nearer for the beam nearest to the player when it began (`TurnWay`, set again when the beam starts again after a seek); it never turns back, goes on round to a player who is behind it, and rests while the player is in a beam. No other boss's size mechanic touches its slices (`LaserEmitter.KeepsSize`): not Raiko's ripples, Sannyo's smoke or Sukuna's distance (drawn and hit), not Gengetsu's thick beams or Remilia's growing ones. Bench 2.0 → 2.7 ms a frame. Wakasagihime: the seaweed grows over 2 beats (was 1: `WAKASAGI_BLADE_GROW_BEATS`), and every blade to its own length, 0.5 to 1.5 times `WAKASAGI_BLADE_HEIGHT` (a quarter to three quarters of the screen; `WAKASAGI_BLADE_SHORTEST`, `_LONGEST`, by the number of the crossing). Yuyuko: a flower only showed in its own attack, so what was brought in an attack that ended while the tree still grew never came out; now a bullet that leaves the bottom while the tree grows counts for the attack that is on when it has grown (or the next), and no flower comes out before the tree has grown: those all come out then.
273. **(2026-11-02) The planner's long lists are tables; a short bench output.** No change in the game. The conditions that named dozens of mechanics one after the other (`a == X || a == Y || ...`, one of them 47 long and 2500 characters on one line) are sets at the top of `BulletPlanner`: `no_pauses`, `own_spinner_bullets` and what the spinner's stars turn into (`round_`, `knife_`, `heart_`, `arrowhead_`, `ellipse_spinner_bullets`), `no_spinner_stars`, `no_big_beams`. A new boss is an entry there. The planned rings are the same as before for 18 bosses that these lists touch (Chirality). `Tools/SectionCheck`: `BENCH_SHORT=1` prints only the times and the two costliest steps, and a list of more than three bosses is named by its first two and how many more (a whole roster from a log was echoed in full).
274. **(2026-11-03) The list of 11-03, the simple ones.** Reimu: three orbs in a throw (`REIMU_ORB_RING` 5 → 3). Meiling: a ball, growing on her or thrown, never has a radius of more than 1.2 times the playfield's height (new `MEILING_MAX_RADIUS`; the feeding goes on, the size stops). Yumemi: the flash before her pauses is bright red (its colour (1, 0.92, 0.95) → (1, 0.08, 0.06)). Wakasagihime: twice as many bubbles (`WAKASAGI_BUBBLE_EVERY` 32 → 16), the ring a bubble pops into is of bullets half the bubble's bullet's size, hitbox too (new `WAKASAGI_POP_SIZE` 0.5), seaweed half as long (`WAKASAGI_BLADE_HEIGHT` 0.5 → 0.25: an eighth to three eighths of the screen). Marisa: the Master Spark turns towards the player half as fast (`MARISA_SPARK_TURN_DEGREES` 10 → 5) and is 20 % thicker (`MARISA_SPARK_WIDE` 4.5 → 5.4, `MARISA_SPARK_THICKNESS` 0.72 → 0.864). Ran: a fan of eight golden spirals of its own every 8 beats of an attack, from its start (was one when the attack began; new `RAN_FAN_EVERY_BEATS`), stars 3 → 4. Tojiko: the see-through time of a bolt is twice as long (`TOJIKO_GHOST_BEATS` 2 → 4) and twice as see-through (`TOJIKO_GHOST_ALPHA` 0.3 → 0.15); the thunder is heard only while its bolt is solid: it fades out over 150 ms when the bolt stops hurting, when time goes back, and when the game is paused or failed (`TOJIKO_THUNDER_FADE_MS`; new `playHeldSound` / `fadeOutSound`, every fading channel has its own fade length now). Read "preview" as the see-through bolt before it turns solid.
275. **(2026-11-03) Lily White, Sukuna, Chimata, Reisen, Kaguya, Joon.** Lily White: branches only from the four corners (the middles of the four sides are gone: "remove top and bottom" read with "only on corners", so the left and right ones went too), half as many flowers on them (new `LILY_SOW_FACTOR` 0.5 of Yuyuko's share; read "flower generation" as the flowers that grow on the branches, not her own rings of flowers), stars 3 → 2. Sukuna: 0.5x at the edge of her magic circle, up to 2x at the edge of the screen (`SUKUNA_MAX_SIZE` 1.5 → 2), by how far a thing is from her against how far from the nearest edge (was: linear up to a playfield's width from her). Chimata: the talismans of her walls are twice as big (new `CHIMATA_WALL_TALISMAN_SIZE` 3; her rings keep `CHIMATA_TALISMAN_SIZE` 1.5) and side by side by their pictures, not their round bodies (`CHIMATA_WALL_SPACING` 2 radii → 1 drawn width, which is 0.8 of a radius); the knives they turn into keep the size they had. Reisen ("Reigen"): her own beams are rings of 25 (`REISEN_BEAMS`, new; were 5, in kiai 1 to 5; 13 with another boss on the screen, as everyone's are halved), half as long (`REISEN_BEAM_MS` 150 → 75), stars 3 → 4. A beam emitter had room to remember 16 finished beams: 25 crashed the field; it is sized by the beams now (`BeamFinished`). Kaguya: her own rings twice as often and twice as full (`KAGUYA_BURST_EVERY_BEATS` 4 → 2, `KAGUYA_RING_BULLETS` 20 → 40; the ninth ring of a burst would fall on the first of the next and is left out, so it is a ring every quarter beat), stars 4 → 5. Joon: her golden bullets bounce off any wall, the top too, and keep 70 % of their energy at a bounce (`JOON_ENERGY_KEPT` 0.5 → 0.7; read "energy reduced is 70 %" as what is left; 0.3 if it meant what is lost).
276. **(2026-11-03) Mugetsu: a vortex round her.** In her attacks every bullet on the screen, whoever fired it and whenever, goes round her as fast as it flies, at any distance, the even bullets of a ring one way and the odd ones the other (new `Bullets/Swirl.cs`, `BulletEmitter.Swirl`, `MUGETSU_SWIRL` 1). A closed form: the bullet's own flight turned round her by an angle that grows by its speed over its distance from her (along a straight line the log of the distance plus how far along the line it is; closer than 16 px counts as 16, `MUGETSU_SWIRL_NEAR`), so replays and seeks stay exact; after the attack it flies on straight the way it was left. The middle follows her while an attack is on and stays where she was at its end (new `SwirlAct`). Not homing arrowheads, ricochets or trains; rings that meet her attacks live a screen diagonal longer (`MUGETSU_SWIRL_LONGER`). Her every-fourth-ring-three-times-as-dense stays; the curl round the ring's own centre is gone (`MUGETSU_CURL`). Stars 2 → 4. Bench (Chirality [Expert] 1:00 + 20 s): alone 0.74 ms a frame, with Utsuho 2.7, with Cirno 3.1.
277. **(2026-11-03) Patchouli: thick water waves of three rings.** The water ripple is twice as thick (`PATCHOULI_WATER_HALF_WIDTH` 2 → 4 times Raiko's), and its riders go on three rings across the band in turn, the middle and one to either side, 0.6 of the half width apart, each ring with an even spread of its own (`PATCHOULI_WATER_RINGS`, `PATCHOULI_WATER_RING_GAP`).
278. **(2026-11-03) Seija warns of her turns.** For 4 beats before every turn of the screen (start and end of each attack, `SEIJA_WARN_BEATS`): a see-through clone of her (35 %, `SEIJA_CLONE_ALPHA`), with her magic circle and the stars of a warning flying in, where she will be seen once the screen has turned (her place mirrored through the middle; a new kind of `BossCharacter`, `Ghost`, upright on the screen), gone as it turns; and 8 red arrows round the player (`SEIJA_ARROW_*`), flashing faster and faster, all pointing at the real Seija.
279. **(2026-11-03) Bosses finish their sequence.** A boss whose section ends before her last attack and the pause after it are over goes through them: the attack is whole, the pause after it is whole, and she stays where she stood until it is over (`finishSequences`, `BossPeriod.SequenceEnd`, `FullBurst`, `FullPause`; shown by the lingering boss of 269; the next boss comes on time). What she does in an attack or a pause goes on until then (the tail pause of `PausesWithTail` runs to the end of the sequence: Utsuho's black hole, Yumemi's push, Futo's boat, Toyohime's extra ricochets, Shizuha...); her own shots stop with her section. Not a boss who never pauses, not the last boss (she explodes), not a spinner (one attack), not where her own next section carries on. On Chirality [Expert] Utsuho's first break now ends with her black hole after the section, until 0:57.7, and Yumemi's kiai with a last pause until 1:12.1. `sectioncheck periods` prints it.
280. **(2026-11-03) Slider beams stretched too far fade out.** A piece of a slider's beam (slices joined to each other) that something has stretched to more than 3 times as long as it would be if nothing had happened to it fades out, and is gone at 3.5 times (`BEAM_STRETCH_FADE_FROM`, `_TO`; `BulletLook.StretchVisible` in 16 steps, `LaserEmitter.fadeStretched`); half faded it no longer hurts. Not the spinner's beams nor any boss's (asked while it was being done: "exclude beams thrown by bosses", then "only slider beams").
281. **(2026-11-03) The second list of 11-03, the simple ones.** Letty: half as many bullets from her dissipating beams (`LETTY_BULLET_INTERVAL_BEATS` 0.0625 → 0.125, `LETTY_MAX_SLICES` 96 → 48: a slice every eighth of a beat, still four bullets each; halving "Bullets per dissipating slice" instead would have changed her fan). Shizuha ("Aki"): twice as many maple leaves (`SHIZUHA_EVERY` 4 → 2: every second bullet), stars 1 → 2. Tenshi ("16 bullets, 8 bullets"): every sixteenth bullet at the bottom (`TENSHI_EVERY` 8 → 16), every eighth in a wave (`TENSHI_WAVE_EVERY` 4 → 8), stars 3 → 2. Wriggle: twice as many fireflies (`WRIGGLE_EVERY` 8 → 4), and their glow is the green the firefly is drawn in (`TINT_GREEN`'s body colour; it was a lime of its own), stars 2 → 3. Ringo: her big fast bullets half as fast (`RINGO_VOLLEY_SPEED_FACTOR` 5 → 2.5). Nue: in the shooting the UFOs get from 60 degrees off to their even angles (straight out) in the first half of it and shoot straight out for the rest (`NUE_SWEEP_SHARE` 0.5, new; was evenly over all of it). Kaguya: every ring of her own is turned 3 degrees further than the one before, over her section, so the bursts spin (`KAGUYA_RING_TURN_DEGREES`, new; they all had the same angle). Chiyari: a vampire's jaw in every pool instead of two fangs: two long fangs above with two small teeth between them, four shorter fangs below pointing up (`StageTexture.BloodPool`; looked at on a picture made from the same code: all well inside the pool).
282. **(2026-11-03) Ringo: why the skewers were not seen.** Two reasons. With another boss on the screen there were none at all: the planner made the threes and remembered their rings, and the halving of two-boss sections then put a new ring in the place of every one, so the act found none of them. Now the rings carry the mark of their three (`BulletEmitter.SkewerId`, `SkewerPart`; `BossPeriod.Skewers` holds the numbers), kept by a ring made in their place (halved, scaled, shot from the boss: `keepSkewer`), and the act finds the threes by it (a three whose parts are not all there once with as many bullets each has none). Where there were skewers they hid under the bullets: the three are 2.4 radii apart, so 0.4 radii of the beam showed between two of them. Now it sticks out 1.8 radii in front of the middle of the first and 3.5 behind the middle of the last, the handle (`RINGO_SKEWER_TIP`, `RINGO_SKEWER_HANDLE`), and hurts there too.
283. **(2026-11-03) Shion: what she has turned round does not turn again.** A thing her turn has reversed is not reversed by a later one (`Trajectory.Trace`, as Utsuho's pull takes a thing once), bullets and beams alike; no red arrow beside it either.
284. **(2026-11-03) Ran: a bullet bounces off her spirals twice.** `RAN_BOUNCES` 2 (was once): her bounces are counted the way Kyouko's walls count theirs (`FoxAct.CountsAsWallBounce`); one that something else has turned is still left alone. A bullet that bounces is put back on the side it came from (where it got to past the beam, mirrored across it): from the far side it would have crossed the beam again at once and bounced straight back. Time going back over a turn now puts back the turn before it, not none (`deflectLog` keeps it, `Undeflect(bullet, before)`): with two bounces, going back between them lost the first.
285. **(2026-11-03) Eiki: smaller circle, and a second one mirrored.** `EIKI_RADIUS_CIRCLES` 6 → 4.8 (20 % smaller). When an attack begins a second circle as big is placed where hers is mirrored through the middle of the screen, left-right and top-bottom ("mirror x y position"), drawn the same. It sends what enters it at its middle the same way; one op for both (`TrajectoryOp.SecondPoint`): a thing is taken by the circle it enters first, once, so its speed comes back whole. The player is kept in the first one only, and only outside that one are bullets dim.
286. **(2026-11-03) Satori: three eyes round the player.** Before every reading three eyes open round the player, evenly (120 degrees apart), the first in front as before, and each is a cone of its own: a bullet in any of the three turns (`SATORI_EYES` 3, new). Read "spawns three eyes" as three cones, an eye showing where each points; if only the picture was meant, the other two cones go.
287. **(2026-11-03) Sakuya: the preview shows what flies on too.** What is fired while time stands still is not frozen but is mirrored when the stop ends: it has its green copy now as well (her own knives fired meanwhile among them), the copy moving with it. So do the bullets of the acts that the ops move (`stageSakuyaAlpha`). The held knives had theirs already (267).
288. **(2026-11-03) Yuyuko: flowers on the growing branches; 69 of her own.** A flower comes out as soon as its branch has grown to its place, also while the tree grows (since 272 none showed before the tree had grown). One whose place is not reached in time to show in its attack goes to the first attack in which it is (it was: to the attack that was on when the tree had grown). A bullet brings its flower once, in whichever attack (one set for all of them; it was one per attack). The tree's own flowers in every attack 8 → 69 (`YUYUKO_OWN_FLOWERS`; read "initial flowers" as those, which come with every attack; if only the first attack was meant, the others go back to 8). Stars 3 → 4.
