289. **(2026-11-03) Bosses finish their sequence: Yukari too.** 279 made a boss go through the pause after her last attack, but Yukari's freeze, her train in the pause and her warnings went by the pauses between two attacks only (`BossOps.Pauses`): she stood there doing nothing and left. They go by `PausesWithTail` now, like every other boss's pause mechanic: in the pause after her last attack, whether it is in her section or after it, three bullets of every eight freeze into talismans and fly to the middle when it ends.
290. **(2026-11-03) Luna Child: slider outlines turn into beams that grow outwards.** In her attacks the outline of every slider (while its body fades out as with Hidden) becomes a beam that grows outwards from it, all round it: everything that is as far from the slider's path as the outline is, further and further out at her rings' speed (`LUNA_OUTLINE_SPEED` 1 of the section's bullet speed), round its ends and bends, until it has left the screen. It hurts like a slider body (the player is on it when their distance to the path is its distance). The path is taken from the slider's zone with a point every 20 px (`LUNA_OUTLINE_STEP`), the beam has a point every 12 px round the bends (`LUNA_OUTLINE_POINT_GAP`); what lies inside a bend is left out (closer to another part of the path), checked on a picture made from the same code. It starts when the slider starts; if it was meant to start when the slider ends, that is one line. Stars 4 → 5.
291. **(2026-11-03) Yuyuko 32, Eiki's second circle is room too, Luna Child's outlines have gaps.** Yuyuko: `YUYUKO_OWN_FLOWERS` 69 → 32. Eiki: the player may be in either circle (`Limit`): inside one they go where they like; outside both they stay on the border of the nearer one, so with the cursor they jump across to the other when the cursor is nearer to it (with the keyboard, Space to the cursor). Only what is outside both is dim (`StageDim.Second`). Luna Child: an outline is cut into 20 pieces all round, each 30 % beam in its middle and the rest a gap, as her rings of arcs are (`LUNA_OUTLINE_PIECES` = `LUNA_ARCS`, `LUNA_OUTLINE_SHARE` = `LUNA_ARC_SHARE`): spread by the length round it, so they grow with it as her arcs grow with their rings; a piece begins and ends exactly where it should, not at the nearest point. What hurts is the pieces only (the player's place on the outline is found from the nearest point of it). Looked at on a picture made from the same code.
292. **(2026-11-05) The list of 11-05, the simple ones.** Kaguya: half as many of her own bullets (`KAGUYA_RING_BULLETS` 40 → 20; `KAGUYA_RING_TURN_DEGREES` 3 → 6, still a third of the gap between two), stars 5 → 4. Chimata: walls half as often (`CHIMATA_WALL_EVERY_BEATS` 4 → 8), stars 4 → 3. Tojiko: half the rain (`TOJIKO_RAIN_DROPS` 2 → 1: one ellipse where the bullet left; the other way to halve it, every second bullet with both drops, `TOJIKO_EVERY` 2, keeps the ones elsewhere along the top), and "Thunder" read as her bolts, as Iku is still a placeholder: harmless for twice as long (`TOJIKO_GHOST_BEATS` 4 → 8), stars 4 → 3. Her own bullets had a `Gravity` but as they are not `Falling` it was never used: they flew straight and bounced off nothing. 296. **(2026-11-05) A new star rating: how much the map makes the player move.** New `Bullets/ForcedMovement.cs` is what `DanmakusuDifficultyCalculator` uses now (`BulletDensity` stays for comparison in the `stars` tool). A lazy player plays the map without bosses: they stand still until something would hit them within 400 ms (`FORCED_HORIZON_MS`), then walk the shortest safe way at keyboard speed (`KEYBOARD_SPEED` 0.4 px/ms), and when no walk saves them they teleport to the nearest safe place (the freeze keys, Space). What aims at the player aims at this player, so aimed rings and beams are what move them. Load of a moment: diagonals walked a second x 4 (`FORCED_WALK_WEIGHT`, 1 made walking count for next to nothing) + dodges started a second x 0.25 (`FORCED_DODGE_WEIGHT`; much the same way, within about 60 degrees, is the same dodge) + teleports a second x 1 (`FORCED_TELEPORT_WEIGHT`; nowhere safe counts as one). From there as before: about the last second counts, the hardest moment of every 400 ms, from the hardest down each 0.9 times the last. Stars = 0.034 x value ^ 1.519 (`FORCED_STAR_SCALE`, `_POWER`), fitted to osu!'s on the 23 example difficulties: rank correlation 0.894 (density's was 0.86), Cyaegha [Normal] about 3 up to Dynamite [DARKNESS] and Final Mission about 9. Planning and rating take 0.3-4 s a map, in the background, on the hardest map about 4 s (with the plan). Nothing random: a map always gets the same stars. `sectioncheck stars` shows walked, dodges, teleports next to osu!'s and the old density's.

302. **(2026-11-05) Boss sections: a floor by the song's averages.** Some songs got too little: X.E.N.O [veto] 24 % of the song in 2 parts, while [Setu] of the same song got 73 %. The user asked why not build on the averages of the hitsounds and the song's volume. Measured with the new tool mode `sectioncheck averages` on the 23 difficulties with wanted sections and 10 without: replacing the score by them (`AverageScore`: hitsounds and level against their averages, alike; a phrase at the average or above) lifts the least coverage to 40 %, but the average error against the wanted sections goes 0.065 → 0.145 (Cyaegha: 57-71 % → 86 % against about 53 % wanted). So the score stays, and it has a floor: a song of which it leaves less than half (`BOSS_FILL_FLOOR_SHARE` 0.5) also gets what is above its averages, then down in steps of 0.05 (`_STEP`) to 0.8 of them (`_LOWEST`) until half is reached (`PickWithFloor`). Result on all 33: least coverage 24 % → 51 %, average coverage 63 % → 65 %, average error 0.065 → 0.062, worst error 0.339 → 0.269 (Final Mission, which had 48 % and now 66 %); of the example maps only Final Mission changes, the others are at half or more already. X.E.N.O [veto] 24 % → 51 %, [rhythm] 44 % → 58 %. Replacing the score altogether is one line in `IntenseParts`, if the user prefers that after playing.

315. **(2026-11-05) Elly: she stops for her blow, the swing is the arc lighting up.** The arc half as thick (`ELLY_ARC_HALF_WIDTH_CIRCLES` 0.9 → 0.45), the blade too (`ELLY_BLADE_WIDTH_CIRCLES` 0.7 → 0.35; read "thickness of scythe attack" as both). Twice as fast on the way (`ELLY_SLIDE_SPEED` 1.5 → 3). A blow now only starts on a mark of two beats, with the player within reach (the arc's radius) and the last blow over, so one every four beats at the most (it was one every two beats, always, wherever the player was); she stands still from the warning to the end of the swing; on her way she comes to 0.95 of her reach (`ELLY_NEAR`, was 0.85: with the thin arc at 0.85 standing still would have been safe). The swing does not happen in the preview: the preview is the warning, her scythe drawn back to the arc's first tip; the swing is the arc lighting up, tip to tip at one speed with the tip of the blade at the end of what is lit, at twice the old average speed (`ELLY_SWING_DEGREES_PER_BEAT` 280, was 70 degrees each way in a beat, 140 a beat; so it takes 0.57 beats, the arc used to light up in 0.5; `ELLY_SCYTHE_SWING_DEGREES`, `ELLY_SCYTHE_BEATS` are gone). The blade lies on the circle of the swing now, and no longer hurts by itself: everything she does comes after the warning, and the blade would otherwise have hit an unwarned band further in than the arc (read from "preview serves as warning"; earlier its blade hurt all the time). Stars 4 → 3.
316. **(2026-11-05) Youmu's slashes and the tips of Ran's spirals cut bullets.** What Kagerou's and Elly's claws do to a bullet moved out of `ClawAct` into a new base class, `CuttingAct` (`UI/Stages/CuttingAct.cs`), which `SlashAct` and `FoxAct` use too. Youmu (read "laserbeams" as her slashes, the long beams across the screen, not the short beams of her rings, which keep their thickness, their number and their room): thickness halved (`YOUMU_SLASH_WIDTH` 0.5, as previews and solid), twice as many (`YOUMU_MORE_SLASHES` 8 → 17, so 18), and as one turns solid it cuts the bullets across it in two along it, one part flying on, the other back (never more unevenly than 8 to 2; it still pokes holes in slider beams, whose slices fall as petals). More spread out: the line through the two nearest to the middle comes first as it did, but every next one is the line through two watched bullets that is least like any chosen so far, by angle and by how far it passes from the middle (`pick`, it used to take the next nearest, so all nine crossed in the middle), up to `YOUMU_WATCHED_MOST` 48 bullets; lines closer than `YOUMU_LINES_APART` 0.05 are not taken; the watched area is wider (`YOUMU_INNER` 0.7 → 0.85). Performance: with 18 slashes she cost 5.25 ms a frame on Chirality [Asymmetry] (worst 27); each slash is a line now (`Normal`, `Off`, a dot product, not a distance to a segment), the beams are looked at once for all the slashes of a mark, and old slashes are no longer gone through: 3.73 ms (worst 16). Ran: while a fan grows the tips of its spirals cut the bullets on their way in two, along the spiral (`FoxAct.Takes`); once they have stopped growing nothing is cut and bullets bounce off as before.
317. **(2026-11-05) Triple Boss Selection (`TS`, `DanmakusuModTripleBossSelection`), a new mod.** Three bosses are on the screen in every section, with a third of the bullets and beams each, instead of two with half (at least 3 bosses, and the picker's slider goes three times as far as the map has room for). `IBossSelection.Triple`; `BulletPlanner.Plan` and `BarPeriods` have `alwaysTriple`, and `atOnce` (1, 2, 3) replaces `together` in `bossSlots`, `bossStep` and the cutting of sections; `BulletEmitter.Thirded` keeps every third bullet, `thirdCounts` divides the beam bursts by three (Marisa keeps all her beams, as with two). `besideHer` replaces `partnerOf`: any of the others can be Marisa, Letty or Junko. Yorihime repeats both of the others in a section she shares. Rank 2: a third `BossCharacter`, `lingeringBoss3`, `bossPosition3`, `moveBoss3`; acts ask `bossPositionOf(Period.Rank)` where they asked rank 1 or not. Those who stand at one place (the middle or opposite the player) circle round it evenly apart and share one magic circle there (`spreadThree`, `shareCirclesOfThree`; with two bosses `updateBossOrbit` goes on as ever). HUD: three lines in the bar, the third name in the middle. Colours: twelve combo colours when exactly three bosses are all on at once. Mokou and Kaguya back together after the last section stay two, with half the bullets. In `SectionCheck`: `periods ... triple`, and `field` with `a+b+c` plays all three at once. Bench on Chirality [Asymmetry], 0:40 + 60 s: Youmu + Ran + Elly 4.3 ms a frame, Hecatia + Kagerou + Elly 3.9, Marisa + Junko + Letty 3.3, Yorihime + Youmu + Ran 4.1, Mokou + Kaguya + Hecatia 3.2, Chen + Utsuho + Seija 3.2, worst 13-20 ms; none crashed.
318. **(2026-11-05) Hecatia's World's Ender: the impact frame lasts 500 ms, whatever the tempo.** It was half a beat (`ENDER_IMPACT_BEATS` 0.5 → `ENDER_IMPACT_MS` 500), about 150 ms on Chirality and less on anything faster, which the user saw as happening instantly. It still ends at the split, which stays two beats after the cut, so it now starts earlier; above 240 BPM two beats are shorter than that, and there it starts before the cut has finished.
319. **(2026-11-05) Elly's scythe only shows with the blow; the error in the boss menu with three bosses.** Elly: her scythe is not there until she swings (it was held at the player on her way, and drawn back to the arc's first tip over the first beat of the preview, which looked like a swing the wrong way during the preview; `ELLY_SCYTHE_READY_BEATS` is gone). It appears as the arc lights up, goes tip to tip the way the arc grew, and fades over half a beat where it ended (`ELLY_SCYTHE_BACK_BEATS` → `ELLY_SCYTHE_FADE_BEATS` 0.5; read as: it does not stay after the swing, which was not said). Swing and lighting up twice as fast (`ELLY_SWING_DEGREES_PER_BEAT` 280 → 560, so 0.29 beats tip to tip). She moves twice as fast (`ELLY_SLIDE_SPEED` 3 → 6). Triple Boss Selection: the picker set its slider's lowest value (3) before its highest (2 at the start), and the framework refuses a lowest above the highest: "An unhandled error has occurred" each time the menu opened, and the rest of the picker's refresh did not run. The highest is set first now.
320. **(2026-11-05) Yukari's trains and frozen bullets, Zanmu, Junko.** Yukari, "the sound plays but no train": her pause's freeze took hold of a train fired when the attack before it began (one bullet, number 0, is always among her three of eight), if the train had not arrived by then: it stopped on its way and turned into a talisman, while its horn played as planned (17 of 37 trains on Chirality [Easy], 11 of 24 on Cyaegha [Normal], 3 of 52 on Dynamite [DARKNESS]; new tool mode `sectioncheck yukari <maps>`). No boss's op touches a train now (`ConfigureOps`; Mima's `SkipTrains` is gone with it). Yukari, "frozen bullets do not pull in": `ConfigureOps` left out an op later than the ring's own lifetime, without counting the time the freeze before it adds, so a ring whose lifetime ran out during the pause was frozen and never sent to the middle: it flew straight on after the pause. That was about 30 % of all her frozen rings (1795 of 5245 on Chirality [Asymmetry]). It counts the frozen time now, which goes for every boss whose freeze is followed by another op. Zanmu: stopped bullets no longer divide, they fall as they are (`ZANMU_SPLIT_*` gone). Junko: slices that are not ready are half as visible (0.3 → `JUNKO_GHOST_ALPHA` 0.15).
321. **(2026-11-07) A boss counts between 120 and 240 BPM.** New `DanmakusuBalance.BossBeat` (`BOSS_MAX_BPM` 240, `BOSS_MIN_BPM` 120): above 240 the tempo is halved as often as needed, below 120 doubled (300 → 150, 250 → 125, 100 → 200, 60 → 120), so it stays in proportion to the music. It is the beat of every boss period (`bossBeat`, so every act and op that goes by `BeatLength`), of the boss patterns (spirals, Gengetsu's, Sanae's, Doremy's, Rumia's, Meiling's, Kaguya's, Lily's, Kyouko's, Letty's beams, Yorihime's pieces, the revival), and of the room an attack and a pause need (`shortestPiece`, so the sections are cut to fit the attacks as they now are). Read as the boss's tempo only: the map's own bullets (their speed by the local BPM) and how sections are found from the map keep `CapBeat` (halved above 480). `lengths` on all 23 example maps: no boss without a whole attack or pause.
322. **(2026-11-07) Hecatia's World's Ender: a dangerous Earth, lines from the split.** The Earth is as big as the playfield is high (`ENDER_EARTH_SIZE` 1.2 → 1), and from when it has grown until it splits it hurts like a slider body (`Hurts`; within `ENDER_EARTH_HIT` 0.95 of its radius; the holes the chunks leave do not count, it is one round thing to hit; read "big Earth" as from when it has grown, not while it grows); so do the very big chunks of its halves while they can be seen (each an ellipse in its picture, `ENDER_CHUNK_HIT`), and every chunk that flies off (they were bullets that are used up by a hit: `Slider` now). The cracks come up as soon as the Sun begins to cut, over a quarter of a beat (`ENDER_CRACKS_BEATS`; they came up from when it had grown). The lines of the impact frame and of the explosion come from the split: each from a place of its own along the cut, off to one side, fanned out over 150 degrees from straight across (`ENDER_PEN_FAN_DEGREES`; they came from the middle of the Earth), and the streaks of the bullets and the blur after the explosion go towards the nearest place on the cut, not the middle of the screen (`towardsImpact`, `TowardsSplit`, `enderAfterBlurAct`; read "split taken as centre" as the line of the cut: its middle is the middle of the screen, which is where they went before). Half as many lines (`ENDER_PEN_LINES` 72 → 36), a new set every 30 ms (was 40) and between two sets they waver, another turn and width every 16 ms (`ENDER_PEN_WOBBLE*`, `ENDER_PEN_TURN_DEGREES`). The golden bullets of the cut and the split: half as big (`ENDER_BULLET_SIZE`), bloom 0.8 → 1 (`ENDER_BULLET_BLOOM`) with a soft orange light under each (`ENDER_BULLET_GLOW`), speeds evenly from 0.5 to 1.5 times a plain bullet's (`ENDER_BURST_SLOWEST` 0.55 → 0.5, `_FASTEST` 1.65 → 1.5; those along the line now too, they all flew at 1). The impact frame is 200 ms (`ENDER_IMPACT_MS`; it read 150, not the 500 of entry 318). Her thrown worlds: what they shoot out at an impact comes out of their edges, not their middles (`Gathering.Release(edge)`).
323. **(2026-11-07) Wakasagihime: what swims leaves a trace of beam.** Every bullet that swims (all that is fired in her attacks) leaves a blue beam behind it along the last four beats of its way, narrowing to 0.3 at its end, half a slider beam wide, that hurts like a slider body (`TideAct.traceNow`; `WAKASAGI_TRACE_BEATS` 4, `_STEPS` 10, `_WIDTH` 0.5, `_END` 0.3). Worked out once a frame from each bullet's own closed form, on several cores, and a trace that is all off the screen is left out: alone on Chirality [Asymmetry] 3.4 ms a frame (6.6 at first).
324. **(2026-11-07) Every cut flashes.** Wherever a bullet is cut in two (Kagerou's and Elly's claws, Youmu's slashes, the tips of Ran's spirals: `CuttingAct.CutInTwo`) a ricochet's flash comes up, and where a claw cuts a beam, once a beam.
325. **(2026-11-07) Elly: her blade hurts, and she shoots bats.** Her blade hurts like a slider body while it swings (not the shaft, not as it fades; it hurt through the arc alone since 315). "Now they shoot a lot of bats around, they do not home": read as her own shots: bats like Chiyari's that do not home, but as fast as what they were (`ELLY_BAT_SPEED` 1), twice as many in every ring (`ELLY_BAT_COUNT_FACTOR` 2), in a spinner the spinner's stars too (she shot round bullets; her own rings are stars now, which the bat windows turn into bats, as Koakuma's and Chiyari's; `BatAct` straight with its own speed). Bats that do not home fly straight: when each leaves the screen is worked out at once, and nothing follows it step by step (Chiyari's too).
326. **(2026-11-07) A boss's shots no longer come out of the next boss.** While the next boss of a rank only gave her warning (her period begins before she shoots, and overlaps the end of the one before), she was already the one drawn and the place the shots of that rank come from: the last shots of the boss before came out of her. Now the boss who shoots is the boss there until the next one starts shooting (`latestBoss`, `BossCharacter`, `Supersedes`). And what a boss who has left does while a mechanic of hers goes on comes from where she lingers, not from the next boss of her rank (`bossVisualOf(BossPeriod)`, `bossPositionOf(BossPeriod)`, in every act).
327. **(2026-11-07) Elly: the shaft hurts too, and shows.** While the scythe swings its shaft hurts like a slider body as well as the blade (not as it fades), so the whole fan inside the arc is swept and close to her is no longer safe. The shaft is twice as thick (`ELLY_SHAFT_HALF_WIDTH_CIRCLES` 0.28, was 0.14 inline) and drawn to be seen: light wood (was dark brown), a dark outline, a bright streak down its middle, and the glow of her light along both sides. Stars 3 → 4.
328. **(2026-11-07) Luna Child's outlines: checked, no change.** The user asked why the beams growing out of the slider outlines do not come out. In the headless field (Chirality [Asymmetry], 0:30 + 60 s) all 285 sliders that start in her attacks get an outline, and about 150 000 pieces of them are handed to the renderer over the minute: they are made, from sliders that start in her attacks only (none in her pauses, and her breaks have no sliders). New counter `BulletField.ProfileOutlinePieces`, printed by `sectioncheck field` with `ACT_PROBE=1`.
329. **(2026-11-07) Sukuna: at most one and a half times as big.** In her attacks a bullet or beam (hitbox too) grows from half its size at the edge of her magic circle to 1.5 times at the edge of the screen (`SUKUNA_MAX_SIZE` 2 → 1.5). Stars stay 2.
330. **(2026-11-07) Ringo: no more echoes.** Every sixth bullet fired in her attacks is no longer thrown out of her again at half the size and speed (the planner no longer makes them; `RINGO_THROW_EVERY`, `RINGO_ECHO_SCALE`, `RINGO_ECHO_SHADE` gone; the field's `DeriveKind.Echo` stays, unused). Her rows of three, the skewers and the big bullets are as they were. Stars stay 4.
331. **(2026-11-07) Chimata's walls half as fast; Ringo's big bullets slower.** Chimata: her walls come in at half a plain bullet's speed (new `CHIMATA_WALL_SPEED` 0.5; they flew at a plain bullet's). The knives they turn into near the player creep as fast as before, a quarter of a plain bullet's speed (they went by the talisman's speed, which would have halved them too). Stars stay 3. Ringo: her big bullets 1.5 times as fast as a plain bullet (`RINGO_VOLLEY_SPEED_FACTOR` 2.5 → 1.5; the user: "1.5x instead of 2.5x"). With the echoes gone as well, stars 4 → 3.
332. **(2026-11-07) Gengetsu up to 2.5 times; Mugetsu's vortex stays where it was.** Gengetsu: everything on the screen speeds up to 2.5 times its speed by the end of her attacks (`GENGETSU_SPEED_UP` 2 → 2.5). Mugetsu: the middle a bullet goes round no longer follows her: a ring fired in one of her attacks goes round where she stood as it was fired (`BulletEmitter.SwirlCentre`, taken by the field as the ring is fired, `SnapSwirl`), and what was on the screen already goes round where she stood when the attack began (`Swirl.Grabbed`); neither moves after that (read "spin centre of already spawned bullets don't change, snapshots boss position that was during its spawn" so: for what was fired before the attack, its spawn as far as the vortex goes is the attack's start). It was one middle per attack that went with her.
333. **(2026-11-07) Elly's scythe is harmless again; her warning grows as fast as it lights up.** The scythe, shaft and blade, no longer hurts (327 is undone; it is still drawn as it was made visible there). The warning still lasts two beats until the arc lights up, but it grows from tip to tip as fast as it will light up (in `lightMs`, 0.29 beats; it took the whole two beats) and then stays faint until then. Stars 4 → 3.
334. **(2026-11-07) Luna Child: half as many arcs.** A ring of hers is 10 arcs, not 20 (`LUNA_ARCS`), each as long as before (`LUNA_ARC_SHARE` 0.3 → 0.15 of its own, twice as wide, share of the circle), so the gaps are wide. Read "halve the amount of her own laserbeams" as fewer arcs in a ring; a ring every 4 beats instead (`LUNA_RING_EVERY_BEATS`) would be the other reading. The pieces of the slider outlines keep 20 and their 30 % (`LUNA_OUTLINE_PIECES`, `LUNA_OUTLINE_SHARE` no longer follow the rings).
335. **(2026-11-07) Wakasagihime: traces of eight beats to a tip, and she shoots bubbles.** The user asked whether she throws straight laserbeams: no boss throws beam bursts but Marisa, so what looked like straight beams were most likely her traces from 323, four beats long through ten points, along a way that swings only a circle radius to each side. The traces now follow the last eight beats of the way (`WAKASAGI_TRACE_BEATS` 4 → 8) through a point every third of a beat (`WAKASAGI_TRACE_STEPS` 10 → 24) and narrow to a sharp tip (`WAKASAGI_TRACE_END` 0.3 → 0). Their points are kept from frame to frame (`KeptTrace`: where the bullet was at the moments of a grid on its own clock; only the new moment is worked out as its clock passes it): 3.2 ms a frame alone on Chirality [Asymmetry]. Her own shots (and in a spinner the spinner's stars) are now hollow bubbles from the start, half as many as before (every ring halved), all through her section (`BulletEmitter.Bubbles`, set at the end of the planning; `TideAct.addBubble`): harmless, each pops into a ring of eight sixteen beats later or at once when touched, as the bubbles of her attacks do. About 4800 bubbles a minute on Chirality [Asymmetry]; 5.1 ms a frame alone, over the 4 ms budget (the eight bullets of every pop). The layer that draws their rims had room for 400 rings and left the rest out: it grows now (`StageLayer.Grows` for `stageRipple`, and `addSprite` grows like `Add`).
336. **(2026-11-07) Wakasagihime: bubbles hurt; her own half as many again.** Every bubble (her own and the sixteenth bullet of her attacks) hurts as a bullet does, with its own hitbox twice a bullet's, and is used up by the hit (it does not pop then); it no longer pops when the player touches it. Her own shots: a quarter as many as before 335 (every ring halved twice). Chirality [Asymmetry], 0:40 + 60 s: 3295 bubbles (4829), 4.1 ms a frame (5.1).
337. **(2026-11-07) Sakuya's preview near the player only; Wriggle's fireflies restless; Reimu's pile half as full.** Sakuya: a green copy shows only where the mirroring will put a thing within four osu! circle radii of the player (`SAKUYA_PREVIEW_CIRCLES` 4, `nearSakuyaPreview`; bullets of rings and of the acts, and her held knives), to cost less: it was a copy of everything on the screen. Wriggle: a firefly wanders twice as restlessly (its two waves go twice as fast: new `StageBullet.WanderPace`, `WRIGGLE_WANDER_PACE` 2; the sway of petals and leaves keeps its pace) and half as far to each side (`WRIGGLE_WANDER_CIRCLES` 1.5 → 0.75; read "move distance" as how far it wanders from its way; its drift on, `WRIGGLE_SPEED` 0.4 of its speed, is the other reading). Reimu: in her attacks only every second bullet stops at the border and piles up, by its ring's time and number (new `REIMU_PILE_EVERY` 2); the others leave the screen as ever.
338. **(2026-11-07) Wakasagihime: short even traces, swimming bullets hurt like a slider, no slider beams.** Traces two beats long (`WAKASAGI_TRACE_BEATS` 8 → 2, a point every quarter of a beat: `WAKASAGI_TRACE_STEPS` 24 → 8) and as wide all along (`WAKASAGI_TRACE_END` 0 → 1: no tip). What swims hurts like a slider body and is not used up by a hit (`HurtsLikeSlider` on every ring that swims). The straight bars the user saw were not traces (a probe of the plain beams, `SEGMENT_PROBE=1` in `sectioncheck field`, found no piece of a trace longer than 80 px in a minute of her): they were most likely the beams of the map's sliders, blue in her attacks like everything else; in her sections the sliders fire no beams now (a boss's own beams, Junko's, stay). 3.8 ms a frame alone.
339. **(2026-11-07) Kagerou: slower rings while she stays, cross swings.** While she stays her rings fly half as fast as while she flies (`KAGEROU_STAY_RING_SPEED` 0.5: as fast as a plain bullet). Every claw is turned 45 degrees round where it goes through the player, clockwise, and the next one as far counter-clockwise (`KAGEROU_CLAW_TILT_DEGREES`; read "claws angle 45 degrees clockwise, then counter-clockwise" so: the three arcs are tilted round the player, so two claws in a row cross like an X and still go through the player; the middle of their arcs is no longer Kagerou herself).
340. **(2026-11-07) Joon: half as many of her own bullets.** Her own golden bullets come half as often (`JOON_OWN_INTERVAL` 2 on the spacing of her stars; in a spinner every second of the spinner's stars). Every fifth bullet turning gold is as it was.
341. **(2026-11-07) Wakasagihime: green petals on her seaweed.** Six green petals grow on every blade of seaweed (new `WAKASAGI_PETALS` 6), from a fifth of the way up to near its tip, on one side and the next on the other, just off its edge, pointing out and a little up, as big as a plain bullet (`WAKASAGI_PETAL_SIZE` 1, smaller higher up) and growing with the blade; harmless while they are on it (drawn as beads: the blade under them hurts). When the blade comes off the ground (after its eight beats) they spread away from it: each becomes a green petal bullet that flies off the way it pointed, turned a little either way, at 0.6 of a plain bullet's speed, each a little faster or slower (`WAKASAGI_PETAL_SPEED`), fluttering from side to side by 0.3 circle radii (`WAKASAGI_PETAL_SWAY_CIRCLES`). Read "spread away once grass detaches" so: they stay on the blade until then, and do not fall up with it. 348 petals in a minute of her on Chirality; 3.8 ms a frame alone, as before. (`sectioncheck field` with `ACT_PROBE=1` also prints the bullets each act has made, by shape and tint.)
342. **(2026-11-07) Wakasagihime: the petals hurt on the blade too.** Replaces "harmless while they are on it" in 341: a petal on a blade hurts the player who touches it, by the petal's own shape (`BulletCollision.Hits` with `BulletShape.Petal`, as big as it is drawn, in `TideAct.Hurts`), like the blade under it, and is not used up by the hit (all six still fly off when the blade comes off the ground). They are still drawn as beads, which do not hurt by themselves (a bead is tested as a circle; the act tests the petal's shape instead).
343. **(2026-11-07) Kaguya: her own rings half as often.** A ring every half beat instead of every quarter beat (`KAGUYA_RING_GAP_BEATS` 0.25 → 0.5; `KAGUYA_RINGS` 9 → 5, so 4 a burst of two beats, the fifth being the first of the next), in a spinner too; still 20 bullets a ring, each ring turned 6 degrees further than the one before (so the stream spins half as fast in time). The user first wrote "halve her own bullets count", then "frequency I mean": read as how often her rings come, the stream kept going without a break; the other reading, bursts every four beats (two beats of rings at a quarter beat, then two beats of nothing), is not done. Stars 4 → 3.
344. **(2026-11-07) HP: 25 hits to 10.** The health a hit takes now goes from 4 % of the bar at the map's HP 0 to 10 % at HP 10 (was 5 % → 20 %), on a straight line (new `DAMAGE_AT_HP_0` 1/25, `DAMAGE_AT_HP_10` 1/10; `DamagePerSliderTick` is `DamagePerBullet`). From a full bar: 25 hits at HP 0, 18 at 3 (5.8 %), 15 at 5 (7 %), 13 at 7 (8.2 %), 10 at 10 (were 20, 11, 8, 7, 5). A slider body (and everything that hurts like one) takes as much every 500 ms, a beam alone half of it; regeneration (10 % a second) is as it was. Read "rescale it to 25 to 10" as the hits from a full bar at HP 0 and HP 10, straight in the damage (the other way, straight in the hits, would give 18 at HP 5).
345. **(2026-11-07) A boss's own things are not hidden by the others.** Yukari's trains and the other things of the bosses that are drawn as bullets but are not bullets that fly (the beads of the acts: Hecatia's chains, what her worlds and Unzan's hands hold, Yuuka's leaves, petals and heads, Wakasagihime's petals on her seaweed, Sekibanki's star heads, Ichirin's; the flowers on Yuyuko's tree and on Lily White's branches) are left as they are by the hiding of the other bosses: Rumia's circles, Koishi's vanishing (near the player, the glitch, everything invisible; no smile on them), Sannyo's smoke making them see-through (new `BulletInstance.Immune`, set for the trains in the ring loop, for every bead and for those flowers; `applyStageHides` and `koishiHideMechanics` skip it). Koishi's glitch took every beam away, the wood of the acts too (Yuyuko's tree, Lily's branches, Patchouli's trees, Yuuka's stems, Wakasagihime's seaweed): the wood now stays (`flickeredOut` in `updateLasers`: the map's beams are skipped, the acts' beams are drawn and all but the wood dropped). The pictures of the acts (moons, planets, the Earth, UFOs, hands, prisms and so on) were never hidden by any of them, nor were the other beams of the acts by Rumia, the smoke or Koishi's full vanishing. What still hides: bullets and beams, the acts' bullets too (bubbles, petals that fly, knives), and the bosses themselves under Koishi. Read "special objects" as everything a boss puts on the field that is not a bullet or a beam; Luna Child's softening is a blur, not see-through, so it still softens the trains.
346. **(2026-11-07) Seija: a screen of arrows before every turn, and the circle stays.** For the four beats of the warning before every turn of the screen (start and end of each attack) the whole screen (the 16:9 one, not only the playfield) fills with big see-through arrows, the one on her dress (the user's picture, cut from her clothes: a triangular head on a straight shaft; new baked `StageTexture.SeijaArrow`, white with a dark outline, tinted), in a grid of 3 rows (`SEIJA_GRID_ROWS`; a cell is a third of the screen's height): red ones in the middles of the cells flowing sideways, from the side the player is nearer to (left or right of the middle the screen turns round) to the other, blue ones at the corners of the cells flowing from the top or the bottom, whichever is nearer, to the other, a cell a beat (`SEIJA_GRID_CELLS_PER_BEAT` 1). Which way is chosen as the warning starts and kept (`flows`, cleared on `Reset`): it is where the turn will throw the player, so they see that their movement is about to be turned round. Each arrow is 0.75 of a cell long (`SEIJA_GRID_ARROW_SIZE`), 35 % opaque at most (`SEIJA_GRID_ALPHA`), under the bullets (`stageBack`), fading in over a beat and out over the half beat before the turn (`SEIJA_GRID_FADE_IN_BEATS` 1, `SEIJA_GRID_FADE_OUT_BEATS` 0.5). The arrows round the player and the clone stay as they were. The red circle after the turn upside down no longer fades out after two beats: it closes in as before and stays round the player until the screen turns back, then fades out in half a beat (`SEIJA_CIRCLE_GONE_BEATS`, new); the one after the turn back is as it was. `sectioncheck texture <make method> <out.png> [tint]` (new) bakes a stage picture and draws it tinted, to look at.

349. **(2026-11-07) Cirno's bursts come out where the frozen bullets are, beside other bosses too.** In a section of two or three bosses every ring is replaced by a copy with half or a third of its bullets (and other mechanics replace rings with bigger, rebuilt or turned copies), after Cirno's bursts are planned: a burst kept pointing at the ring as it was before, which was not in play any more (never frozen, never pulled by Utsuho's hole), so its bullets came out where that ring would have flown to, or not at all when that was off the screen, while the frozen bullets vanished elsewhere. Every copy now knows the ring it was first made from (new `BulletEmitter.Lineage`, set in `copy`; `Source` can be set), and at the end of `Plan` a ring made out of another one (`Source`: Cirno's bursts, Kyouko's echoes) that points at a ring no longer in play follows it to the copy that took its place and fires when it did. Cirno with Utsuho on Chirality [Asymmetry], 0:40 + 90 s: 10893 bursts had a source not in play, now 0; 4779 bursts checked against where their bullet was drawn the frame before, none off (`sectioncheck field` with `FREEZE_PROBE=check`, new; `FREEZE_PROBE=1|both|homing` follows one frozen bullet). 352. **(2026-11-07) HP: the same on every map, 12 bullets or 8 slider ticks.** The map's HP drain no longer counts (replaces 344's 4 % → 10 %): a bullet takes a twelfth of the bar (`DAMAGE_BULLET_HITS` 12), a tick of a slider body and of everything that hurts like one (every 500 ms while it is touched) an eighth (`DAMAGE_SLIDER_HITS` 8; was the same as a bullet); a beam alone half of a tick, as before (`LASER_DAMAGE_SCALE` 0.5: 16 ticks). `DAMAGE_AT_HP_0` / `_10` gone. 356. **(2026-11-07) Fast bullets whoosh past the player.** The user's "Fast Fly By" recording (`Danmakusu\Fast-Fly-By-SnapYT.App.mp3`, 5.6 s) holds seven whooshes: cut out one by one by loudness (new `Bake flybys <wav> <folder> <prefix>`: 10 ms levels smoothed over 50 ms, peaks at least 0.3 of the loudest and 0.2 s apart, each from the quietest moment before it to the quietest after it, the quiet ends trimmed to a twentieth of its peak, 5 ms fade in and 40 ms out, peak 0.9), `Resources/Samples/fly_by_0..6.wav` (0.36 to 0.95 s). A bullet (of a ring, or of an act that can hurt) within three circle radii of the player that has moved at least 1.5 times as fast as a plain bullet since the frame before (whatever makes it fast: its ring, a boss's speeding up; not a jump through a portal or a mirroring, over 20 times) whooshes once while it is near (`BulletField.FlyBy.cs`; `PlainBulletSpeed` set by the playfield from the approach rate): one of the seven at random, never the same twice in a row, at most one every 0.3 s (`FLYBY_*`). Every whoosh is played twice at the same moment (`FLYBY_COPIES` 2): twice as loud, in step so nothing cancels (the user's way to make a sound louder without raising its level; read as meant for these). About 100-130 whooshes a minute with Gengetsu, Kagerou or Reimu on Chirality with a player who stands still (`FLYBY_PROBE=1` in `sectioncheck field`, which sets the plain speed as the game does).
357. **(2026-11-07) Beams: what is stretched too far is cut.** A slice of a beam (between two neighbouring points) is cut when it has grown to more than three times as long as it would be if nothing had happened to it and by more than 24 px (new `BEAM_SLICE_MOST_STRETCH` 3, `BEAM_SLICE_LEAST_PX` 24), whichever of that and the old rule (1.5 times + 32 px + the beam's width) is shorter: short slices no longer hide in the 32 px of slack. A piece of a slider's beam stretched to more than three times is cut away at once (not seen, not hurting) instead of fading out until three and a half times (`BEAM_STRETCH_FADE_TO` gone). Read "any laserbeam slice that got abnormally prolonged is cut" so: every beam that something can stretch (not Marisa's rigid spark, not what keeps its stretches), every slice. With Okina, Zanmu or Seija on Chirality no piece of a plain beam longer than 80 px is drawn (`SEGMENT_PROBE=1`), beams as many as before.
358. **(2026-11-07) Circles and sliders take the colours of the boss of their section.** A circle or slider in a boss section (from the warning to its end, also while she finishes her sequence) has the colours of that boss, her four one after the other as the combos go; with two or three bosses on the screen the combos take turns between them (`BossColours.SectionColour`; the skin transformer answers a combo colour lookup of a hit object in a section with it, `BossColours.SetSections` from the playfield after the planning). Outside the sections they keep what they had (the bosses' colours one after the other with a boss selection mod, the map's own without). With a boss selection mod (the palette is the bosses' colours, four each) the bullets and slider beams a circle or slider fires take the colour it has (`ColourSections`, `styleOf`, `laserStyle`, worked out once per ring and beam: `SectionStyle`). The user: "combo colour all osu objects respectively to boss sections", confirmed as this.
359. **(2026-11-07) Beams: the pair that stands out of its beam is cut (replaces 357).** The user's rule: every pair of neighbouring slices of a beam is looked at against the rest of the same beam. Each pair's stretch is how far apart the two are against how far apart they would be if nothing had happened to them; the beam's own stretch is its middle pair's (at least 1); a pair stretched more than three times as much as that, and more than 24 px further apart, is cut (`BEAM_SLICE_OUTLIER` 3, `BEAM_SLICE_LEAST_PX` 24). A beam that a boss stretches all along (Yumemi's push widens it as it flies out from her) is no longer cut anywhere, as nothing in it stands out; a frozen part beside a moving one, a jump through a portal, a mirroring still are. A beam of fewer than three pairs, which cannot be compared, goes by the old fixed limit (1.5 times + 32 px + its width; `BEAM_SLICE_COMPARE_PAIRS` 3). The cut of a whole stretched piece of a slider's beam at three times (357; before it, the fade from three to three and a half times) is gone (`fadeStretched`, `BEAM_STRETCH_FADE_FROM`, `BEAM_SLICE_MOST_STRETCH` removed). With Okina, Zanmu, Seija or Cirno on Chirality no piece of a beam longer than 80 px is drawn (`SEGMENT_PROBE=1`; Yumemi's long pieces are the petals her act draws, not beams), beams as many as before.
360. **(2026-11-07) Utsuho: while the black hole is there, the bullets of the map come in from outside the screen.** What the circles and sliders fire in her pauses (with their tails) no longer comes out of them: every bullet starts just beyond the edge of the screen (a 16:9 screen round the playfield, `UTSUHO_INWARD_HALF_WIDTH` 426, `_HEIGHT` 240) the way it would have flown out from the middle, and flies straight in at the hole, which takes it (new `BulletEmitter.FromOutside`: a copy with a start of its own for every bullet, flying inwards, not aimed at the player; `copy` takes `aimAtPlayer`). Not what is pulled or bounces (homing and ricochet rings find their own ways), Utsuho's big balls, trains, or what bosses fire. Utsuho alone on Chirality, 0:40 + 60 s: about 1100 of the map's bullets come in from the edges (`EDGE_PROBE=1`), 2.2 ms a frame.
361. **(2026-11-08) Minamitsu Murasa: anchors (`Anchors`, no longer a placeholder).** New `UI/Stages/AnchorAct.cs`, constants `MURASA_*`. She stands in the middle (`bossAtCentre`) and never pauses (`no_pauses`); she shoots nothing of her own besides the anchors (no placeholder rings, no big beams of her own: `no_big_beams`; in a spinner no stars: `own_spinner_bullets` + `no_spinner_stars`). Every 16 beats (`MURASA_SEQUENCE_BEATS`) eight anchors (`MURASA_ANCHORS`) are thrown round her, one every half beat over 4 beats (`MURASA_THROW_SPREAD_BEATS`), evenly round the circle starting straight up, every second sequence turned by half a step; on top of them one at the player every 4 beats (`MURASA_AIMED_EVERY_BEATS`, aimed where the player is when it is thrown). An anchor (new baked texture `StageTexture.Anchor`: ring, stock, shank, curved arms with flukes, dark grey iron with a thin lighter edge, 4 circle radii long, `MURASA_ANCHOR_CIRCLES`) flies crown first to the wall in 1 beat (`MURASA_FLIGHT_BEATS`) and stops with its crown a quarter of its length out of the wall (`MURASA_ANCHOR_OUT`); the screen shakes 2 px dying to 1 px for 150 ms (`MURASA_SHAKE_MS`). It is chained to her: the links are plain bullets, 5 of their radii apart (`MURASA_LINK_GAP_RADII`), a little grey (`MURASA_LINK_GREY` 0.6), strung from the anchor's ring towards her. After 8 beats at the wall (`MURASA_HOLD_BEATS`) it is unchained: every link becomes a ring of 6 bullets (`MURASA_RING_BULLETS`) 0.4 the size of a plain one (`MURASA_RING_SIZE`), at 0.6 of a plain bullet's speed (`MURASA_RING_SPEED`), and the anchor sinks (fades out, shrinking to 0.6, over 1 beat). Anchors (their real outline, `StageLayer.AnchorDistance`) and chains hurt like a slider body. In the last 9 beats of a section she throws nothing more, so no anchor is let go early (first tried: all that were out burst at the section's end at once, about 1300 bullets in one moment). Read "archon" as anchor; "another sequence 8 beats later, total 16 beats" as: a sequence every 16 beats (4 beats of throws + 8 beats held ≈ the first unchaining at beat 9, the next sequence at beat 16). Link spacing first 3.5 radii: up to 26 links a chain, about 4800 burst bullets in 20 s of Chirality's kiai; at 5 radii up to 18 links, 3100. Difficulty 4 (`BossDifficulty`). Bench (Chirality [Asymmetry] 0:48, 20 s): Murasa 1.41 ms per frame (worst 8.6), + Nue 1.78, + Junko + Utsuho 6.85 (worst 18.4).
362. **(2026-11-08) Map watcher: the importer was started again every minute.** Once the download had finished the importer ended normally, but PowerShell could not read its exit code (the process handle was not kept), so `watch.ps1` took it for an error and started it again, 182 times, and every start backed up `client.realm` (185 copies, 10.8 GB in `%APPDATA%\osu`). Fixed: the watcher keeps the handle and also takes "watching over" in the importer's output as a normal end, and it stops when everything is downloaded and imported; the importer backs up the database only before the first map it actually imports. The 183 extra copies were removed (the one from before the first import and the newest one are kept). Download: 3255 of 3266 sets (11 failed), all imported. Danmakusu stars for the downloaded maps: new SectionCheck mode `starlist <folder> <out.csv>` (the game's own calculator, no mods and DT, next to osu!'s stars; resumable, every core, about 0.25 s a map), run over all 10 548 .osu files into `H:\osu maps\danmakusu_stars.csv`, and by the watcher for every new set. (osu!lazer cannot keep them itself: its database holds one star rating per map, osu!'s; song select works out the Danmakusu stars live when the ruleset is chosen.) Seen on the way: on some maps the DT rating comes out lower than the plain one (Ayakashi Normal 2.86, DT 2.20).
363. **(2026-11-08) Murasa: anchors clang when they hit the wall.** The user's "Big Hammer Hit" (`Danmakusu\Big-Hammer-Hit-SnapYT.App.mp3`, 4.1 s): the whole hit (several strikes in the first 0.7 s) cut as one, 0 to 1.3 s, the ring faded out over the last 0.4 s, no limiter, peak 0.9 (new Bake mode `cut`), `Resources/Samples/murasa_anchor.wav`. Played every time an anchor stops at the wall (with the shake), at 0.7 volume (`MURASA_SOUND_VOLUME`); not again when time goes back, and not when the hit was more than 100 ms ago (a jump forward). Read "anchor drop" as the moment it hits the wall (the alternative: when it is thrown).
364. **(2026-11-08) Star rating: bullet density and movement to safer spots together.** New `Bullets/StarRating.cs`: the map is planned once (without bosses), both ratings are measured on that plan (`BulletDensity.Rate` and `ForcedMovement.Rate` take the planned rings and beams now), and stars = `STAR_SCALE` x density value ^ `STAR_DENSITY_POWER` x movement value ^ `STAR_MOVEMENT_POWER` (0.0595, 0.157, 1.199; before: movement alone, 0.034 x value ^ 1.519). Fitted on 151 of the downloaded maps (every 70th .osu) against osu!'s stars: fitted freely density got next to nothing (power 0.06; alone it follows osu!'s order much less well, rank 0.57 against movement's 0.87), so its share was fixed at a quarter of the influence (on the spread of each), where it costs nothing (rank 0.872 against 0.873, 0.78 stars off on average against 0.79); half and half would be rank 0.82, 0.90 stars off. DT: density grows with the speed by itself; the movement part is never rated below its no-mod value x rate ^ `FORCED_RATE_POWER` (0.25) and HT never above (on 61 maps 9 came out easier with DT: the lazy player looks as far ahead in real time, which at DT covers more of the map, so on sparse maps it found places that stay safe longer and dodged less, e.g. a Normal 2.86, DT 2.20; 0.25 is the median of what DT did to the value, x1.10). `sectioncheck stars` takes STARS_RATE (the breakdown at another speed) and STARS_CSV (rows with both values, to fit on).
365. **(2026-11-08) Hecatia's World's Ender: the user's two recordings stacked, split on split, shatter on shatter.** The Man of Steel blast (7.45-12.7 s, music taken out as before: `blast` 2.0-7.35 s) has two surges: its first (0-1.45 s of it, faded out over 0.25 s) is laid on the cut (`hecatia_cut`, Elder Titan's Earth Splitter, 0.60-3.32 s), its second (1.3-5.25 s, faded out over 0.5 s) on the explosion (`hecatia_explosion`, Elder Titan 3.32-7.75 s). Each pair lined up at its first hit (the first 10 ms a quarter as loud as the loudest: Man of Steel's split moved 0.25 s earlier, its shatter 0.04 s), added in stereo and soft-limited (new Bake mode `mix`; `cut` cuts a stretch without a limiter). The split is about 1.5 dB louder; the shatter keeps its force about a second longer (-7 dB at 1.0 s, was -13) and dies out at the same time.

368. **(2026-11-08) Fly-bys: only fast bullets, only close.** A bullet whooshes past from 3 times a plain bullet's speed (`FLYBY_SPEED_FACTOR` 1.5 -> 3), within 1.5 circle radii of the player (`FLYBY_CIRCLES` 3 -> 1.5; "distance decrease" without a number read as half).
369. **(2026-11-08) Frozen, trapped, at the edge of Eiki's circle: the cursor and its ghost swap places, only to look at.** Whenever the character is held away from the mouse with mouse controls (the freeze keys, Enoko's trap, Eiki's circle; `BulletField.CursorSwapped`: more than 1 px apart), the skinned osu! cursor is drawn where the character really is, and the character's ring with its hitbox dot (and the freeze tether, back to the cursor) where the mouse is, i.e. where it will go when it is let go. Nothing else changes: the hits, the aim of the bosses, the teleport all go by the character as before. The field reads the mouse from the cursor drawable, so the playfield moves the drawable onto the character only after the field has read it (`UpdateAfterChildren`, `swapCursor`) and puts it back on the mouse at the start of the next frame unless the mouse has moved meanwhile (`restoreCursor`). The cursor trail stays with the mouse. Keyboard controls are left as they were (there the ring is the player all the time).

379. **(2026-11-08) Beams bend where their slices go; Reisen's slide no longer tears beams apart; cuts by neighbours.** The user's screenshots (Eiki's circle: beams stretched long towards its middle; Seija + Shion: beams drawn as loops and lines). The "long" probe of `sectioncheck field` showed the long pieces were of beams of only two points (a straight slider's: its start and where the ball is), whose two ends had gone different ways: Eiki's circle turns what enters it to its middle, Shion turns things back, and nothing in between was drawn but the straight line; the cut by comparison (359/371) needs three pairs and so never looked at them. New `LaserEmitter.refineBends`: between every two points of a beam that the bosses steer (an op that has begun and did not end before the beam began, or portals, or ripples) the slice halfway is looked at; if it is more than 6 px off the line, or the pair is 1.5 times as far apart as it would be (plus those px), it is put in and both halves are looked at again, at most 4 times (`BEAM_BEND_PX`, `BEAM_BEND_STRETCH`, `BEAM_BEND_DEPTH`; 3 px and 6 times cost Eiki 7 ms a frame). Reisen's slide (beams slide by the way they fly, so that a beam slides as one) took the way after Eiki had turned each slice to her circle's middle: neighbouring slices went up and down by turns, half a screen apart; now the way each slice was fired (`Trajectory.Trace`). A pair is now compared with up to two pairs on each side (`BEAM_SLICE_NEIGHBOURS`), not with the whole beam. Chirality [Asymmetry] 0:48 + 20 s, long pieces drawn (taller or wider than 60 % of the screen) and ms per frame, before -> after: Eiki 10894 / 2.3 -> 2036 / 4.9, Eiki + Reisen 105795 / 5.5 -> 13 / 8.4, Seija + Shion 2509 / 3.9 -> 0 / 5.1, Yumemi 386 / 1.7 -> 37 / 4.0. 383. **(2026-11-08) Yukari's trains ignore every mechanic, really.** The user's Stealth-Dash [Hypersonic] log (75 bosses, triple): a train broke off, did not come out, or changed its way. Replayed headlessly (new `TRAIN_PROBE=1` on `sectioncheck field`: what took a train, how often the rings were reset, how far a train turned or jumped; new `BENCH_TRIPLE=1`, triple selection with a list of any length), five causes: (1) every frame the screen shook (Murasa's clangs, Hecatia, Ichirin...) the field took the shaken screen for a resized window and reset every ring, about 1700 times over one train's flight, and every reset aimed the trains again at where the player was then: `updateScreenBounds` measures the screen through the parent now, unshaken. (2) Nue's UFOs, and any act that takes bullets in the middle of the field (`absorbIntoStage`), took the train in: trains are skipped. (3) Clownpiece's moon could destroy one: skipped (`scanMoonOf`). (4) Sakuya's stop, Gengetsu's and Saki's haste, Utsuho's slowing and Mystia's slowdown warped a train's clock: a train goes by real time now (`BossEffects.Effective`). (5) A train keeps the aim it was fired with when the rings are worked out again (`trainAims`; forgotten when time goes back before it). The replay: taken 165 frames -> 0, ring resets about 1679 -> 0, no turns, 26 -> 9.3 ms a frame. Also checked with Yukari + Nue, + Sakuya, + Utsuho + Clownpiece.

389. **(2026-11-08) Maps of the other modes: mania, taiko and catch.** osu! converted them with osu!standard's own converter: mania's notes stood in a line (each at its column's x, y 192), its long notes were spinners. New `DanmakusuBeatmapConverter` (osu!'s for osu! maps) and `Bullets/ConvertedModes` (what every object came from, by object). **Mania:** the objects stand on a ring round the middle of the playfield, the first column in the middle and the others evenly round it (the second at the top), as big as the keys are many (`CONVERT_RING_RADIUS` 40 + `CONVERT_RING_PER_KEY` 8 a key, at most `CONVERT_RING_MOST` 96 osu! px: 4K 72, 7K 96); it turns a whole turn one way in 32 beats, then back the other way, and so on (`CONVERT_RING_TURN`, `CONVERT_RING_REVERSE_BEATS`), and is twice as big in a boss section (`CONVERT_BOSS_RING` 2; the map is planned once to know the sections, and the jump distance that the section scores measure uses the ring as it is outside them, `BaseStart`/`BaseEnd`, so that the sections do not depend on themselves). Every long note is a slider with a whistle on its head that does not move (its path goes back and forth by a pixel, as long as the note at slider velocity 0.1, `CONVERT_HOLD_VELOCITY`), shown as a circle (no ball, no follow circle); its beams are a slider's (as many, as wide, as fast) but turn as a spinner's do, towards the player as they fire, so they bend (`LaserEmitter.HoldBeam`). The boss rules that take a spinner's beams away took these too (none were left on a 4K map with any boss): they tell them apart now (`SpinnerLike`), and the rules for a slider's beams count them as a slider's (Nemuno does not take them: they have no path). The circle size is at most 7 (`CONVERT_MOST_CS`: a mania map's is its key count) and nothing is stacked (stack leniency 0). **Taiko:** the same ring, always as 4 keys: kat, don, don, kat; the kats take turns on the middle and the last place, the dons on the two others, a drumroll is a long note on the dons' places, a swell a spinner. A big note shoots one ring of Luna Child's arc beams instead of its bullets, as many arcs as a plain ring of that moment has bullets (`BulletsPerRing` by OD), from its edge at her rings' speed; they hurt like a slider body (new `BulletField.Drums`). **Catch:** fruits and juice streams are circles and sliders that fall straight down, evenly, from the top of the playfield (`CATCH_TOP` 0) to its bottom (`CATCH_BOTTOM` 384) over their approach time (0.64 px/ms at AR 9), landing when they are due; a stream is its trail (a point every 16 ms, `CATCH_TRAIL_STEP_MS`, each as far above as the stream comes there later: falling evenly, every point lands when the stream is there; repeats are part of the one path), as fast along it as it must be to last as long as the stream. No approach circles, slider ball or follow points. They hurt like a slider body all the way down (`SliderZone.FallSpeed`; a circle has no moment-of-hit zone then). What lands shoots the rings and beams it would, in two halves: every second bullet from where it landed, spread evenly over 150 degrees up (`CATCH_FAN_DEGREES`), the rest from the top straight above it, 150 degrees down - the user: from the bottom alone the bullets would never cross the bottom of the screen (the other reading: every second object from the top). A stream's beams are split the same way (`LaserEmitter.Fan`). Hard Rock's flip is turned back. A banana shower is a spinner (a spinner section). **Tools:** without the game the decoder leaves the mode out (every map read as mode 0): the tools read the file's `Mode:` line (`ConvertedModes.KeepMode`), so `starlist` now really skips what is not osu!standard (it rated them as osu! maps before); new `sectioncheck convert <map.osu> [...]` prints what the decoder makes of a map, what the game converts it into, where the first objects stand and what the planner makes of it. Checked headlessly: Knight Rider [Fight Like Animals] (4K: 81 long notes, 81 turning beams, about 17 beams a frame with a boss), Chiisana Chiisana Kenshou [Inner Oni] (taiko: 19 big notes, 1 drumroll), Miracle 5ympho X [Kurokami's Rain] (catch: 178 streams, ends where they ended). Not yet seen in the game.
390. **(2026-11-08) Maps of mania, taiko and catch are offered in Danmakusu.** The user: choosing Danmakusu, the other modes' maps do not show up. osu! offers a map in a ruleset only if it is that ruleset's own or an osu!standard map to convert (`BeatmapInfoExtensions.AllowGameplayWithRuleset`: song select's list, a set's difficulties, and whether picking a map switches to its own ruleset all ask it); they had shown only while the importer had wrongly filed them as osu! maps. New `OtherModesInSongSelect`: when osu! loads the ruleset, that answer is changed for Danmakusu alone - with "show converted beatmaps" on, a mania, taiko or catch map is offered too, converted as 389 says. The maps, where osu! files them and what every other ruleset is offered stay as they are. Done with Harmony 2.4.3 (`Lib.Harmony`, its .NET 10 build embedded in the ruleset's dll, written out once under the temp folder and loaded from there: loaded from memory it cannot work); if it fails only this is lost and the log says why (`Danmakusu: maps of the other modes cannot be offered`). New `sectioncheck offered`: what is offered where (osu! maps in Danmakusu, osu! and mania as before; taiko, catch, mania maps in Danmakusu only with converts on, and still not in osu!standard), also after 200000 calls.
